# Changelog
- **[2026-10-15]**: fix(web): **S5 investment lines now carry a per-line unit with a confirm trust model (decider bug report).** Every manual line gets an amount unit select (KRW won/cheon-won/baek-man-won/eok-won, foreign million/100-million of the line currency) beside the amount with a visible converted-text preview; legacy demo rows (old `kind`/`growth_rate` shape) are normalized WITHOUT conversion — category, currency and period are only kept when known, any previously stamped unit is dropped and the row shows a "unit needs confirmation" chip, is excluded from the investment totals with an exclusion note, and is not copied to S8 at promotion (audited skipped with reason unit_unconfirmed or currency_non_krw). Confirmation happens only through a human unit choice; unknown posted units are rejected with 400 invalid_amount_unit; totals split lumpsum vs annualized recurring; dismissible sanity chips warn when a line or the grand total looks implausibly large.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: S8 structure decision recorded (Decided 2026-10-15) in the S8 technical/financial lens design and an ADR-0171 amendment 3 — after the S7 per-target capability judgement lands, S8 keeps its current structure (technical-feasibility tab = read-only capability summary + T2-T5 inputs + capability-adjusted scenario pointer), does not become finance-only, T2-T5 stay at S8 with S9 as paired Brown Field verification, and the summary table may later compress to one summary-strip line.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: compensation benchmark minimum cohort aligned to five across skill and policy references.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: ADR-0175 S5 staffing plan and derived labor cost design (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: ADR-0174 accepted (S5 investment, labor and operating cost lines must reach the S8 business-case analysis; OD-1..OD-6 resolved as recommended); acceptance recorded in DEC-20261015-02; README index row updated; slice implementation follows.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: ADR-0174 total investment definition, S5 investment summary and S8 hand-off design (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-15]**: fix(web): **S5 investment item list is now one unified list (decider bug report).** Reflected and saved capability cost lines appear in the investment items list after the manual lines, with a capability-link chip, save-state and rejudge chips, a judgement link, an unreflect action and an item count and sum footer; the header component counter counts a capability-only list as filled. Totals logic and the save payload are unchanged.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: ADR-0173 accepted (capability cost and milestone linkage); acceptance recorded in DEC-20261015-01; README index rows updated.
- **[2026-10-15]**: fix(web): **S5/S8 capability judgement table: summary drops months, columns rebalanced (decider feedback).** The judgement summary now shows only the resolution and the attainability grade (months and the expected level stay in the tooltip and the dialog); all six columns are sized from their content with rem minimums so chips never wrap mid-label, the capability name column is the widest, and the confirmation column shows a status chip with a muted name and date line.
- **[2026-10-15]**: docs: ADR-0173 capability cost and milestone linkage design (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-15]**: feat(web): **S5 entry strategy options and AI proposal (ADR-0171 amendment 2).** The first S5 section now offers four selectable entry modes (build, partner, hire, invest; multi-select with exactly one main mode), one rationale field and one optional rejected-alternatives field, plus a muted caution when a capability resolution is not among the selected modes. Modes are stored without a migration as an optional header line in the existing entry_strategy text (legacy plain text still reads as rationale only); consumers read it through a parser. New proposal-only route POST /api/greenfield/{oppId}/entry-strategy-draft and MCP tool draft_entry_strategy fill only empty fields as unsaved drafts from stored S3/S4/profile evidence (no web search); a person saves. The strategy section has its own save button (strategy fields only), the capability cost block has an explicit reflect action that saves only the investment lines and shows saved vs unsaved state and the S8 promotion hand-off, and all S5 investment amounts are now shown and stored in million KRW with a unit marker (legacy lines in 100 million KRW are converted value-preserving; promotion copy and the amount check follow).
- **[2026-10-15]**: feat(web): **S5 capability closing cost lines and milestone candidates, ADR-0173 slice 1.** The S5 investment section shows a read-only block of derived capability cost lines (from the live judgements, amounts in eok from million KRW) with a per-line reflect checkbox (pre-checked only for confirmed, non-stale judgements), category suggestion by resolution route, totals, a dismissible double-count warning and a read-only milestone candidate list. Reflected lines are stored in the same lines array with server-recomputed amounts (tampered amounts rejected, stale reflected lines stay flagged), copied at promotion as exact million-KRW lump sums with unreflected lines skipped, and the S3 row gains a server-derived milestone candidate. No schema change; decision support only; not exercised in a browser.
- **[2026-10-15]**: refactor(web): **S5/S8 capability judgement filter tabs reduced to two (decider decision).** The separate confirmed tab duplicated the confirmation column and is removed; the tabs are now "to handle (N)" and "all (N)". When "to handle" is empty a friendly line points to "all"; the confirmation column and the confirm action are unchanged.
- **[2026-10-15]**: feat(web): **S5 AI judgement proposal (ADR-0171 amendment).** New proposal-only route `POST /api/opportunities/{id}/capability-attainability/ai-draft` and MCP tool `draft_capability_judgement` propose the resolution, expected level, cost, months, attainability grade and rationale with a basis per field from stored S3 data, the S5 entry strategy and company profiles (no web search, estimates only when defensible, high or low grade needs a rationale, empty fields only, 4 rows per LLM call). The S5 toolbar gets a secondary AI proposal button, the judgement dialog gets a per-capability AI proposal button, proposed fields carry an AI-draft chip, an unsaved-AI-draft counter with discard and a dismissible result note; nothing is saved or confirmed by the AI and S8 stays read-only.
- **[2026-10-15]**: fix(web): **ClampedText no longer has a show-more/show-less toggle (decider decision).** Required-capability notes and narrative previews are clamped to 2 lines with an ellipsis at all lengths; the full text is available as a title tooltip on a focusable element and as visually hidden text for screen readers.
- **[2026-10-15]**: feat(web): **Analyst-provided evidence for company profiles (ADR-0172 amendment 4).** Profile generation accepts up to 8 source URLs (SSRF-guarded fetch) and a company note that are fed to the model as cited untrusted data, confirm cited claims in the verification ledger and keep the no-search-evidence flag off; an existing profile gains hand-entered sources and analyst edits of industry exposure, strengths, weaknesses and business assessment, each saved as a new audited version with a required reason.
- **[2026-10-15]**: fix(web): **S3 required-capability table: the necessity and holding-judgement columns are narrowed to content width (decider feedback).** The two selects no longer stretch with the table; the freed width goes to the required-capability and level narrative columns, and the inline level textareas are taller (4 rows, min 5.75rem, auto-grow to 15rem) with top-aligned row cells.
- **[2026-10-15]**: feat(web): **S3 AI actions: proposals without a synergy analysis, holding-check search and evidence fixes (ADR-0172 amendment 3).** The required-capability proposal now also works from the S2 sketch and S1 factors (row source and note name the origin; a precise error only when nothing exists); the holding check adds a Playwright headless search as the last provider, a short reason per unevaluated row with a grouped note and a hint, an optional same-group profile scope, and treats unverified (LLM-guessed) profile text as non-citable; profiles generated without search evidence are flagged with a warning chip and the regenerate hint.
- **[2026-10-15]**: refactor(web): **S5/S8 required-capability judgement redesigned (decider-approved).** The table is now one line per capability with no inputs (wider name column with one status-chip line, necessity, holding judgement, a short judgement summary, confirmation, action); judgement is entered in a dialog (S3 definition read-only on top, S5 inputs below, apply / apply and next / previous / cancel) that writes to the page draft, with to-do / confirmed / all filter tabs that drive next and previous. S8 keeps the same read-only table with an S5 link and no dialog; behaviour and field order unchanged.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **S3 holding check: clear search failures, fail-fast, more evidence (ADR-0172 amendment 2).** Web research reports per-provider status (Tavily quota 432, DuckDuckGo bot challenge detected, lite endpoint tried), pauses a failing provider for 10 minutes, and adds optional Brave and Serper providers via BRAVE_SEARCH_API_KEY and SERPER_API_KEY. The holding check probes once and skips per-row searches when all providers are down, naming the causes and the action in the note and the 400 text; queries are shorter; the LLM now also gets all profile text fields and stored disclosure facts (counters: profile / disclosure / web).
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **Holding assessment unification, slice 1 (ADR-0172).** The S3 capability register gains a necessity field (essential, partial essential, optional) and narrative required-level and held-level modals (400 chars); the held assessment moves to leading/average/partial/none/unevaluated through a read-only compat mapper (legacy met reads as average with a re-assessment hint, unmet as none); coverage weights are 1/1/0.5/0; caution chips are decision support only; row hash v2 recomputes v1 stamps so untouched judgements stay fresh; S5 and S8 show the S3 narrative read-only.
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: ADR-0169 accepted (S8 technical and financial lens); acceptance recorded in DEC-20261014-06; README index row updated.
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: ADR-0171 and ADR-0172 accepted; design open decisions resolved as recommended; acceptance recorded in DEC-20261014-08 and DEC-20261014-09; README index rows updated.
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **Capability held-by/evidence preview shows one muted "not entered" marker when both are empty (S3 table, S8 summary); a single filled field shows only its labelled value.**
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: ADR-0172 holding assessment unification design (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **Required-capability notes are clamped to two lines (decider feedback).** The long required note under the capability name (S3 table, S5 capability table) and in the S8 required-content column now shows two lines with a keyboard-accessible show-more/collapse toggle (`aria-expanded`, only when the note exceeds 80 characters) through one shared `ClampedText` component; the capability name stays fully visible, the source label has its own line, and the S3 first column is width-capped. The row origin line no longer says "S3 source" inside S3 itself: one shared helper gives origin wording (derived from S2 sketch / S1 factors / synergy area, or entered manually), naming S3 only outside the S3 screen. No data or API change.
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: ADR-0171 capability check at S5/S7 design (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **S3/S8 capability linkage correction: 5-step levels stay in S5.** Slice 1 wrongly added required-level/held-level columns to the S3 register and showed them in S8. The S3 table is restored to required capability (with source and required note), held judgement, holders/evidence (keeping the new modal button) and resolution hint; the S8 attainability panel now shows the S3 required note and held judgement (status, holders, evidence preview with a read-only dialog) read-only, with a small S5 level caption only when an S5 level exists. The gate-brief capability line quotes required note and held judgement instead of levels. Row hash and route guards unchanged. ADR-0170 and the design doc carry a correction addendum.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(scripts): **`restore-capability-levels.ts` restores S3/S5 required-capability levels from a backup.** The S8 attainability panel used to write requiredLevel/currentLevel into the shared S3 register (ADR-0170); the audit log has no before-values, so the script takes them from a pre-edit DB backup. Dry run by default (per-row live vs backup table); `--apply` makes a `VACUUM INTO` safety copy and, in one transaction, resets only those two fields on matching rows, leaving held, notes, attainability and technicalLens untouched, with one `capability_levels.restore` audit row per changed opportunity. Idempotent; rows and opportunities absent from the backup are never touched. Added unit tests. No live database was changed.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **S3 to S8 capability linkage, slice 1 (ADR-0170, decider-approved).** S3 is now the single source of the required-capability requirement: the S3 table gains editable required-level and held-level columns and a held-by/evidence dialog (replacing the inline inputs), while the S8 attainability panel shows levels and the held judgement read-only with links back to S3. A versioned row hash over the S3-owned fields is stamped into each S8 judgement; when S3 changes a row the judgement is marked re-judgement-required, its confirmation is cleared, and the old judgement moves to a capped history with an S3 snapshot (never deleted); judgements of rows deleted in S3 are archived; the S8 save route rejects S3-owned fields with `s3_owned_field`, merges only S8 fields onto the live register in one transaction, and refuses to confirm a stale row (`stale_requires_rejudge`). Stale rows count as unjudged in the banner, technical lens T1 and the gate-brief line, with a `re-judgement needed N` count; no grade cap and no gate block. Audit events `capability_attainability.invalidate` / `.archive_removed` / `.history_trim` are recorded under the calling user. Existing judgements without a basis hash are treated as not stale until stamped (stamping script is a later slice). No migration.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 AI proposals consolidated into one decision-tab section (decider-approved).** The adoption recommendation (formerly in the overview tab) and the gate brief now live together in one "AI judgement support" section on the decision tab, above the gate decision form, with two blocks (case to adopt, gate judgement) and a single generate/regenerate button; each block has its own loading and error state. The gate brief keeps its staleness note and rationale prefill; GateDecisionPanel gains `hideBrief` and `rationalePrefill` props and other gates keep the standalone brief. The overview tab keeps a pointer to the decision tab. Proposals only; people record the decision. No API or storage change.
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: ADR-0170 accepted (S3-S8 capability linkage); design open decisions OD-1..OD-5 resolved as recommended; acceptance recorded in DEC-20261014-07.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 financial sub-tab order puts the overview first (decider request).** The financial feasibility sub-tab row is now overview (explain, generate, recommend), scenario comparison, WACC, financial analysis, parameters and snapshots, target costing; the overview is the default landing for `?tab=financial`, missing or unknown `?tab=`, and the Green Field targets redirect. Existing `?tab=` deep links still resolve.
- **[2026-10-14]**: docs: S3-S8 capability linkage design and ADR-0170 (Proposed)
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **S8 Green Field shows a single market-based perspective.** A Green Field has no target company, so the past-based (historical) variant never exists; the S8 heading, intro, overview section, generate button, scenario section labels, the `past-based N/A` row, the ValuationMethodology two-perspective explanation, and the report-composer comparison line no longer mention the dual perspective for Green Field. Brown Field and acquisition wording is unchanged.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 capability attainability grade control is a dropdown.** The grade cell now uses a select (unjudged, high, medium, low) instead of the segmented chip group; the redundant grade status chip and the human-input marker were dropped, while the AI-draft marker stays beneath it. Column widths are rebalanced (grade narrower, rationale-and-evidence wider). No API or storage change.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 capability attainability panel cleanup.** The attainability cell in the existing table now uses a compact segmented grade control (icon plus text) with the grade chip and draft/human marker laid out beneath it, and column widths are rebalanced. The separate evidence textarea is removed in favour of one rationale-and-evidence field, shown in the table as a short read-only preview with an enter/edit button that opens a modal dialog (one large textarea with a character counter, save and cancel; read-only view when the user cannot edit); saves omit the evidence key so stored evidence is never wiped, and existing evidence lines show read-only under the field. No API or storage change.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 business-case screen IA dedupe (decider-approved).** Business-goal setting and status now live only in the goals tab: the goals banner is removed from every other tab and from the WACC and financial-analysis pages. The technical/financial lens summary strip renders only on the decision tab (above the target check and gate decision), no longer above every S8 tab. The explanation/generation/recommendation, parameters/snapshot and target-costing tabs moved from the more-menu into the financial-feasibility sub-tab row; the more-menu keeps only the summary and the risk heatmap. Their ?tab= deep links still resolve and highlight the financial tab. The goals tab is re-laid out for visibility: one primary add-goal button, a status line, an editor that opens above the table, an applied-goals section and a per-scenario attainment section with a status legend and an empty state. Gate approval stays human-only.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S8 business-case screen IA cleanup — one home per fact (decider-approved).** Goal attainment now renders as one GoalAttainmentTable on the goals tab (was one table per scenario section; scenario sections keep a one-line pointer). TargetCheckPanel (fs gate evidence, ADR-0114) renders only on the decision tab, above GateDecisionPanel (was on every tab). Capability content lives only on the technical tab; the scenarios tab keeps a pointer line. Generation has one primary entry (the overview auto-generate row; when the already-run guard reports an existing run the same button escalates to a true regeneration) — the global recalculate row is removed and the legacy notice keeps its own regeneration only while legacy models exist. The EV/Revenue band status lives only on the target-comparison tab (the params confirm form keeps a pointer); the duplicated band warnings are gone. The full "why the two lenses differ" explanation stays only in the overview/methodology; the p50-gap hint and the scenario footer became short links. GateDecisionJump is hidden on the decision tab; dead code removed (NoGoalsCallout, a dead sub-tab guard, an outdated sr-only note). The embedded deals surface is unchanged.
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **Establishment-mode wording and charter source cleanup.** The `mode_not_chosen` 409 and the S11 completion blocker are now plain Korean with no English parenthetical or ADR number; `PromotionPanel` renders blockers through `localizeRequirement`. `lib/project-charter.ts` now builds capability KPIs from the S3/S5 register (`opportunity.capability_gap_json`) instead of the dead `greenfield_assessment.capability_gaps_json` column (column kept, no migration), with a regression test. The ~100 tracked `.playwright-mcp/` files are removed from git; `.gitignore` already ignores the directory.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(establishment): **Green Field establishment mode, backend slice 1 (ADR-0167, Proposed).** Migration 0142 adds `md_project.establishment_mode` (`new_entity` | `business_unit`, business unit only with origin internal), a non-binding `establishment_mode_suggested`, a `mode` column on the establishment step and step-event tables (existing rows default to `new_entity`) and an `establishment_mode_event` history; projects with origin internal/joint are backfilled to `new_entity`, new Green Field projects have no default. Step titles, listing, progress, the step-5 sequence rule and reopen/assign are scoped to the project's mode with a five-step business-unit set; the S11 completion check reports `establishment mode not chosen` instead of assuming a mode. New human-only `PUT /api/projects/:id/establishment-mode` (reason of 10+ characters required to change a stored mode, old-mode steps kept as read-only history), `GET` mode state, extra fields on the establishment GET, optional `establishment_mode_suggested` on promotion, optional `mode` consistency check on `record_establishment`, and the proposal-only MCP tool `propose_establishment_mode`. The entity procedure is gated on mode `new_entity` or unset; new sibling procedure `business-unit-establishment-execution`; routing var `project.establishment_mode`. Checklists, IC wording, glossary and wiki follow in slice 2.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **Capability check moves to S5 for Green Field, S8 becomes a read-only summary (ADR-0171 slice 1, ADR-0172 slice 2a).** S5 is now the judgement edit surface: read-only S3 columns in the canonical order (required capability, necessity, required level, held assessment, held level, holders and evidence), then resolution route, a narrative expected level (400 chars, muted per-route hint text only), cost, months, attainability grade, rationale and evidence, and the human-only confirm; a row with a resolution route needs an expected level to be confirmed (400 expected_level_required). New routes PUT and POST /api/opportunities/{id}/capability-attainability (and /confirm, human-only); the S8 project PUT and confirm return 410 moved with editAt S5 for Green Field for one release (Brown Field keeps the old write path until its S7 surface ships), GET stays as the read-only summary. The S8 panel and S5 share one table component with the stale marker, history and removed-capabilities list; the 5-step scale (LevelSelect and its helpers) is deleted. Gate brief, S4 evaluation, charter text and route advisory read the new order and add large-gap cautions (decision support only, no gate block).
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(glossary): **User-manual terminology defects D-17 and W1-29.** Registered the missing terms (new business, workforce/organization plan, priority investment area, status quo option, partnership, overall grade) plus the three checklist review statuses as their own entries; aligned the display names of Stage Gate and Waiver with the on-screen wording (old names kept as aliases, keys unchanged); `business.yaml` 1.3.0; refreshed `glossary-data.generated.ts` and the library projection; added `tests/unit/glossary-agreed-terms.test.ts`.

- **[2026-10-12]**: docs(governance): **ADR-0159/0160/0161/0162 accepted by the user (2026-10-12).** `docs/adr/README.md` rows added/updated for ADR-0161 (greenfield execution and post-management by track) and ADR-0162 (brownfield DD workspace scope by sub-track), alongside the accepted ADR-0159 (project business goals) and ADR-0160 (greenfield DD and structure by sub-track).
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(db): **Migration runner stop-on-error (ADR-0163).** `db/migrate.ts` now executes each migration statement-by-statement (`splitSqlStatements`) instead of one multi-statement `Database.run()`: bun:sqlite silently swallowed constraint failures raised by non-final statements, so the CHECK-guarded safety assertions in migration 0132/0137 headers could never abort. A middle-statement constraint failure now aborts and rolls back the migration; the 0132/0137 header assertions are load-bearing; `@notransaction`, `schema_migrations` bookkeeping and the CLI interface are unchanged, and stored DDL stays byte-identical (proven by a full legacy-vs-per-statement equivalence test over all migrations).
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(db): **Migration 0139 widens the venture_scorecard unique key with review_cycle (ADR-0164, Proposed).** `UNIQUE (run_id, source_procedure, review_cycle, section, measure_key)` — same-run later quarters now record without `run_required` (kept only for projects with no analysis run at all); 0033-pattern table rebuild copies every column/CHECK/FK/index verbatim, and the header rollback guard refuses while any (run, procedure, section, measure) group spans more than one cycle.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(web): **Table sizing batch 2 — 11 tables onto the shared ColGroup/table-fixed-pct/tmin system.** Route-decision decision history, capability registers, S4 competitive structure (incl. adaptive columns), /agents roster + execution-steps, deals aging, API token table CSS cleanup, and the synergy analysis list; per-table tmin tokens and truncation classes are pinned by `tests/unit/table-sizing.test.ts` batch-2 source checks.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **Eliminate Turbopack NFT warnings.** Replaced runtime filesystem traversal in web-next server bundles with generated static projections for glossary, library, committee registry, and flow-spec metadata, and routed report/glossary write-only paths through runtime-only loaders/subprocesses so `bun run --cwd app/web-next build` completes without Turbopack warnings.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **Restore production build.** Scoped Turbopack's root to the co-newbiz repo (not a parent checkout directory) so `app/web-next` dependencies such as `marked` resolve while `../../db` imports remain in-bounds, and raised the web TS target to ES2020 for shared BigInt-based ULID code. `bun run --cwd app/web-next build` now completes.
- **[2026-09-09]**: feat(wave-d): **UX gaps — notification read state, project-list freshness signal, DD-tailor Tavily provenance.** Added `notification.read_at` plus caller-scoped read/read-all APIs and topbar unread count; project rows now derive `is_stale`/`stale_reasons` from live project-industry facts and `evidence_var.max_age_days` without storing stale state; DD checklist tailoring can run in `CO_NEWBIZ_DD_TAILOR_AGENTS=tavily` mode to attach real source URLs while preserving deterministic unsourced/C fallback and proposed-only boundary #2.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **S3 AI proposals split into a requirement action and a holding action (decider reversal, ADR-0172 amendment).** The add-capabilities proposal now also appends new rows and drafts empty necessity (with a scope note for partial essential) and required level with a basis, and works with zero rows; the new holding-check action drafts holding status, level and evidence from group/affiliate profiles plus bounded external web search with required citations, and a per-row re-check is applied only by a person. Drafts fill empty fields only, show an AI-draft chip with basis and source links, and nothing is written to the database. Required level and held level are now edited inline in the table, and the S3 section is tidied (fixed column widths, grouped caution chips, icon delete, one primary action, unsaved-changes chip). The synergy pipeline's automatic row derivation is unchanged. Not exercised in a browser.
- **[2026-10-14]**: refactor(web): **S3 capability table drops the resolution hint and the holders field (decider-approved, ADR-0172 amendment (2)).** The S3 table no longer edits the resolution (owned by S5/S7) and the held-by field is gone: the column is now evidence only, shown as a single button (input, edit with a check icon, or view; tooltip with the first 60 characters; no preview text). Legacy holders stay stored and appear as a read-only caption; the S3 save keeps the stored resolution and holders when omitted, and the AI holding check no longer proposes holders. The per-row re-check button and dialog are removed. The requirement proposal button is renamed "Propose required capabilities (AI)", always detects and proposes missing necessity and required-level cells (header chip and per-cell marker, honest filled/remaining note), and both AI actions now parse LLM output in small batches with salvage of truncated or fenced JSON and one retry (root cause of the reported parse failure: one oversized call with a small output cap and all-or-nothing parsing). The holding check reports why rows stayed unjudged, and unsaved AI drafts now survive refetches (indicator and discard action). Nothing was exercised in a browser.
All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.


## [Unreleased]

- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): **Goals tab: metric type is selectable again.** The add-goal button now opens the editor directly with the metric select focused and preselected to the first unset preset; the select lists every preset with already-set ones disabled and marked as set. Starter chips remain only in the empty state; the intermediate chip-picking step was removed.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **S8 two-lens view, slices 1-3 (ADR-0169, Proposed; DEC-20261014-06).** The S8 screen is reorganised into three parts with one-line purposes — business goals (`?tab=goals`), technical feasibility (`?tab=technical`) and financial feasibility (`?tab=financial`, with sub-tabs scenarios / WACC / financial analysis only) — plus target comparison (Brown Field) and decision, 5 primary tabs BF / 4 GF within the 5-tab limit; old deep links keep working (`capability` maps to the technical tab, `financial` to scenarios, anchors stripped, unknown values fall back). A `technicalLens` key now rides the existing capability register JSON (no migration): items T1-T5 per subject (`project` or `target:<id>`) with human-only confirmation (status + confirmedBy + confirmedAt) and a reserved verification row shape for the Brown Field S9 pairing (the designed but not-yet-implemented dd-gate blocker: the selected target's T2/T5 verification). Every writer of the register (S3 PUT, attainability writes, synergy merge, backfill) routes through a single `serializeGapRegister` helper so the key can be neither dropped nor forged — enforced by a filesystem-walk regression test. A read-only summary strip (both lenses with met / caution / unjudged chips, icon + text, plus a goals line and a technical warning) renders on top of every S8 page from the new `GET /api/projects/:id/s8-lens-summary` (deny-by-default 404; financial lookups degrade to unjudged on failure), and the FS gate brief carries one informational line sourced from it. Warning only: no grade cap, no gate block; the S8 completion check is unchanged. T1 derives from the ADR-0168 capability register (the panel moves under the technical tab); T2-T5 show unjudged placeholders pending the next release. Existing projects show unjudged with no backfill. Tests: `tests/unit/technical-lens.test.ts`, updated `stage-tabs` / `s8-goals-ui` suites.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(governance): **Green Field establishment mode, slice 2 (ADR-0167): checklists, mode-based attach, IC standing risk, glossary.** New checklist `checklists/business-unit-formation-checklist.yaml` 1.0.0 (`bu-01..bu-09`, all draft-AI) mirroring the five business-unit steps, with legal wording taken from the design's 2026-10-14 statute check (Commercial Act, VAT Act and Decree, Labor Standards Act via the Ministry of Government Legislation Open API); statements the statute text did not settle (permit holder per statute, certification scope, case-law test for a justified transfer, accounting segment) stay marked as needing confirmation. `entity-formation-checklist.yaml` 1.3.0 tags every item with `modes` (ef-01..ef-19 `new_entity` only, ef-13..ef-18 also `origins: [joint]`, gx-01..gx-03 both modes). `checklists/_validate.ts` 1.6.0 validates the optional `modes` / `origins` tags; the library projection carries them and lists the new checklist. A business_unit project attaches the business-unit checklist (bu-01/04/05 as plan evidence) in place of entity-formation on the intra-venture S9 sub-track; legacy and unchosen modes keep the entity-formation set unchanged. The IC package returns `standing_risks` and the IC screen shows `no liability isolation` as a standing, informational risk line for business_unit projects (never gates, does not change the proposed grade). Glossary 1.4.0 registers Establishment Mode, New Legal Entity Formation, New Business Unit and Business Unit Form; `flow.spec.yaml` S10/S11 text now names both modes (spec version unchanged, generated files refreshed). A contractual unincorporated JV stays out of scope. Counsel confirmations are still open for the items marked as needing confirmation. Tests: `tests/unit/business-unit-checklist.test.ts`.

- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(scripts): **`align-existing-projects.ts` 1.1.0 adds `--apply-approved-decisions`.** Executes only the three decisions the human decider approved (delete the pending duplicate of PRJ-DSE4PW establishment step 1 and re-point its events, re-pin the two stale overlay pins, set DEMO-S1-S12 origin to `external`), each behind a precondition guard, in one transaction with a backup and `data_alignment.approved.<item>` audit rows. The demo seed (`seed-s1-s12-demo.ts` 1.8.3) now creates the demo project with origin `external`. WACC subject, DEMO goals, PRJ-QG5WDY and the S10 mode choice are deliberately not applied.

- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(api): **S8 required-capability attainability, slices 1-2 (ADR-0168 Proposed, DEC-20261014-03).** The S3 capability register gets a shared 5-step level scale (`none`/`basic`/`working`/`skilled`/`leading`, Korean labels in the UI; legacy free text is kept verbatim and read as unjudged) and an optional per-row `attainability` object (closing cost in KRW million, months to attain, human grade high/medium/low or unjudged, rationale, evidence, drafted-by, confirmed-by/at). The shared row parser `normalizeGapRows` now round-trips the object, and the S3/S5 register save carries the stored judgement over by row id (a client cannot drop or forge it); no migration. New `GET/PUT /api/projects/:id/capability-attainability` (per-row draft patch, rationale required for high/low, levels on the scale, cost >= 0, audit `capability_attainability.save`) and human-only `POST .../confirm` (403 `human_only` for bearer/MCP/agent, in `HUMAN_ONLY_ROUTES` and the MCP coverage exclusions, audit `capability_attainability.confirm`). S5 capability table gets 5-step required/current pickers; S8 gets a `필요 역량 달성 가능성` panel (overview tab, compact strip in scenarios) with counts and a warning banner when any row is unjudged or low or the register is empty. Warning only: figures, runs and grades are unchanged and nothing is added to the case automatically. Tests: `tests/unit/capability-attainability.test.ts`.

- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(web): **Green Field establishment mode UI, slice 1 (ADR-0167).** S10 Green Field structure view gets an establishment-mode panel with two radio cards (new legal entity / new business unit); the business-unit card is disabled with a stated reason unless the project origin is internal, there is no default, a person confirms the choice, and the non-binding S5 suggestion is shown as a hint. Changing a stored mode opens a form that requires a reason of 10 to 2000 characters, warns that recorded steps stay as read-only history, and lists the mode history. For a business unit the legal-entity-form select is replaced by a business-unit form select (independent unit / team inside an existing unit / CIC); no field exists for it, so the choice is stored as a labelled prefix in the existing free-text liability-isolation note of the structure hypothesis. The S11 establishment table is titled and filled by the active mode using `step_titles` from the API, shows a notice with a link to S10 and no actions while the mode is not chosen, keeps rows of a previous mode in a collapsed read-only block, and the IC materials chip shows the mode badge. API error codes are mapped to plain Korean in the new `lib/establishment-mode-view.ts`, and the English `establishment mode not chosen` parenthetical of the S11 blocker is stripped on the process and stage screens. The greenfield scenario now targets the visible stage-rail not-applicable marker (the first text match was the hidden compact list after PR #533) and covers the S10 mode panel and the mode-titled S11 table. Tests: `tests/unit/establishment-mode-view.test.ts`.
- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(scripts): **Existing-project data alignment script and report.** Added `scripts/co-newbiz/align-existing-projects.ts` (requires `--db`, dry-run by default, refuses `db/data/` and docker-volume paths unless `--apply --i-know-this-is-a-live-db`). It classifies each gap between projects created before 2026-10 and the current model (ADR-0159..0167, migrations 0135..0142) as deterministic, human-decision or no-action; `--apply` runs only the deterministic backfills in one transaction with a `VACUUM INTO` backup, one `audit_log` row per change (actor `system:data-alignment`) and post-checks, and is idempotent. Added unit tests and `docs/reports/2026-10-14-existing-project-data-alignment.md` (inventory, dry-run results for the dev projects, human decisions and operator commands). No live database was changed.

- **[2026-10-14]**: feat(api): **Green Field promotion defaults project origin to `internal` (ADR-0166 Proposed, DEC-20261014-01; amends ADR-0047 A.5).** `deriveOriginFromSubTrack` returns `internal` for every Green Field sub-track (corrected the same day from an earlier joint default); an explicit `origin` in the promote body (e.g. `joint`) still wins and the Brown Field mapping is unchanged. A freshly promoted Green Field project therefore takes the S11 establishment-steps path. No backfill of existing NULL-origin rows; the isolated demo seed Green Field project keeps `internal`. Open consideration (separate design, not implemented): a new business unit without a new legal entity. Tests: derivation for all tracks/sub-tracks and the promote default in `tests/unit/wave-f8-subtrack-gaps.test.ts` and `tests/unit/project-promotion.test.ts`.

- **[2026-10-14]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0167 (Green Field establishment mode) accepted with notes and ADR-0168 (S8 capability attainability, slices 1-2) accepted by the human decider, recorded separately (DEC-20261014-04, DEC-20261014-05).** Acceptance is by chat instruction with no per-item checklist; known deviations carry owners and exit conditions in each ADR Acceptance record; ADR-0168 slices 3-6 remain open.
- **[2026-10-14]**: fix(web): Align the last strategic-alliance wording in code comments (PostManagementPanel, PortfolioStage) with glossary 1.3.0; no behaviour change. Spec-coupled occurrences (process/flow.spec.yaml and its lib/process-flow.ts projection) and one backend LLM prompt are left for their owners.

- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **User-manual UI wording sweep (D-01..D-24 UI subset, W1/W2/W3 items).** The S4 route-decision screen tells the user the scoring form lives in the decision tab (D-11/W1-08) and shows the recorded decision (track, route, rationale, alternatives, decider) above the empty form when a decision is already on record (D-22/W1-09). Stage rails and stage tabs are now track-aware: stages that do not apply to the deal's track render with the existing not-applicable state instead of disappearing (W3-09/W1-12, status derivation untouched), and a stage screen for the other track shows an explanatory notice card with a jump link (D-03/W2-02). Promoted-project cards, the project header and the project stage tabs link back to the original opportunity's S4 screen ("view original opportunity") via `source_opportunity_id` (W1-04/D-18). The S11 investment-review screen localizes scenario and percentile cells and the overall-grade chips (green/amber/red with plain Korean wording, no "RAG"), S12 replaces raw enum tokens (venture classes, the S12 transition vocabulary, `decided_by=...`) with Korean labels, and the S8 goals banner no longer duplicates the goals-not-set callout (W1-19/W2-07). English and jargon leaks across S5-S12, the proxy-chain page (now the Korean value-chain wording), the report template heading, the admin master-data section (Korean table and column labels), the risk heatmap (Chinese axis characters to plain Korean), the login hero flow and the onboarding demo-data note were replaced with plain Korean; "Failed to fetch" and other raw system errors localize through `localizeMessage`; the S9 workforce/organization plan tab explains why it exists on a Green Field project (W2-08) and the S11 execution-baseline intro states the recording order (W2-09). The partnership term uses the approved Korean term everywhere per glossary 1.3.0 (PostManagementPanel, capability labels, benchmarks, decision-tab preclusion text, DD checklist titles). Guard updates: `tests/unit/table-sizing.test.ts` follows the renamed candidate-list table, `tests/unit/s8-goals-ui.test.ts` pins the de-duplicated banner, `tests/unit/labels.test.ts` adds localization and S12-requirement render guards.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **S8 median-gap cross-check explanation.** The historical-variant row's median-gap cell now appends, when the gap exceeds the fade-DCF divergence threshold, a one-line muted note explaining that the gap is dominated by the growth-assumption difference (market-based vs historical CAGR, with the percentage-point delta) since both variants share the exit multiple, with a jump button to the methodology tab. Guards added to the S8 UI test.

- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(data): **User-manual data defects (D-13, D-15, D-16, W1-05, W1-07, W1-12, W1-14).** The demo seeds stored `bottomUpCagr`/`topDownCagr` as fractions while every consumer and the sizing pipeline use percent numbers, so S1/S2 printed a 13% growth as 0.13%; the seeds now write percent values and the opportunity market size (KRW million) matches the S1 USD anchor at about 1,350 KRW/USD. The S1 anchor label no longer rounds $0.9B up to $1B (`fmtBillions`). The portfolio backtest page appended the "eok" suffix to KRW-million values (1,800 million shown as 1,800 eok); it now converts through `fmtNpvEok`. The project timeline derives stage status from the same process evaluation as the stage rail (S1-S7 from the opportunity, S8-S12 from the project), flags not-applicable stages, no longer marks every timestamp-less stage in progress, and computes an S1 milestone; the demo seeds add a stage SLA policy. The demo seeds link a completed synergy analysis to each opportunity so the S3 list agrees with the confirmed score. The deals board shows a promoted or route-decided opportunity as such instead of the S4 waiting label (`route_decided` added to `GET /api/opportunities`). The route advisory no longer reads a competitive structure that says dispersed as concentrated because of the leader ratio, and the Green Field demo seed now carries high growth so the advisory agrees with the recorded Green Field decision. Regression tests: `tests/unit/user-manual-data-defects.test.ts`.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(wiki): **User manual wiki foundation (segment 1) under `docs/wiki/ko/`.** Home, sidebar and footer, getting-started tutorial, roles and permissions, glossary links, Green Field and Brown Field lane overviews, and shared stage pages for S1 to S4, with screen labels checked against the live dev screens. Screenshots are pending because the logged-in browser cannot save files; defects D-11 to D-20 added to the defect log. Green Field lane completed: lane overview plus S5, S8, S9, S10, S11 and S12 how-to pages (sub-track choice, S9 scope and people/organization plan tab, establishment five steps with assign/reopen/notifications, execution baseline, post-management). Segment 3 (Brown Field lane): the full Brown Field journey page plus S6 to S12 stage pages (`50` to `56`), each keyed to on-screen labels with demo-data captures, the seven sub-tracks, the six mandatory M&A validators, review-status badges, and the entity and group investment committee flow.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(wiki): **User manual wiki, writer 1 (common screens and shared stages).** Embeds the demo-data captures in the S1 to S4 pages, adds the common screens page, the shared S8 to S12 overview pages, project and admin screen guides, FAQ, troubleshooting, change log, persona entry page and the two end-to-end checklists, and rewrites Home, sidebar and footer with the Green Field versus Brown Field comparison.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(tests): **Clear typecheck baseline regressions in three unit tests (types only, no behavior change).** `beta-collection-view` uses a literal-typed index label, `migration-runner` gets typed helper parameters and `{ c: number }` / `{ sql: string }` row casts for `bun:sqlite` `.get()`, and `process-flow` casts through `unknown` and types `subjectScope` values as strings.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(api): **ADR-0165 follow-up: establishment reopen/assign notifications, server `reopened_after_gate`, call path (`via`) in the audit trail, human-only enforced on REST (DEC-20261013-09).** Migration 0141 rebuilds `notification` to allow `establishment_step_reopened` and `establishment_step_assigned` (plus a per-recipient `dedupe_key` unique index) and adds `entity_establishment_step_event.via` (`ui|api|mcp|unknown`, default `unknown`); rollback block included. A reopen notifies the project lead(s) and the step's assignee (never the actor, one notification per recipient per action), an assignment notifies the new assignee; both appear in the bell, inbox and MCP `list_notifications`. `reopened_after_gate` (+ `reopened_after_gate_detail`) is derived once in `lib/establishment.ts` (a reopen newer than the latest valid, non-stale GO of `dd`/`entity_ic`/`group_ic`) and returned by `GET /api/projects/:id/establishment`, the IC package and project process evaluations; `EstablishmentPanel`/`IcReviewStage` render it instead of deriving it. Audit target is now `<projectId>#step<n>@<via>`. Completing a step (`status=done`), assignment and reopen now return 403 `human_only` for bearer tokens, OAuth-delegated MCP calls and agent accounts on the REST routes themselves (including `PUT /api/opportunities/:id/establishment` with `done`), not only at the MCP port.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(jobs): **Execution job claiming no longer drops to "queue empty" under write contention.** `claimNextJob` ran a deferred SELECT-then-UPDATE transaction; when another worker committed in between, SQLite failed the lock upgrade instantly (busy_timeout does not apply), the catch returned null, and a `--loop` worker exited with jobs still queued. The claim transaction is now `immediate`, so busy_timeout applies. This was the root cause of the intermittent `tests/unit/execution-jobs.test.ts` e2e failure (2 workers, 4 jobs); the test now also prints worker output and exit codes on failure.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(docker): **Runtime image missing `lib/dev-origins.ts` (crash loop on `next start`).** `next.config.ts` imports `./lib/dev-origins.ts` (added 2026-10-11), but the runtime stage copied only `next.config.ts`, so a locally built production image crash-looped with `Cannot find module './lib/dev-origins.ts'`; production was rolled back to the previous image and stayed healthy. The runtime stage now copies the file, a new guard test (`tests/unit/dockerfile-next-config-imports.test.ts`) fails when any relative import of `next.config.ts` (transitively) lacks a Dockerfile runtime `COPY`, and the deployment runbook documents the rule and the `docker logs co-newbiz-web` first-check after a failed health check.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **S4 route-decision history shows the full rationale and alternatives.** The compact history table keeps its truncated cells but each row now has a "show more" button (aria-expanded, keyboard accessible) opening a detail row with the full rationale and alternatives (line breaks preserved, wrapped), the score evidence list (criterion, score, evidence note), the staging recommendation text and the full decided-by/at timestamp; truncated cells also carry the full text as a tooltip, and an empty alternatives field reads as an explicit "no alternative recorded" label instead of a dash. View model `lib/route-decision-history-view.ts` with unit tests. The other history views (GateDecisionPanel, portfolio, legacy route page) do not truncate rationale.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(governance): **Legal counsel sign-off protocol approved (human decider, chat) and decree art. 18(3) verified.** New `docs/governance/legal-signoff-protocol.md`, `legal-signoff/` (README, `_template.yaml`): per-item records with authority (law, MST, article), verdict confirm|revise|hold, named signer and qualification, human approver, 30-day rule (informational, never blocks), and the mapping to `review_status`/`reviewer`/`verified_on`. The signer is not yet named, so no item is counsel-reviewed. `checklists/_validate.ts` v1.5.0 validates sign-off files and requires a matching confirm entry for counsel-reviewed/confirmed items. DEC-20261013-08 records the human classification of the entity-establishment-execution 1.1.1 edit as class (a) citation correction (no ADR) with a Commercial Act art. 530-9(2)(4) note. ma-special-topics 1.4.3 ma-02 now states the foreign-company domestic-sales condition (decree art. 18(3)) as verified 2026-10-13 against the statute Open API (MST 284737); the citation verification report gains a dated addendum resolving the counsel/antitrust conflict.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(dd): **Checklist item review status (informational, no schema change).** Items in `checklists/*.yaml` may carry optional `review_status` (draft-AI | counsel-reviewed | confirmed), `reviewer`, `verified_on`; a missing status reads as draft-AI, and counsel-reviewed/confirmed require a named reviewer and a date (`checklists/_validate.ts` v1.4.0 rejects the rest). The status flows through the library projection and the S9 DD workspace items, shows as a badge on each item in the S9 workspace and the checklist library (draft-AI carries the marker text as tooltip/aria), and the S11 IC package adds a one-line count of in-scope items still draft-AI to the could-not-verify area. It never blocks a gate. Design note: status lives on the item, not in the DB, so a counsel review is a reviewed YAML change; no ADR because no schema, authorization or gate behavior changes. Terms decided by the human decider: the Korean wording for the strategic-fit hunt-zone item and the do-minimum option was replaced (strategic-fit-screening-checklist 1.1.1), guarded in `tests/unit/no-entity-group-ic-term.test.ts`; glossary 1.2.0 adds the three statuses.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(dd): **A recorded dd gate decision pins the pre-scope S9 view (human decider, DEC-20261013-05; ADR-0160 addendum f, ADR-0162 addendum c).** Projects with a valid non-stale dd gate decision (any verdict) are excluded from the by-date workspace scope on both tracks, so the retroactive 2026-07-01 cut-off cannot add unassessed extras to a passed gate or flip S9 and later prerequisites; a person-recorded opt-in stays valid and a stale marker releases the pin. Shared helper `hasValidDdGateDecision`. ADR-0165 Decision section extended (step 5 rule, reopen notification scope, warning derivation, owner column removal placeholder, audit limits; status stays Proposed). New read-only operator script `scripts/co-newbiz/scope-impact.ts`.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(dd): **S9 workspace scope applied retroactively from 2026-07-01 (human decider, DEC-20261013-04; ADR-0160 addendum e, ADR-0162 addendum b).** `GF_WORKSPACE_SCOPE_DATE`, `BF_WORKSPACE_SCOPE_DATE` and `GF_SUBTRACK_PREDICATE_DATE` move from the 2026-10-12 placeholder to 2026-07-01 (the sub-track selection must move too, because the greenfield overlay only applies to the two named sub-tracks when that selection is active). `WAVE_F_PREDICATE_DATE` stays 2026-10-08; the brownfield by-date scoped path is now checked ahead of it, so an in-scope-by-date project gets the same scoped extras as an opted-in one. Projects before 2026-07-01 stay grandfathered with the opt-in path unchanged; no item state is deleted. Tests now use pre-2026-07-01 fixtures for the grandfathered cases. Dev impact: PRJ-QGB2W5 moves to the scoped view (41 judgeable, 5 unassessed); no project changes S9 completion status.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(governance): **ADR-0160, ADR-0161 and ADR-0162 acceptance recorded in full, including all addenda and the 2026-10-13 merger-control addendum of ADR-0162 (human decider, chat instruction).** Stale "status stays Proposed" and "human final review pending" sentences in the three ADRs now read "Status at time of writing: Proposed. Accepted 2026-10-13 by the human decider (chat instruction), covering this addendum."; earlier history is kept in past tense. The items recorded as open at the time (DEC-20261012-01 user decision, Row 4 cross review) are not claimed closed. `docs/adr/README.md` rows for ADR-0160..0162 now read Accepted (2026-10-13) and the ADR-0163 row shows its header date (2026-10-12). ADR-0164 and ADR-0165 are unchanged (not approved).
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(governance): **ADR-0164 and ADR-0165 acceptance recorded separately (human decider, chat instruction; DEC-20261013-06 and DEC-20261013-07).** ADR-0164 is Accepted (2026-10-13); ADR-0165 is Accepted (2026-10-13) with notes, listing five accepted deviations with platform-lead as closure owner and the dissent seat's withdrawal conditions. Both Acceptance records state plainly that acceptance was by chat instruction with no per-item checklist, and name what the AI could not verify (ADR-0164: openapi 409 wording, brownfield/JV writer parity, rollback SQL on a real database). `docs/adr/README.md` rows updated. ADR-0162 wording fix: PRJ-T7T81A was on partnership when written, was re-recorded as strategic_investment on 2026-10-12 21:03 and confirmed 2026-10-13, and its opt-in applies to strategic_investment.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(checklists): **Legal citation wording fixes from the statute Open API verification (docs/reports/2026-10-13-legal-citation-verification.md).** entity-formation 1.2.3 (ef-08 rewritten with the Supreme Court 2018du54705 succession principle, Labor Standards Act art. 23(1) and the Dispatch Act art. 2(1) risk; ef-04 adds Commercial Act art. 374 with a counsel-judgement note), ma-special-topics 1.4.2 (ma-03 now says notice of review result instead of approval and scopes the standstill to Fair Trade Act art. 11(8); ma-01/ma-02 add Korean thresholds, the HHI notice source is marked as needing confirmation), legal-regulatory 1.1.1 (lr-04 Korean FDI and technology-protection statutes), deal-shape-presigning 1.1.1 (min-05 Korean basis) and brownfield overlay 1.2.3 (ma-03 and min-05 readings: board-nomination/veto and information exchange labelled as prudent practice or other-jurisdiction rules, observer and sensitive-personal-data triggers marked as non-Korean). Items the report could not verify (individual-consent transfer precedent, non-compete, MNPI, employee-invention statute) are untouched or marked as needing confirmation. Wording only; no classification changes.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **Plain wording for the grade and whole-number NPV everywhere.** The visible "RAG"-prefixed grade label is replaced by the plain grade wording (overall grade, with a green-amber-red legend where colours matter) on the strategy page, the proposal confirmation page, the legal DD checklist (1.1.1), glossary text (business 1.1.3; technical 1.0.2 keeps the old label as an alias) and the IC memo template; the guard test now forbids it in visible app strings. NPV and valuation amounts show whole numbers (no decimals) in the goal and target formatter (`formatTargetValue`, krw_m now whole 100M-KRW units), the incentive NPV column, the acquisition EV in the valuation methodology panel, and the generated business-case narratives (NPV, p05/p50/p95, standard deviation, acquisition price) plus the adoption-recommendation prompt; stored values and IRR/percent displays are unchanged. Skipped: market sizes in USD billions, revenue lines and ratios (not NPV).
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(checklists): **Merger-control and foreign-investment items apply to strategic, minority and CVC brownfield sub-tracks (ADR-0162 addendum, DEC-20261013-03).** On the S9 scoped path only (opt-in or past the cut-off), ma-01..03 attach to strategic_investment, minority_investment and corporate_venture, and min-05 to strategic_investment and corporate_venture (minority already had it); all other items of those extras are excluded. Brownfield overlay 1.2.0 adds extra-item R readings (`readings`) that state the filing trigger; the validator rejects readings on binding, excluded, unknown or baseline items. ma-02 is A for strategic and minority stakes and R for CVC; ma-01 and ma-03 are R; min-05 is R for CVC. Totals: strategic_investment 71 -> 75, minority_investment 87 -> 90, corporate_venture 71 -> 75. Legacy unscoped projects are unchanged. Readings await antitrust and legal confirmation. Review wording fixes: overlay 1.2.1 and ma-special-topics 1.4.1 (gun-jumping applies only where a pre-closing filing is required, common-ownership and HSR passive-investment wording, FDI trigger list), entity-formation 1.2.1 and greenfield-feasibility 1.1.1 Korean text refinements; no classification changes. Follow-up user decision: corporate_venture ma-02 is A, and min-04 applies to strategic_investment with code R (overlay 1.2.2; strategic_investment 71/76). Company-split citations (entity-formation ef-19 v1.2.2 and the entity-establishment-execution procedure text, v1.1.1) corrected to arts. 530-3, 530-9, 530-11 and 527-5, verified against the statute Open API on 2026-10-13; the former art. 527-2 reference (simplified merger) was wrong.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(checklists): **Korean item text for five more checklists.** `site-selection` (ss-01..09, v1.2.0), `entity-formation` (ef-01..19, gx-01..03, v1.2.0), `greenfield-feasibility` (gf-01..11, v1.1.0), `legal-regulatory` (lr-01..07, v1.1.0) and `strategic-fit-screening` (sf-01..12, v1.1.0). Ids, mandatory/waiver flags, gates and owners are unchanged; project terms are used for the investing party, the establishment entity form and the investment committees. Legal items (entity formation corporate law, foreign-investment and NDA wording) are flagged for legal-counsel review.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **S11 establishment table shows a visible reopen button for done steps.** Done steps previously showed only a done label and the reopen action was hidden in an icon-only row menu; a visible ghost button now opens the existing reopen form (reason of 10+ characters, step-5 cascade confirmation), the row menu keeps assignee assignment with a clearer accessible label, and a hint under the table explains how to change status when every step is done.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **S11 investment review material simulation NPV (millions of KRW) is shown as whole numbers with thousand separators** (new display helper `lib/npv-format.ts`); stored and API values and IRR formatting are unchanged. Unit test added. The same whole-number display now applies to NPV on the S8 target comparison, scenario matrix, fade-DCF cross-check, the valuation methodology explainer (whole eok) and the back-test screen.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(api): **S11 establishment assign/status/reopen no longer create a duplicate step row.** The step lookup matched `checklist_item IS NULL`, so a seeded row carrying a checklist item was missed and a second empty `pending` row was inserted by assign. Lookup now resolves the existing row of `(project, step_no)` regardless of checklist item or origin (legacy backlink rows included); `listEstablishmentSteps` collapses to one row per step (most advanced status, then assigned, then oldest) so already-created stray duplicates are hidden. `establishmentProgress` counts distinct done steps and was not affected. Regression test added.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(web): **S11 establishment table with assignee, status change and reopen (ADR-0165, UI), and one merged committee decision section.** The establishment table now has columns step, status, assignee, last recorded, updated and actions: a "change status" form (evidence is required only when done is chosen), a row menu with assign and reopen (reason of 10+ characters; a second confirmation sends `cascade_step5` when step 5 is done), a per-step history disclosure, a reopen count badge, and a warning when a step was reopened after a GO decision. Brownfield projects see an explanatory note (ADR-0047) instead of an empty table, and the wrong caption is replaced. The client no longer PUTs the old body (`owner` semantics). The two duplicate decision panels are merged into one `GateDecisionPanel` (`sequence` mode) with an entity-then-group step indicator (pending/GO/HOLD/KILL), a prominent target committee that defaults to the current turn, the group form opening only after the entity GO, and per-committee history; gate rules and API are unchanged. Jump anchors now match multi-gate panels via `data-gate-form~=`. New view-model libs `lib/establishment-view.ts` and `lib/gate-sequence-view.ts` with unit tests.
- **[2026-10-13]**: feat(api): **S11 establishment steps: assignee, server-enforced status rules, audited reopen (ADR-0165, Proposed; backend).** Migration 0140 adds `entity_establishment_step.assignee_user_id/assigned_by/assigned_at` and the append-only `entity_establishment_step_event` history (reopen needs a reason of 10+ characters, enforced by CHECK; no backfill). `PUT /api/projects/:id/establishment` is now a status change (`in_progress|done|halted|escalated`): `done` needs an evidence URL or note on the server (`evidence_required`), the origin must be internal/joint and match (`not_applicable`), and a done step is only changed through the new `POST .../establishment/reopen` (409 `reopen_required`; reopening steps 1-4 while step 5 is done needs `cascade_step5`). New `PUT .../establishment/assignee` (active members only). `done`, assignment and reopen are human-only (`HUMAN_ONLY_ROUTES` + coverage exclusions); MCP `record_establishment` is limited to `in_progress|halted|escalated`. GET adds `assignee_*`, `last_recorded_by`, `reopen_count`, `members` and `history`. Audit target is now `<projectId>#step<n>` with actions `update|complete|assign|reopen|reopen_cascade`.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **Replaced the terms "Entity IC", "Group IC" and "IC review" in user-visible Korean wording** with the field terms for the entity investment committee, the group investment committee and a short form for the review step (tab, labels, S11 text, process-flow spec and document, glossary, technical-dd checklist item v1.0.1). Glossary `business.yaml` v1.1.2 keeps the English forms and the old short forms as aliases so lookups still resolve; gate ids, procedure gate keys, file names and ADRs are unchanged. New guard test `tests/unit/no-entity-group-ic-term.test.ts`.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(web): **Renamed the user-facing term for the Investment Committee package.** The old "IC package" wording was not used in the field; UI labels, S11 stage text, process-flow spec and projections, the financing checklist (v1.3.1), ui-spec and unit-test assertions now use the field term for it (investment review material). Route paths, API fields and MCP tool names (get_project_ic_package) are unchanged. Added a glossary entry (business.yaml v1.1.1) and a guard test (tests/unit/no-ic-package-term.test.ts) that rejects the old wording in app/web-next source.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(wacc): **Beta regression pairs the stock with the index of its listing market.** `GET beta-series` now returns `listing_market`, `default_index` and `index_options` and accepts `?index=KOSPI|KOSDAQ` (any other value returns 400); when the requested index has no data it falls back to the index that does. The listing market is stored in the existing `raw_payload` table (id `krx:stock-master:<company id>`) with no migration. Collector 1.2.0 records it, and `--backfill-market` fills already collected rows with zero KRX calls.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(wacc): **Beta panel redesign.** Step 1 "get data" card (collected data vs paste, index select and load button on one row, status summary, raw rows collapsed) and step 2 "calculate" card; the undefined `ghost-button` class (which rendered transparent buttons) is replaced by the design-system classes.
- **[2026-10-13]**: docs(checklists): **Korean item text for two more checklists.** `brownfield-feasibility` (bf-01..13, v1.2.0, including the term change) and `ma-special-topics` (ma-01..12, v1.4.0).
- **[2026-10-13]**: chore(terminology): **Transliteration of "investment vehicle" replaced.** Now a native Korean term for the investing party (brownfield) and one for the establishment entity form (greenfield) in flow.spec S10/S11 text (wording only, version stays 2.8.0), `process-flow.ts`, S10 labels, bf-12, ui-spec and the business manual; two glossary entries added (Investment Vehicle, Establishment Entity Form; glossary 1.1.0) and a test guards the term.
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(s9): **Clearer notice for not-counted DD tabs.**
- **[2026-10-13]**: fix(review): **AI second-review status message states the reason itself** instead of "shown below" (it is shown above); the developer-details disclosure is renamed "raw record (for developers)" with an explanatory note.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(s8): **Scenario table row actions redesigned.** Adopt stays a visible ghost button; lines edit and delete moved into a row menu (delete keeps its confirm dialog). The lines action is renamed "edit lines" and now scrolls the editor into view with focus and a caption naming the target, scenario and variant (it previously opened an editor at the bottom of the page, so it looked like nothing happened).
- **[2026-10-12]**: docs(checklists): **Korean item text for sub-track checklists.** `partnership-checklist` (pt-01..12, v1.2.0) and `deal-shape-presigning-checklist` (lic/cdv/min/jvd, 26 items, v1.1.0) now carry Korean item text shown on the S9 sub-track tabs.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(s9): **Korean tab titles for sub-track checklist tabs** and for the first three domain titles in the dd payload (interim wording, not yet in the glossary).
- **[2026-10-12]**: chore(scripts): **Version bumps** beta-series 1.1.0, newbiz 1.21.0, source-adapters 1.3.0; `SCRIPTS.md` and `VERSION_MANIFEST.md` regenerated.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(beta): **KRX beta series collection now actually collects.** The adapter calls the daily service with `basDd` per weekday instead of a date range that KRX ignored (empty results), matches the index name exactly, and reads the stock close field `TDD_CLSPRC` matched by `ISU_CD`. `md_period` day rows were written with an invalid `period_type` `day` and no `fiscal_year`, so every row failed; they now use `date` + `fiscal_year` (also fixed in `newbiz fetch` for day series). Calls are throttled with 403/429 retry and a per-run call budget; collection output reports per-series counts and the likely cause instead of ending silently; the stock master lookup surfaces the failure reason. Runbooks (en/ko) updated.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(wacc): **Beta panel no longer shows a successful load as failed, and explains empty collections.** The panel read a non-existent `ok` field of the response envelope (now `parseBetaSeriesResponse`). `GET beta-series` adds `collection{counts, dates, status none|no_stock|no_index|insufficient|ready, hint, runbook_command}`; the panel shows Korean guidance, a collector command and a copy button. API spec and OpenAPI updated.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(s8): **Business-case comparison table polish.** Fixed badge overlap and column widths, collapsed the raw provenance string, reworded the goals banner and removed the stale sr-only tab note.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(s12): **Hydration error from RawKey details inside a paragraph** in PortfolioStage, `projects/[id]/page` and `projects/[id]/process/page`.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(s11): **Execution baseline panel shows Korean-only finding text and formatted money** (`fmtMoney`).
- **[2026-10-12]**: chore(lint): **Cleared 4 app/web-next design-lint violations.** `window.confirm` replaced by `useConfirm` in connected-apps; route-decision now uses StageFrame S4; `/our-company` removed from the `STAGE_ROUTES` list in `design-lint.ts` because ui-label-sweep pins that `/agents` and `/our-company` do not use StageFrame.
- **[2026-10-12]**: docs: **S8-S12 human review checklist report** (`docs/reports/2026-10-12-s8-s12-human-review-checklist.md`).
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(web): **Table sizing batch 3 — rollout complete.** All remaining wide tables moved onto the shared ColGroup/table-fixed-pct/tmin system: backtest, charter KPI, strategy matrix, establishment steps (IcReviewStage), Long List, proxy-chain, share, DataTab, admin users, synergy tabs, beta panel and ~20 more wide tables. New design:lint rule R10 flags any wide table (6+ header cells) using `.data-table` without `table-fixed-pct`, or with hardcoded th width classes, with a documented `{/* table-sizing: skip */}` escape marker; `KNOWN_OFFENDERS` in `tests/unit/table-sizing.test.ts` is emptied (batch-3 source pins added) and zero exceptions were needed.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(greenfield): **ADR-0161 greenfield execution baseline (S11) and post-management (S12) scoped by track.** New procedure `greenfield-execution-baseline` v1.0.0 (six steps; step 1 reconciles live `capex_tranche` rows against the S8 adopted case capex lines and never writes tranche status; step 6 is human confirmation by the new human role `execution-baseline-confirmer`); `portfolio-venture-monitoring` v1.2.0 widens its predicate with `case_type eq greenfield` and documents the greenfield measure keys, transition mapping and the default `venture_class` (driving / enabling / emergent, changeable and audited by the recorder); `pmi-integration-execution` v1.1.0 gains additive step 6 (post-close integration monitoring, reading the existing `pmi_*` ledgers); `jv-lifecycle-governance` v1.1.0 gains a partnership variant of step 2 (`partnership-monitoring`); `entity-ic-review` v1.3.0 scopes step 3 to brownfield and adds the greenfield IC memo step 3.1 (greenfield-agent with financing-agent co-review); `flow.spec.yaml` bumped to v2.8.0 (S11 activity `greenfield-execution-baseline`, S12 activities `integration-monitoring` and `partnership-monitoring`, track-neutral S11 objective wording, S12 requiredArtifacts and a greenfield completion criterion, mirrored in `lib/process-flow.ts`); `entity-formation-checklist` v1.1.0 adds gx-01..gx-03 (gx-03 human confirmation is binding, `waiver_eligible: false`); `financing-plan-checklist` v1.3.0 scopes fin-04..fin-08 to brownfield at S11 in its header (item text and S9 overlays unchanged; binding fin-09 carries a greenfield reading of funds flow as the capex drawdown plan). The brownfield m_and_a baseline stays byte-identical. No new migration.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(web-next): **Human-only routes for the greenfield execution baseline, retroactive policy migration and greenfield scorecard cycles.** `GET/POST /api/projects/{id}/execution-baseline`: GET reconciles live `capex_tranche` rows (planned and released only; withheld listed but excluded, retired never listed) against the S8 adopted case capex lines in KRW_M, lists source permits, certifications, incentives and ADR-0159 goals (reference only); POST `record_entries` stores permit and certification milestones as separate lists, an aggregate-only hiring plan, KPI targets and incentive obligations; `confirm` is restricted to the project lead (403 `lead_required`) and requires a rationale plus `acknowledged_findings` for every open finding code (409 `baseline_empty` / `findings_open`); reads and writes run in one immediate transaction; a tranche change after confirmation sets `reconfirm_required`. `GET/POST /api/projects/{id}/policy-migration`: one human-initiated audited `policy_migration` entry per project (`adr`, `from_spec` 2.7.0, `to_spec` 2.8.0, `migration_note`, server-computed `completed_stages`); recording is lead-only; no re-pin of `project_policy_pin`. `GET /api/projects/{id}/post-management` returns S12 applicability (all recommended), read views over `pmi_*`, `jv_term` and `venture_scorecard`, and the greenfield ramp-up view; `POST /api/projects/{id}/post-management/scorecard` records one greenfield ramp-up cycle with the Q7 default `venture_class` prefilled and any override audited (brownfield 409 `not_applicable_track`, unresolved track 409 `needs_classification`, cycles land on the latest run not already carrying those measures, else 409 `run_required`). All three POST routes are human-only (`HUMAN_ONLY_ROUTES`, mcp-coverage exclusions; cookie, CSRF, analyst+, MNPI insider check on reads too). S8 goals stay reference evidence and never block a gate.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(policy): **Retroactive application rule (ADR-0161 Q2).** The v2.8.0 predicates apply to all in-flight projects without re-pinning. New steps are recommended, not blocking, for stages completed before 2026-10-12, judged only by a `policy_migration` audit entry whose server-completed `completed_stages` lists the stage (IC-GO dates are not evidence of a completed S11 — review fix R1); they block only for stages not yet reached. S12 items are never blocking. A `case_type` vs route-track conflict blocks S11 with an explicit `classification_conflict` blocker; no track signal never blocks and shows a warning instead (`lib/project-track.ts` is the single resolver for S11, S12 and PMI). Nothing is deleted and no completed gate is reopened.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(web-next): **PMI guards for non-applicable tracks.** The PMI tab is removed from the S12 stage tabs (`stageTabGroupsFor({ pmiApplies })`) and the PMI surface when `pmiKickoffApplies` is false; the writes to `pmi/kickoff`, `pmi/synergy-certifications[/:id]` and `pmi/key-talent[/:id]` return 403 `not_applicable_track` (the kickoff error path changed from 400 `not_applicable` to 403 `not_applicable_track`). Reads are unchanged and the brownfield m_and_a path is untouched.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(web-next): **PMI key-talent status validation.** The status field now validates against the DB values `active | departed_regretted | departed_planned`; the previously accepted `retained` / `exited` values now return 400 instead of writing rows that violate the schema CHECK.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(security): **Committee session IDOR fixed.** The committee transcript, minutes GET/POST, advance and positions routes bind the session to the path opportunity and to a genuine committee run (a run that is not an `s<N>_*` ledger and carries `meta_json.committee_key`, with promoted-project visibility); `confirmCommitteeMinutes` refuses ledger runs, and an `s11_execution_baseline` run answers 404 and is never rewritten. The business-case run GET also excludes that stage.
- **[2026-10-12]**: docs(governance): **ADR-0161 and DEC-20261012-02 recorded.** `docs/adr/0161-greenfield-execution-and-post-management-by-track.md` (Proposed; updated 2026-10-12 with the Q1-Q7 chat decisions and Row 1 / Row 2 / review-fix addenda) and `docs/decisions/DEC-20261012-02.md` cover how the decisions were encoded; design doc `2026-10-11-greenfield-investment-execution-and-post-management-design.md` updated to the decided state. The ADR, the procedure/flow.spec changes and the retroactive application cannot pass on AI review alone and stay awaiting human final review.
- **[2026-10-12]**: docs(design): **Design drafts for later work.** S11/S12 greenfield execution and post-management by track (design 2026-10-11-greenfield-investment-execution-and-post-management, ADR-0161 draft, not implemented) and the brownfield S9 DD scope design (2026-10-12-brownfield-dd-workspace-scope-by-subtrack). Spec entries added to the registry.
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(web-next): **Review-surface fixes.** `IcReviewStage` busy-state race closed, `CarrierGate` breadcrumb corrected, the S9 panel now has priority on the people/organization plan, and site wording is neutral (no site assumed for tracks without one).
- **[2026-10-12]**: fix(security): **Hardening found in the pre-merge reviews.** Capex edits are locked while a release is pending; custom DD items carry provenance and an audit entry and cannot be reviewed by their own author; scope opt-ins run in one atomic transaction; input length limits added on the new routes; the DD tailor review route is human-only.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(checklists): **Korean translations of six checklists (text only, no item ids or applicability changed).** `rbd-technology` 1.5.0, `financial-dd` 1.2.0, `hr-dd` 1.2.0, `market-dd` 1.2.0, `financing-plan` 1.2.0, `legal-dd` 1.1.0. Wording still awaiting glossary review is marked `INTERIM-WORDING`.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(dd): **Seven DD domain tabs fixed, custom DD items by domain, flow.spec v2.7.0.** Items that were already counted per domain but never shown now appear in the seven tabs; migration 0137 adds domains to custom DD items; new human-only custom item route `POST /api/projects/{id}/dd-checklist/custom`; `process/flow.spec.yaml` 2.7.0 adds four S9 activities.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(dd): **S9 DD workspace scoped per track (ADR-0160 addendum b, ADR-0162, decision DEC-20261012-01).** Greenfield and brownfield overlays (`checklists/_overlays/*-workspace-scope.yaml`, codes A/R/X/N, validated by `checklists/_validate.ts`) decide which workspace items apply to each sub-track. Opt-in with preview, recorded atomically; a brownfield opt-in also attaches the sub-track's extra checklists; the HR tab is counted for in-scope greenfield; binding items are always A. m_and_a stays byte-identical.
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(greenfield): **Greenfield S9/S10 by sub-track (ADR-0160).** Procedures `greenfield-dd` 1.1.0 and `deal-structuring` 1.1.0; migration 0136 adds routing variables; migration 0138 allows a single-person `site_required` confirmation without an evidence reference (audited as `dimension_input_single_person_confirmation`); capex tranche table with two-person release; establishment structure fields (vehicle, funding sources, incentives).
- **[2026-10-12]**: feat(s8): **Project-level business goals (ADR-0159, design 2026-10-11-s8-project-business-goals).** Migration 0135 adds `project_business_goal` (append-only versions, soft delete); human-only `POST /api/projects/{id}/business-goals`; resolution order project > affiliate > group; adopting a scenario requires a `goals_ack` acknowledgement with a note; attainment shown per scenario; the greenfield target-compare tab is hidden. Goals remain reference evidence and never block a gate or change RAG.
