- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(web-next): **Shared table column-sizing system, batch 1 (design 2026-10-11-table-column-sizing-system).** An app-wide audit (about 125 tables, no `<colgroup>`, no shared table component) found twelve tables where one column absorbs the width change while the others stay fixed (S2 funnel 100%, dd checklist 100%, API-token label 100%, greenfield decision history 99%, and others), plus the S6/S7 pre-review / committee table at 1424 px with no scroll region. New `ColGroup` helper in `components/ui.tsx` (numbers only; in development it throws when the widths do not sum to 100 or one is not a positive number), `.table-fixed-pct` with minimum-width tokens `tmin-32…80`, `.cell-truncate` (ellipsis plus tooltip), `.cell-clamp-3`, `.list-wide` and `.list-mid`; the mobile rule that forced `width: max-content` on every `.data-table` no longer applies to fixed-percentage tables. A guard test (`tests/unit/table-sizing.test.ts`) requires a `.table-scroll` ancestor or an allowlist entry for every table, a `ColGroup` whose length matches the header cells for fixed-percentage tables, forbids `auto` or `100%` column widths, and keeps a shrinking list of known offenders that must be empty after batch 3. Migrated in this batch: the S2 opportunity funnel (14/34/12/16/24, name truncated with a tooltip; replaces the bespoke rem widths), the dd checklist (44/9/17/30, and 64/12/24 for read-only viewers; mobile card stack kept), the S6/S7 pre-review table (now 1280 px inside a focusable `.table-scroll` region, was 1424 px unscrolled; the preset follows the checklist length), and the PR 509 affiliate and competitor tables moved onto the helper. Measured in a logged-in browser at 1280, 1024, 768 and 375 px: every migrated table keeps identical column shares at all widths (0 percentage-point difference) with no cell overflow. Batches 2 and 3 migrate the remaining tables; design-lint rules follow in batch 4.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(web-next): **Deal overview, group/affiliate screen and company list UI refinements (designs 2026-10-11-deal-overview-ui-refinement, our-company-ui-refinement, companies-list-exclude-group-affiliates).** *Deal overview (`/deals/<id>`)*: the deal name is now the page heading (and `document.title`) with status, current-stage and HOLD chips; the shared `StageRail` stepper gets an optional status prop (check, filled disc, minus icons with screen-reader state words, muted pending steps, compact mode naming the previous and next stage; other rail users are unchanged); a two-column grid at 1024 px and up (current stage with "receives from / hands to" and artifact counts, a next-action card with one specific primary button and reason, completion criteria with "N of M" and a next marker; HOLD card and key facts on the side) and one column below; key facts distinguish value, pending ("decided at S4"), missing (with a link to where to record it) and no gate decision (next gate with a link); hold age comes from the gate-decision history; per-region skeletons and error states with retry. The data contract in `lib/deal-overview.ts` is extended (identity, structured next action, fact states, flow artifacts, hold age, completion counts) and `GET /api/projects/{id}/process` returns the project name. *Group/affiliate screen (`/our-company`)*: one-line header with a closed "how this is used" section (not shown in the embedded S3 tab), emoji removed from headings, group cards with a clear selected state (accent border, tint, check icon), group header with "N affiliates, M analyzed, last analysis" and a 90-day stale cue (`PROFILE_STALE_DAYS`), one visible main action per region with the rest in a shared keyboard-accessible `RowMenu` (affiliate rows: one main action plus a menu; group header: register plus a menu; destructive actions only in menus with the existing confirm; member-detail toolbar, forms and competitor rows reduced the same way), and the affiliate and confirmed-competitor tables use percentage column widths (`<colgroup>`, no auto column, name truncated with an ellipsis and tooltip) so every column scales together instead of only the name column stretching. *Company list (`/companies`)*: `GET /api/companies?scope=targets` hides companies that belong to the group/affiliate profile (matched by DART code, or by exact name when the company has no code; no data deleted, no schema change) and the internal synergy-registry sentinel row; the project "connect target company" search keeps the unscoped list. Full unit suite: 2189 pass.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(web-next): **Deal overview rebuilt and the S3 synergy tab scoped to the deal (design 2026-10-11-deal-overview-and-s3-deal-scope).** A logged-in review of an S3 deal found the overview page (`/deals/<id>`) nearly empty (a stepper of bare numbers, an unlabeled four-line "what to do now" list with raw `(selected)` values, a blank "track … progress:" value, empty input/output lines, no key facts, both "review/deal board" and "current deal overview" highlighted in the sidebar, a breadcrumb duplicating the top-bar back), and the S3 tab listing only other deals' synergy analyses (none linked to this deal) with no explanation and no per-deal action. The overview now has a named stepper (stage names from the shared metadata), labelled sections (current stage with objective, one primary next action, completion criteria with done and not-done state, other blockers), a key-facts card (attractiveness, synergy with an "unconfirmed" note while no confirmation is recorded, track, gates, HOLD rows linking to the decision screen), an input/output strip only when a previous or next stage exists, every empty value shown as "not recorded" through `displayOrNotRecorded`, and a single active sidebar item; the data model lives in the pure helper `lib/deal-overview.ts`. The S3 tab fetches `GET /api/synergy/analyses?opportunityId=<id>` (new optional filter; an unknown id returns an empty list, no change when omitted), defaults to "this deal only" with an "all" toggle and a "this deal" badge, shows an explicit empty state with "start analysis for this deal" (opens the form pre-filled with the deal; nothing is submitted automatically, the existing profile-required handling is unchanged; disabled with a visible reason without analyst permission or without a source opportunity), and the company table scrolls inside `.table-scroll`. Project deals (the route id is a project id) resolve their source opportunity through one shared resolver (`lib/deal-carrier.ts`, `useDealOpportunityId`) used by both the overview and S3. `localizeRequirement` also strips an English key after Korean text even with a space before the parenthesis and capitalizes a lowercase gate token. No schema, auth or MCP change; no ADR. Full unit suite: 2146 pass.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **S2 funnel action buttons no longer cover the synergy column (design 2026-10-11-s2-funnel-action-column-overflow).** In the S2 opportunity funnel table the actions cell was 208 px wide, one non-wrapping right-aligned row, and held two or three buttons (up to about 272 px: "go to S3" or "attractiveness evaluation", "business model review", "delete"), so it overflowed to the left by 9–72 px and covered the synergy score and recommendation text (the table is `table-layout: fixed`). `.sizing-actions-cell > .actions-row` now wraps with a token gap, and a new `.list-table.opp-funnel-table` (min 56 rem, max 80 rem, modelled on the S1 list table) with wider synergy (10 rem) and actions (20 rem) columns keeps every row's buttons on one line at the desktop width while the table stays inside `.table-scroll` and scrolls horizontally on narrow viewports. Measured in a logged-in browser at 1349, 1024, 768 and 375 px: no cell content crosses its cell rect; the sibling S1, S3, project-list and deal-board tables had no overlap. Labels and behaviour are unchanged.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Review/deal board links go straight to the deal's own stage instead of through the S2 redirect (design 2026-10-11-direct-deal-stage-links).** S1–S3 are list-structured, but the board built every opportunity link as `/opportunities?opportunity=<id>`, which `CarrierGate` always redirects to `/deals/<id>/s2`, so an assessed (S3) or selected (S4) deal always opened S2 "discovery evaluation" after an extra redirect hop and an "auto-moved" notice. A shared client-safe helper `lib/deal-links.ts` (`dealStageHref`, `opportunityStatusHref`; an unknown stage falls back to the deal overview) now builds `/deals/<id>/<stage>` at the six places that used the redirect hop: the board's opportunity cards (the card's own stage: identified S2, assessed S3, selected S4), the board's HOLD aging rows (the stage hosting the gate decision via `gateHoldHref`), the portfolio view, the project list's "view opportunity", the project process screen's opportunity-stage link, and the `href` value of the portfolio overlap API (field name unchanged). The `/opportunities?opportunity=` redirect and the flat routes stay for old deep links, bookmarks and notifications. Reviewed but left unchanged: `/route-decision` pickers link every candidate to S4, and the `CarrierGate` picker rows use the flat route's fixed stage.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(config): **Dev-server allowed origins and the production origin settings are configured from `.env`, documented in tracked samples, with a production start-up warning (design 2026-10-11-port-swap-and-public-domain-access, sections 6-7).** A new Cloudflare or Tailscale hostname in front of the dev server (`dev-newbiz.bizknights.org` was the case) made Next block HMR and dev chunks with 403 until the host was added to `allowedDevOrigins` in code. `CO_NEWBIZ_ALLOWED_DEV_ORIGINS` (comma or whitespace separated bare hosts or IPs; scheme, port and path stripped with a warning; invalid tokens dropped) is now read by `next.config.ts` through the pure helper `lib/dev-origins.ts` from the process environment or, as a fallback, from the single key in the repo-root `.env` (Next itself only loads `app/web-next/.env*`), and is added to the built-in defaults (which now also cover `dev-newbiz.bizknights.org`, the current Tailscale IP and its MagicDNS name, so nothing breaks when the variable is unset). Production already takes its origin settings from `.env.prod` through the compose `env_file`; they are now documented in a Production section of the tracked `.env.sample` (placeholders only): `CO_NEWBIZ_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` (pins the OAuth/MCP issuer, required in production), `CO_NEWBIZ_MCP_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, `CO_NEWBIZ_OAUTH_REDIRECT_HOST_ALLOWLIST`; the runbooks (EN/KO) gain a "Production environment settings" section (the container reads `.env.prod`, not the root `.env`; apply changes with `docker compose up -d --force-recreate`; the cookie-session CSRF check only compares the Origin host with the Host header, so new public hostnames need no app setting). In production a boot-time `console.warn` (never blocking) now fires when `CO_NEWBIZ_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` is unset or not a valid http(s) origin (`lib/public-origin-check.ts`). `.env.sample` is brought up to date: the API base URL (dev 7748, Docker production 7749, public `https://newbiz.bizknights.org`; the outdated Hono reference is removed), personal API tokens issued in Settings > Connected apps (shown once, `cnb_pat_`, 30-day default and 90-day maximum, 5 active per user, read or read+write, no write for viewer or admin, hosted AI apps use OAuth with `<origin>/api/mcp` instead of a pasted token), the dev-origins example, the Production section, and the operator-settable `CO_NEWBIZ_COMMITTEE_AGENTS`, `CO_NEWBIZ_BUSINESS_CASE_AGENTS` and `CO_NEWBIZ_DB_PATH`.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(docker): **The production image build failed because the Dockerfile left out top-level directories the app imports (design 2026-10-11-docker-build-missing-workspace-dirs).** While cutting the public site over to the production container, `docker compose build` failed in the build stage with Turbopack `Module not found` for `mcp/co-newbiz/registry.ts` and `packages/core-scoring/src/*` (plus follow-on missing-export errors). The Dockerfile copied only `db`, `scripts`, `procedures`, `process`, `docs` and `app/web-next`, and the whitelist `.dockerignore` hid everything else, so `mcp/` (imported by the `/api/mcp` route) and `packages/core-scoring` (re-exported by `db/lib`) were absent; it went unnoticed because GitHub Actions has been blocked by billing since 2026-10-08. A scan of imports that escape `app/web-next` found two more directories read at runtime, `glossary/` (the glossary directory locator throws without it) and `config/` (`llm-pricing.yaml`). The build and runtime stages now copy `mcp`, `packages`, `glossary` and `config`, and `.dockerignore` re-includes them (package tests and nested `node_modules` stay excluded). Verified: the image builds, an isolated smoke container (port 7790, no volume) passes `/api/health` and serves `/login`, and the production container started on 7749 against the existing data volume (pre-deploy backup taken, 14 pending migrations applied) with the public site answering 200 from the production server.
- **[2026-10-11]**: chore(deploy): **Port swap (host development 7748, Docker 7749:7749) and the public hostname added to the dev-origin allowlist (design 2026-10-11-port-swap-and-public-domain-access).** The public site `newbiz.bizknights.org` was found to be served by the development server through the Cloudflare tunnel (the HTML carried the Turbopack HMR client), and the dev server answered 403 for dev-only resources when the request origin was the new hostname because `allowedDevOrigins` only listed the older `co-newbiz.bizknights.org`. `newbiz.bizknights.org` is now in the list (older host kept, pinned by `tests/unit/next-config-origins.test.ts`). Ports are swapped as requested: host development (`bun run dev` and `start`) uses 7748, `docker-compose.yml` maps `7749:7749` (container-internal port and healthcheck stay 7749), and `.claude/launch.json`, `.env.sample` and the local MCP client default (`CO_NEWBIZ_API_BASE_URL`), `README.md`, `scripts/deploy-web.ts` (health URL), the scenario harness (it now forbids both 7748 and 7749) and the runbooks (English and Korean, regenerated library projection) follow. `deployment.md` gains an "Operator cutover steps" section with a rollback line: stop dev on 7749, start dev on 7748, bring up the production container, check health and the public URL. Operator-only checks recorded: the tunnel target port, running containers, `CO_NEWBIZ_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` in `.env.prod`, and whether the tunnel rewrites the Host header (the cookie-session CSRF check requires the Origin host to equal the Host header).
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Navigation consistency, connected-apps token table and decision-inbox links (design 2026-10-11-ui-polish-back-nav-token-table-inbox-link).** `/projects` is the only list screen that rendered an in-page breadcrumb instead of the top-bar back button; it now uses the same `AppShell` back ("review/deal board") as the other flat routes, and the duplicate in-page breadcrumbs were removed from `/route-decision`, `/agents`, `/our-company`, the `CarrierGate` screens and the non-deal S1 list. The personal API token table on `/settings/connected-apps` was crowded because the shared fixed table layout gave every column equal width (identifier wrapped, date cells overflowed into the revoke button): a `.pat-token-table` modifier gives per-column minimum widths, nowrap identifier and dates in a compact ko-KR short format, a flexible label with ellipsis and tooltip, and it now fits without scrolling at 1208 px while keeping the horizontal-scroll fallback below that. The decision-inbox gate HOLD button opened `/opportunities/<id>` (which redirects to S2) although the gate decision is recorded elsewhere: `gateHoldHref` maps each gate to the stage tab hosting its decision panel (screening to S3, pre_fs to S5, target_screening to S7, fs to S8, dd to S9, entity_ic and group_ic to S11); the set of inbox items is unchanged.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(portfolio): **Portfolio-view overlap detection on a verified industry key instead of free-text scope matching (design 2026-10-11-portfolio-view-overlap).** `/portfolio-view` detected duplicate market exposure client-side by exact string equality on `opportunity.market_scope`, which missed same-industry opportunities worded differently, falsely grouped coincidences, and never showed promoted projects (the cases with deployed capital). The new read-only `GET /api/portfolio/overlap` (analyst+ read; admin refused per the business-content separation) groups active opportunities and visible projects by `md_industry.id` — `opportunity.industry_id` and live `project_industry` bindings, no migration — in an `exact` tier (FK-verified identity) kept strictly separate from a labelled `name_prefix` fallback (identical scope text with no pinned industry), counts a promoted opportunity once on the project side, excludes dropped, deleted and superseded rows, and lists items it cannot key in an honest N/A section (no pinned industry, no live binding, industry unresolved) instead of force-grouping. Review fixes from group-review-agent: promoted opportunities whose project the caller cannot see are surfaced as a count only (no ids or names) so the exposure is never silently understated; an unknown industry id degrades to N/A instead of failing the whole route with a 500; the empty state never claims "no overlap" while items are N/A or hidden; a limits note explains that opportunity-to-project overlap needs a pinned industry and a project binding; the group header prints the industry name once (shared `dedupTermNames`). Proposal-only wording kept: the group suggests a combined synergy review or timing coordination and the judgement belongs to Group IC. Open policy questions recorded: whether MNPI projects belong in a portfolio-wide view and whether MNPI-excluded promoted cases count as hidden.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Projects list breadcrumb and connected-apps issue-form layout, with a 375px pass (design 2026-10-11-ui-label-and-mobile-table-sweep section 11).** The projects list had no way back (no breadcrumb above the h1); it now uses the shared breadcrumb (deal board > projects) like comparable pages. The personal-token issue form on Connected apps was an ad-hoc squeezed flex row; it is now a labeled grid (labels stacked above full-width controls, desktop row with a wide label column, single column below 640px, token-based classes) and the token list date cells no longer wrap. Mobile 375px verified across projects, deals, project process, connected-apps and glossary: no horizontal page overflow anywhere; tables scroll inside their wrappers.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(web-next): **Self-service personal API tokens on the Connected apps screen, with the token-issuance security holes closed (design 2026-10-11-self-service-api-tokens).** Analysts had no way to obtain a personal bearer token for the local stdio MCP (issuance UI existed only in the admin console, where ADR-0146 forbids write-scoped issue), and the token API itself let any valid bearer mint or revoke tokens and never expired them. Backend: `POST/DELETE /api/tokens` are now session-only (any bearer form gets 403 `session_required`), tokens default to a 30-day expiry (7/30/90 selectable, 90 hard max) enforced at validation with a one-time 30-day grace stamp for legacy never-expiring rows, write scope is dropped at validation when the owner has been demoted to viewer/admin, issuance is capped at 5 active tokens per user and 10 issues per user per hour (409/429), `cnb_pat_` prefixes new tokens (legacy tokens keep validating), use/issue-denial/revocation are audited with no secret material, and error bodies carry fixed codes only. UI: the Connected apps screen gains a decision-tree guide (hosted connector → OAuth `<origin>/api/mcp`; local stdio MCP → personal token), an issue form (label, read vs read-write scope, expiry), a one-time reveal modal with copy button and the verified `mcp/co-newbiz/index.ts` config snippet, and a list with status chips, masked identifiers (`cnb_pat_••••<last6>`) and revoke-with-confirm; viewer/admin see a read-only list (stricter than the design's hide-only-write option, recorded in §14.1). Security second review closed all eight threat-model rows; accepted single-process limitations (cap TOCTOU, in-memory rate limit) are recorded in the design. No schema change, no ADR. 88 token tests pass; tsc clean.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Korean label sweep, shared mobile-table rule, corrected page shells and humane access states (design 2026-10-11-ui-label-and-mobile-table-sweep).** A read-only logged-in QA sweep (~150 URLs) found raw enum values, developer wording and English fragments across the Korean UI. `lib/labels.ts` becomes the single glossary-backed label map (tracks, sub-tracks, gates, stage statuses, decision modes, the S12 transition vocabulary, RAG dimensions, record keys, finding and glossary categories) with `resolveLabel` returning a fallback contract so a raw key appears only inside a collapsed developer disclosure; seven duplicated local maps were consolidated; every interim wording is marked `INTERIM-WORDING` with its source. Wired across the project, deal, charter, portfolio, committee, IC-review/DD, glossary (no more `BUSINESS` category or `Green Field · Green Field` duplication), strategy and company pages; `/agents` and `/our-company` lose the mismatched S2/S3 stage chrome and get proper h1/titles. All 57 table-scroll wrappers share one mobile rule (content-sized table scrolling inside the wrapper, nowrap chips, scrollable-region focus) replacing the per-table hacks, fixing the one-character-per-line squeeze at 375px. Non-admins hitting `/admin/*` now get an in-place Korean notice instead of a silent redirect to /deals; `/settings` redirects to connected apps; an inaccessible project shows a Korean not-found state with no stage chrome and no existence leak. 2,043 unit tests pass; design:lint and tsc clean.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Three defects found in a logged-in browser review: S8 explanation `NaN`, decision-inbox text, mobile target-table chips (design 2026-10-11-qa-sweep-fixes-s8-inbox).** (1) The S8 explanation tab rendered `NaN배 = NaN억원` in the year-0 investment row because the simulation note stores `acq_ev=3.00x` and the component read it with `Number()`; a client-safe `lib/sim-note.ts` (`parseSimNote`, `noteNumber`, strips the trailing `x`, null for missing/empty/`null`) now serves every numeric note read (acquisition and exit multiple, WACC, iterations, horizon, growth, sigma, fade-DCF terminal growth and NPV), and `crossCheckOfNote` uses the same parser. (2) The decision inbox showed stray punctuation from empty values (`보완 필요: .`, `..`) and English or raw values in the Korean review text (`? 도메인 · none`, English not-performed reasons): `cleanList` drops punctuation-only entries, a display-layer `tidyReasonText`/`localizeItem` handles rows already stored (stored data and content hashes unchanged), a Korean catalog covers every not-performed reason with a guard test that fails when `ai-second.ts` gains an uncatalogued reason, and `reviewOriginText` never prints `?` or `none`. (3) At 375 px the S8 target-comparison chips and the select-target button wrapped one character per line; they are now no-wrap with token-based minimum widths inside the existing horizontal scroll wrapper (a small scoped `.targets-table` rule overrides the generic mobile table rule). Observed, not changed: the S8 valuation uses the default 10 percent WACC until a person confirms a WACC session; the inbox link for a Screening HOLD opens S2 rather than S3 and whether an in-progress FS gate belongs in the inbox are pending a product decision.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(web-next): **Company financials WACC reference now reads the collected ECOS and DART data, the WACC input no longer shrinks D/E, and sync feedback is visible (design 2026-10-11-company-financials-wacc-reference-fix).** `GET /api/companies/[companyId]/financials` hardcoded risk-free, credit-spread and beta as `null` ("source to be connected") although ECOS observations already existed in the database, so the company screen showed N/A for values that were available. The route now reuses `computeWaccReference` through a pure mapper (`lib/company-wacc-reference.ts`): ECOS percent points are converted to ratios in exactly one place, each entry carries unit, dated basis (month or fiscal year), source label, note and reason, the credit spread uses the latest month in which both the treasury and corporate-bond series exist (basis month shown), observed D/E uses the latest fiscal year and is labelled as a book-value ratio, and beta stays an honest N/A (KRX data-use approval pending, no fabricated value). Review-found defect fixed: the WACC session's `Apply` action divided every suggestion by 100, so an observed D/E of 0.85 was entered as 0.0085; only the two ECOS rates are converted now (`lib/wacc-suggestion-convert.ts`, pinned by test) and the suggestion note reaches the panel. The DART sync button now shows a persistent `role="alert"` failure message with actionable guidance mapped by error code and message (`DART_HTML_BLOCKED` temporary block, `DART_RATE_LIMITED`, missing key, unresolved corp code) and an explicit status line when DART values are identical to the stored ones (the last-sync date only moves when a new revision is written). Proposal-only wording is unchanged: a person confirms values in the WACC session.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(s8): **Human-confirmed EV/Revenue band for the target comparison; the global 2–6x band is demoted to an unsourced reference (design 2026-10-11-s8-ev-band-user-confirmed).** The comparison judged every project against a global robotics/automation M&A band even for domains it was never sourced for (service, care and hospitality humanoid solutions). Without a confirmed band the table now shows `band not confirmed` with the global range as a labelled reference and no in/out verdict; a person can confirm a per-project band (min, max, mandatory rationale and source) through the new human-only `POST /api/projects/{id}/business-case/ev-band` (analyst+, stored as two `assumption` rows in one run — no schema change; agents and the generic params route are rejected; length caps; generic error messages). A confirmed band is shown beside the global reference with rationale, source, who and when; warnings (display only, never changing rank or the recommendation) cover an upper bound above 1.5x the global upper bound, a max/min spread above 4.5, a band older than 90 days (`re-confirmation needed`), mixed valuation bases, and the same person confirming the band and selecting the target. Cross-domain review by risk-officer and security-engineer applied. Deferred for human policy decisions (design doc): bearer-token bypass of the human-only list, admin role separation, reviewer approval for band confirmation, source-format validation, concurrent-overwrite warning.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(s8): **Fade-DCF cross-check and acquisition multiple surfaced in the target comparison (design 2026-10-11-s8-target-comparison-crosscheck).** The `대상 비교·선정` table ranked on base-scenario p05 only, while the fade-DCF cross-check and the acquisition multiple lived inside the simulation note text and never reached the comparison. `TargetComparisonRow` gains display-only `xcheckFadeNpv`, `xcheckFadeIrr`, `xcheckDivergence` (signed, fade NPV vs base p50), `structureMismatch` (starred row only, |divergence| > `XCHECK_DIVERGENCE_WARN_RATIO` = 0.5), `acqEvRevenue` and `acqEvRevenueInBand` (`ACQUISITION_EV_REVENUE_BAND` 2–6x, inclusive), read through the typed parser `crossCheckOfNote` over the existing note fragments (no migration, no ADR). Rank, sort and the p05-survival ★ recommendation are unchanged and pinned by tests; missing values are `N/A`, never 0. Cross-domain review (valuation-agent, risk-officer) amendments: the column is labelled `가정 EV/매출 배수 (진입=처분)` because the generator uses one multiple for entry and exit, the in-band chip is a neutral screening reference rather than a price verdict, a starred row without a cross-check shows an explicit `교차검증 없음` flag (silence is never approval), the absolute gap is shown beside the percentage, mixed valuation bases carry a not-comparable footnote, and a test pins the UI-mirrored constants to the generator. Known gaps left for follow-up: the 2–6x band is a global robotics M&A screening range, not calibrated to the service/care/hospitality humanoid solution domain (should come from an industry profile with source and verified_on); the 0.5 threshold is not yet calibrated on real runs; rendered 1280/375 px verification was not completed because the dev server needed a login.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(demo): **Dual-track demo — a full greenfield S1–S12 chain beside the brownfield one (design 2026-10-10-demo-dual-track).** `bun run demo` previously demonstrated only the brownfield M&A lifecycle end to end; the greenfield side was an E2E-minimum fixture whose process rail was broken (compact S1 payload the report UI cannot render, no gates, no establishment record). New `seedGreenfieldS1ToS12Demo` (scripts/co-newbiz/seed-s1-s12-demo.ts) seeds the track-appropriate twin: full `SizingMatrix` S1, greenfield route decision, S5 entry-strategy evidence, S6/S7 correctly skipped, **S8 through the REAL generator** (project-level market-capture anchor, idempotency-guarded so re-runs do not stack models), standard-only S9 DD, S10 structure/financing, the `설립 실행` 5-step record (`entity_establishment_step`, all done) instead of the brownfield PMI kickoff, six human gates (no `target_screening`), and the S12 scorecard + transition. `demo.ts` seeds both tracks, prints both deal/project URLs, and `--capture` now writes 19 screenshots (10 brownfield + 9 greenfield incl. S5). Two latent seed defects fixed on the way: all three seed fixtures wrote the adoption meta as `adopted_model_id` while the canonical reader/writer key is `model_id` (the seeded `채택됨` badge never resolved), and the greenfield `stake_type 'wholly_owned'` violated the enum CHECK and was silently swallowed by `INSERT OR IGNORE` (now `'full'`). Verified: `evaluateProcess` passes every stage on both tracks (S5 vs S6/S7 skips per spec), full unit suite, `demo -- --capture` renders real content (greenfield S8 shows live generator numbers), greenfield E2E 6/6.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(s8): **Valuation transparency — methodology explainer, explicit DB unit contract, and comparison-basis guard (design 2026-10-10-s8-valuation-transparency).** New `ValuationMethodology` section (`설명·생성·권고` tab) renders the 8-step computation chain with the live parameter values (anchor, growth per perspective, sigma, margins, acquisition price, WACC/exit, MC, fade cross-check) and explains WHY the two perspectives differ (same anchor/price/margins; growth 40% vs 14.9% compounded to a 2.2x final-revenue gap amplified ~4.3x by the fixed entry price). Migration 0134 makes the money-unit contract explicit: `business_case_line.unit` / `valuation_simulation.unit` (`KRW_M`, backfilled; system-wide SSOT documented in the migration), writers stamp it, `listBusinessCases` surfaces it, and the new unit-chain regression test pins the whole `원`(DART)→`억원`(analysis)→KRW-M(lines/sims/snapshot) path (exact /1e6 identity + NPV/anchor scale band — would have caught this session's real conversion bug). The target comparison gains a `평가 기준` column (`실적 앵커 (DART)` vs `시장 포획 폴백`) and a `methods_mixed` warning when bases are mixed, mirroring the D2 engine-provenance pattern. Boot transparency: `instrumentation.ts` prints the actually-bound database file (`PRAGMA database_list`) once per boot.
- **[2026-10-11]**: feat(s8): **Acquisition pricing from external benchmarks + fade-DCF dual-structure cross-check (design 2026-10-10-s8-acquisition-price-benchmark).** The year-0 outflow for company-anchored brownfield cases is no longer a greenfield setup ratio (50% of year-1 revenue — a `389억`-revenue target "bought" for `195억`, IRR 65%) but the acquisition enterprise value: anchor revenue x EV/Revenue multiple (default 3.0x — global robotics M&A band 2–6x, conservative screening basis; Samsung–Rainbow Robotics 2023 ≈`2,674억` on `152억` revenue and its 2026 ~`2조`/`341억` ≈59x market pricing recorded as the Korean strategic-premium counterpoint). The price is scenario-independent and user-overridable via `bc.acquisition_ev_revenue` (rationale mandatory); an explicit `bc.initial_investment_ratio` still wins as the direct knob. Because a 5-year window without terminal value would make market-priced entries systematically negative, the Python engine gains an `exit_ev_revenue` case driver (sell at the entry multiple on the final year's drawn revenue; 0 = legacy contract, byte-identical) mirrored exactly in the TS fallback (closed-form parity + noise-free engine↔TS equivalence tests). The horizon question is answered with a persisted cross-check: every acquisition-priced case also reports a deterministic fade-DCF (`bc.fade_horizon_years`, default 10; growth converging linearly to `bc.growth_terminal`, default 3%; same exit multiple) in the note (`xcheck_*` fragments) and a new scenario-matrix column — with fade disabled it reduces exactly to the primary structure (identity test), and unfaded 40% growth over 10/20y (5x/63x NPV inflation) is documented as the artifact that motivates the fade. Real-project numbers: `로보티즈` entry `1,168억` (`389억` x 3.0) → NPV p05 `1,701억` / p50 `2,394억`, IRR 40.0% (market) / 20.0% (own CAGR), fade-DCF `3,333억`; the two structures agree within +39–50% across all cases and the gap itself flags multiple-vs-growth consistency.
- **[2026-10-11]**: fix(s8): **Brownfield per-target valuation anchors on the target's actual DART revenue (design 2026-10-10-s8-brownfield-revenue-anchor).** S8 per-target cases inherited the greenfield "market-size x 0.5% capture" revenue model, and this project's opportunity had no market size (fallback `500억`), so every target was valued off a fictitious year-1 revenue of `2.5억` — a `몇백억`-revenue company (`로보티즈` FY2025 `389억`, Rainbow Robotics `341억` per DART) came out at NPV p50 `3.7억`, and the ADR-0117 `과거 실적이 지속되면` perspective was broken at the level anchor. `GrowthModel.companyRevenue` extracts the latest annual revenue from the same DART analysis the historical CAGR already used (`억원`-normalized x100 back to KRW millions), `CaseInputs.revenueAnchor` carries it, and one `year1RevenueOf` accessor feeds the simulator, deterministic flows, job-spec `revenue_y1` (provenance-labeled), the persisted lines and the prompt prose; greenfield/project-level paths gain no anchor (legacy numbers and seeds byte-identical), and targets without company facts keep the labeled capture-rate fallback. Notes stamp `revenue_anchor=…` instead of `capture_rate=…`, and executor prose no longer mislabels KRW-millions as `억원` (100x display error in run logs). Verified on the live project: `로보티즈` base/market revenue `389.4억` → NPV p50 `592억` before acquisition pricing landed on top.
- **[2026-10-10]**: fix(s8): **Brownfield business-case valuation now consumes per-target WACC sessions (ADR-0156 amendment 2, design 2026-10-10-s8-per-target-wacc-consumption).** The S8 `밸류에이션 재계산 (재생성)` action ignored ADR-0156 per-target WACC sessions: `caseInputsBaseForProject` resolved the project-level WACC once and `caseInputsForTarget` spread it to every shortlisted target, so all targets were discounted at one cost of capital and the comparison/recommendation stayed skewed. `resolveTargetCaseWacc` now applies the same scope rule the WACC page displays: the bound target (`md_project.company_id`) keeps the project-level gate scope (even when its own session exists), a non-bound target with its own session is discounted at `{ subjectCompanyId }`, and targets without a session (or an unresolvable company) fall back to the project scope — generation never blocks on a missing manual confirmation. The scope is provenance: the simulation note carries `wacc_scope=project|target`, the job-spec snapshot (`inputs_json`) gains `discount_rate.scope` plus `subject_company_id` (REQ-W5-1 reproducibility), the valuation prose and the report `산출 근거` line label target-scope sessions, and the seed policy is unchanged. Greenfield, `wacc_subject` (REQ-W4) and every gate-path resolution (fs gate, kill criteria, snapshot filling, S10/S11) stay project-level per ADR-0156 decision 2; the axis matches ADR-0157 per-target DD. Tests: `business-case-wacc-scope` (session consumed, fallback, bound-target gate scope, gate isolation).
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **S8 units, default analysis target, S9 subject header and DD table widths (design 2026-10-09-ui-units-targets-dd).** S8 NPV p05/p50/p95 and p50-difference columns now state `(백만 원)` (values are stored in KRW millions) and IRR says `(%)`, via `KRW_MILLIONS_UNIT`/`formatKrwMillionsPlain` in `lib/format.ts`. The WACC and financial-analysis selector drops the `프로젝트 기준` chip on multi-target brownfield projects: the default is the S8-selected target (else the first shortlisted), keeps the `게이트 기준` marker and maps to the project-level API scope so ADR-0156 gate semantics are unchanged (`lib/target-default.ts`, unit-tested); chips are the shared `FilterChip`. S9 shows `실사 대상: <name>` with an `S8 선정 대상 · 게이트 기준` or `비교 근거` chip, or the no-selection and greenfield state as text. All S9 DD tables share one fixed column grid so the commercial table no longer squeezes judgement and waiver columns.
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **Legacy S8 model cleanup — delete unreferenced, archive referenced (ADR-0158, design 2026-10-09-legacy-model-cleanup).** The R2-E legacy notice counted pre-ADR-0143 models (`target_id` NULL) that the target-filtered S8 list never shows, so a user trying to clear it deleted six visible per-target models instead. The notice now lists each legacy model (name, scenario, variant, created date, referenced flag) with per-model and bulk `예전 모델 정리` behind a confirm dialog. Migration 0133 adds nullable `business_case_model.archived_at/archived_by`; the human-only, analyst+ `POST /api/projects/{id}/business-case/legacy-cleanup` (agents rejected, registered in `HUMAN_ONLY_ROUTES`) deletes unreferenced legacy models through the existing path (audit `business_case.delete`) and ARCHIVES models an adoption (`s8_case_adopt`) or gate record references (audit `business_case.archive`) so the decision audit trail stays intact. Archived models drop out of the notice count, S8 list, comparison and RAG/completion predicates but still resolve in the adoption, IC package and report views with a `보관됨` label. The ordinary delete button and confirm now name the model's target (`대상: X`).
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(dd): **Optional per-target DD on shortlisted brownfield targets (ADR-0157, design 2026-10-09-optional-per-target-dd, flow.spec v2.6.0).** The selected S8 target stays the only subject of the S9 predicate and the `dd` gate; a person can now open DD on another shortlisted target as comparison evidence. Migration 0132 (additive) adds a nullable `target_id` to `project_checklist_item_state` (rebuilt with an asserted-equal row copy; the inline UNIQUE becomes a per-target unique index), `project_checklist_item_custom`, `dd_finding` and `waiver`, plus `dd_target_open` and a `gate_decision_stale` marker table; NULL means the selected or legacy target, no backfill, so existing projects behave exactly as before. New `POST /api/projects/{id}/dd/targets` (human web session only — bearer, MCP and agent identities are refused; rationale required; shortlisted non-selected target only; idempotent; audited) and `POST /api/projects/{id}/dd/findings`; the DD routes (workspace GET with a new `targets[]` summary, checklist state, tailor, waivers) accept an optional `target_id`, and a target whose DD was never opened answers 409 `DD가 열리지 않은 대상입니다`. `ddCompletionSummary`, the S9 predicate, gate brief, IC package, S10 recommendation, RAG snapshot and `findActiveWaiver` read the gate scope only; a waiver applies to exactly one scope. Re-selecting A to B runs in the same transaction: no row is deleted, A's DD is stamped to A and auto-opened, B's opened DD becomes the gate scope, and any existing `dd` gate decision is marked stale (history keeps it; re-judgement required). DdStage reuses `TargetSelector` (selected target first with a `선정 대상` badge), adds a `DD 열기` dialog with a required rationale, a `비교 근거 — 게이트 판정에 반영되지 않습니다` banner, a `비교 근거` comparison table without any PASS stamp, and names the target the gate judges. MCP: `get_project_dd_workspace` takes `target_id`; `tailor_dd_checklist` and `request_dd_waiver` document the body passthrough; no tool opens DD — `propose_approval` gains the suggestion-only subject `dd_target_open`. flow.spec v2.6.0 is wording-only (S9 objective/outputs), mirrored in lib/process-flow.ts and the generated projections. Tests: `migration-0132` and `dd-target-axis` unit suites plus an e2e `brownfield-ma` walk-through with axe on the selector, dialog and comparison table; openapi.yaml, mcp-coverage exclusions and the in-process human-only route list updated.
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(wacc): **Per-target WACC input sessions and financial analysis on shortlist-scoped brownfield projects (ADR-0156, design 2026-10-09-per-target-wacc-financials).** Migration 0131 adds a nullable `run.subject_company_id` (+ `idx_run_project_company`); NULL keeps today's project-level sessions byte-identical, so the fs-gate figure, snapshot filling and S10/S11 resolve exactly as before (`resolveProjectWacc`/`loadProjectComponents` now filter `IS NULL` at project scope and accept `{ subjectCompanyId }` for a target's own session). `POST /wacc` accepts an optional `target_id` (must be a shortlisted target with a resolvable company, otherwise 400); `GET /wacc`, `GET /wacc/suggestions` and `GET /financial-analysis` accept `?target_id=`; new `GET /api/projects/[id]/targets` feeds the shared `TargetSelector` chip group, shown on the WACC and financial-analysis pages only when two or more targets are shortlisted (per-target mode hides the project-level subject decision and labels the session as non-gate). Tests: `wacc-target-session` unit (grandfathering, isolation, route validation, suggestions and financial scoping) and an e2e `brownfield-ma` selector walk-through. Design registered as implemented; script versions bumped (newbiz 1.20.0, source-adapters 1.2.0, kosis-units 1.0.2).
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(wacc): **KRX beta-series collection and the WACC collected-data loader (beta-series task b-d).** Seeded KRX day-grain series descriptors (`krx:index:KOSPI:close`, `krx:index:KOSDAQ:close`, `_verified: false` — per-service params need the the k-krx development specification) plus a per-company daily-close helper (`krxStockSeries`, seriesId convention `krx:stock:<md_company.id>:close` — documented deviation, md_company has no ISIN/short-code column) in scripts/co-newbiz/source-adapters.ts, with a day-grain `listDailyRows` adapter hook (one API call per series×month) wired through the `newbiz fetch` ensure_fresh path. New `scripts/co-newbiz/beta-series.ts` (`--project <id>`, `--from/--to`, optional `--ticker 이름=코드` pin) registers the index series plus each shortlisted/subject company close series, resolves `단축코드` (short code) via the stock-master services with a `db/data/krx-stock-code-cache.json` cache, and collects KOSPI plus the two shortlisted companies (`로보티즈`, `레인보우로보틱스`) — one honest cycle ran against the production DB: **0 observations**, every KRX service returns `respCode 401 Unauthorized API Call` (key valid, services not approved/expired — re-apply via the portal My Page API-usage application screen; KRX cannot serve history until then). GET `/api/projects/[id]/wacc/beta-series` returns aligned date/price/index rows; BetaPanel gains a `수집 데이터 불러오기` action that fills the existing paste box (unchanged) and warns `시계열 회귀에는 최소 약 3개월(거래일 63개) 데이터가 필요합니다 — 현재 N건` below 63 rows. Runbook rb-beta-series (en/ko, registry + library projection) documents the manual collection cadence (no host scheduler locally) and the KRX re-apply prerequisite. UI follows Onyx 3.0 tokens; no `.claude/**` or `AGENTS.md` staged; no `as any`.
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-F: S8 per-target regeneration and AI terminology (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design section 8.7).** The per-target regeneration button (the legacy-notice primary, `대상별 재생성`) was a silent no-op: POST `/api/projects/{id}/business-case/generate` returned the existing non-failed `s8_business_case_generation` run with `alreadyRun: true` instead of generating. The route now accepts `{ regenerate: true }` which bypasses the already-run guard and starts a NEW run (still analyst+, still proposals-only, boundary #2); the legacy-notice primary and the recalculation ghost action send it, and the ordinary generate path keeps the guard but surfaces an explicit already-run message pointing at regeneration instead of doing nothing. New E2E seeds a legacy NULL-target model plus a done run and asserts per-target (`market_forward`, `target_id` set) models appear. All user-facing AI labels are unified: `AI 추천` for suggestion/recommendation/draft labels (was `LLM 추천/제안`, `AI 제안`) and a plain `AI` qualifier for neutral ones (`AI 추정`, `AI 브리프`, `AI 요약`, `AI 모니터링`, `AI 신뢰도`); `LLM 미구성` now reads `AI 미설정`. Display mapping only — stored source enums, API fields, logs and developer disclosures are unchanged, and zero `LLM` remains in components/app .tsx user-facing strings (titles and aria-labels included).
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-E: six partials closed and the legacy business-case notice (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design).** (1) The project gate-decisions POST now server-enforces the IC-gap contract: an `entity_ic`/`group_ic` GO over gaps re-derived at decision time without a non-empty `gap_rationale` is refused `422 ic_gap_rationale_required` naming the gaps; `gate_decision` proposal payloads (lib/proposals.ts, MCP `propose_gate_decision`) carry an optional `gap_rationale` through confirm, so both paths agree; pre-existing rows are grandfathered. (2) POST waivers accepts `dd_item {checklist_id, item_id}` for per-row prefill — the route derives `clause_ref` and a default reason, `WaiverRequestForm` sends the item identity directly (ddMode), documented in openapi.yaml. (3) S8 keeps one tab row: the stage row (summary, scenario comparison, per-target selection, WACC, financial analysis; More holds overview, parameters, target costing, decision, risk heatmap) is authoritative, the in-page TabNav renders only embedded, old `?tab=` deep links redirect, IA decision recorded in design §8.3. (4) `/projects/archives` gains a closure-type column fed by the shared `closureTypeLabel`/`CLOSURE_TYPE_LABEL` (lib/labels.ts) — ClosureModal now sources its names from the same map so list and modal cannot drift. (5) The S1 industry pin button gets an `aria-label` naming the industry with `aria-pressed`; S2 has no star control (its toggles are filters) — the S1 name was the actual defect. (6) The sixth verification-report partial is covered by 1 to 5. Item 1: `countLegacyUnassignedModels` (lib/business-case-legacy.ts, unit-tested) drives a labelled S8 warning notice for ADR-0143-preceding NULL-target business-case models with per-target regeneration as the primary action; legacy models are never migrated or deleted. New/updated tests: `business-case-legacy`, `ic-gap-rationale` (422 path), `stage-tabs`, mcp-parity, portfolio/wave-c3, e2e `brownfield-ma` (legacy notice) and `mcp-proposal`.
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-D: verification and residual localization (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design, now `implemented`).** Hands-on re-run of all three UX walkthroughs against the merged R2-A/B/C build: 45 Round 1 findings — 39 fixed, 6 partial, 0 open; axe 0 serious/critical on 63 routes in light AND dark; every acceptance task completes UI-only (new market analysis 6 to 1 clicks, S12 KILL 3-6 to 2, shared gate confirm adds one deliberate click to HOLD). Residual jargon fixed in the UI layer: S11 confidence cap renders `높음/중간/낮음` instead of the raw `green/amber/red` enum (`IcReviewStage`), parameterized closure precondition record keys fall back to their base Korean label and the English "The latest S12 transition decision must be ..." precondition reads Korean (`lib/labels.ts`, `ClosureModal`), and the unconfigured-LLM not-performed reason in the 3-stage review is localized (`ReviewBuckets`). Walkthrough reports carry `lang: ko` frontmatter and pass the markdown language validator; screenshot evidence curated to 31 before/after pairs (7.7 MB) under `docs/evidence/gui-review/walkthrough-2026-10-09/curated/`. Verification report: `docs/reports/ux/2026-10-09-walkthrough-round2-verification.md`.
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-C: system quality (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design, items 1 to 10).** Dark-mode contrast is fixed in the tokens (new `--primary-fg`, `--primary-strong`, lighter dark `--primary`/`--primary-dim`; `contrast:check` enforces the new pairs) and `tests/scenarios/axe-sweep.test.ts` runs axe on 70 route instances in light and dark, 0 serious/critical (before: light 3 nodes, dark 136 nodes), plus one h1 and a distinct document title on every route, and no horizontal overflow at 390 px. New themed Korean `not-found.tsx` and `error.tsx` with exits back; per-route titles through server layouts (`AdminGuard` split out of the admin layout). `design:lint` rejects a class-less `<button>` (four fixed). Loading skeletons are `role="status"`; source tooltips, the inbox failure list and risk-heatmap notes no longer fail axe. Offline or synthesized S1/S2 numbers carry `합성 추정 (검증 안 됨)` and degenerate values (CAGR -100%, $0) read `산정 불가` (`lib/synthetic-value.ts`, additive `synthetic` on market-overview insights). `lib/labels.ts` is the glossary-backed label map; jargon (`gate_decision(kill)`, `missing_artifacts`, `LLM provider is not configured`, `origin∈{...}`, `RECOMMENDED`, bare `FS`, `Hurdle IRR`, `Run`, English DD tabs, inbox `딜`) is replaced and ADR references moved into a `개발자 참고` disclosure. Viewers see disabled actions with `보기 권한만 있습니다`; non-admins opening `/admin/*` get a notice. Fix: viewer writes to `deal-structure`, `charter` and `charter/confirm` returned 400 and now return 403, pinned by `tests/unit/viewer-write-guard.test.ts` (33 write routes). `/proposals/{bogus}/confirm` shows `제안을 찾을 수 없습니다` and `/oauth/authorize` without valid parameters shows an error instead of consent.
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-B: decision surfaces (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design, items 1 to 7).** One gate-recording flow on every gate panel (S8, S9, both S11 gates and the earlier gates): confirm dialog, toast, the form collapses into a recorded summary with no resubmission, and a next-stage link. The decision is reachable from the top (the stage primary scrolls to and focuses the form; S8 has a "decision" tab instead of repeating the panel under every tab) and the 3-stage review shows once per gate (S11 no longer duplicates it). An IC GO with missing artifacts (no adopted business case, RAG not graded, establishment pending) is never blocked: a warning dialog lists each gap and requires a typed rationale, which the route stores with the decision (existing rationale column, no migration; gaps re-derived server-side) and the IC package shows. S8 keeps WACC and financial analysis in the stage row only. S9 gets an inline waiver dialog per row (prefilled, with disabled reasons and a visible non-waivable explanation for bf-01 to bf-03), Korean M&A checklist text from a display catalog, a "pending judgement" default for items without a judgement while every existing judgement is shown as stored (grandfathered, who and when), row filters, a non-overlapping waiver form and a card layout below 640 px. S10 has one primary "record structure hypothesis", Korean stake and payment-method labels and "payment method mix" instead of the mistranslated label. S11 establishment steps have an owner column and a "complete with evidence" action (human only), the PMI form collapses after recording, and the charter is linked from S11 with the right tagline. New `tests/unit/ic-gap-rationale.test.ts`, `tests/unit/dd-grandfather.test.ts` and E2E `tests/scenarios/gate-flow.test.ts` (confirm, toast, summary, no resubmit; IC GO with gaps; S9 inline waiver; axe).
- **[2026-10-09]**: feat(web-next): **UX round 2, batch R2-A: blockers and wayfinding (spec 2026-10-09-ux-r2-design, items 1 to 8).** The board's "new review (market analysis first)" now opens the new-industry form directly (`/market?new=1`, focused): a new market analysis takes 3 clicks instead of 6 through an unrelated deal. The picker pages `/market`, `/opportunities`, `/synergy` and `/route-decision` have an h1, a breadcrumb, a create action, an active sidebar item and a contextual back. The stage rail resolves S8 to S12 to the promoted project id (it used the opportunity id and showed "Not found."); for an unpromoted deal those stages are disabled with a visible reason. S3 no longer refers to a non-existent "company register" button: it links to the group and affiliate profile surface and explains why "start analysis" is disabled. Each S2 row advances its own opportunity ("evaluate" or "go to S3"). New shared `Breadcrumb` and `DealBreadcrumb` on the deal, stage and opportunity pages; the opportunity detail goes back to its S2 list; the S1 report gets the stage rail, a back to the industry list and a "next: S2" action. Known-target entry lands on a focused form at the top and the guide steps are visible links. Orphan routes: `/pipeline` and `/process/[stage]` redirect with a visible notice, automatic single-deal redirects are announced, and the board lists entry points for `/projects`, `/portfolio-view`, `/our-company`, `/companies`, `/agents` and `/onboarding`. Per-page document titles through server layouts. New E2E `tests/scenarios/wayfinding.test.ts` (12 tests, includes axe).
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **UI critique leftovers.** Ghost buttons with an icon (deal board search) now lay out icon and text on one line. The review "developer details" disclosure has a chevron, hover state and focus ring and stays collapsed by default. The deal board card no longer shows "S11 HOLD" for a deal held at a different gate: the stage chip and a separate gate chip (for example "FS HOLD") are built by the new `lib/deal-card-label.ts` (`buildCardChips`, unit-tested). Evidence: `docs/evidence/gui-review/critique-2026-10-09/followup-*.png`.
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **Website critique fixes (spec 2026-10-09-ui-critique-fixes-design, items 1 to 10).** The empty "Missing:" list in review reasons is fixed at the source: a HOLD whose missing list held blank entries no longer overwrites the stage's required artifacts, and a dangling "needs supplement" label now reads "needs supplement: confirm evidence" (new `lib/review/review-display.ts`, unit-tested). One shared `FilterChip` (selected = filled with a check mark, `aria-pressed`) replaces the all-filled inbox chips, which gain an explicit "All" chip, and the deal board chips; the deal-board primary action regains its button shape. The inbox detail shows the three counts beside the single primary link with the buckets expanded below, on the full content width. AI 1st reasons, gate display names and bucket titles have a Korean catalog (titles stay exactly "Verified / Could not verify / Needs judgement" in their Korean wording, English only as a tooltip) and file paths move into a collapsed developer-details disclosure. Stage screens show a non-dismissible review summary strip that expands to the buckets; the could-not-verify count uses the warning color, an icon and the words "needs check" whenever it is above zero, while gate panels keep the buckets expanded. Buckets are responsive (3 columns at 1100px container width, else stacked, one indent). Artifact tab labels use Korean display names. The sidebar badge counts open decision items and the bell counts unread notifications, each with an explanatory tooltip. The sidebar collapse icon no longer shrinks to 2px, the stage stepper is a single scrollable row with a separate gate marker, the deal board no longer overflows at 1280px and the inbox deal filter no longer overflows at 390px. Screenshots in `docs/evidence/gui-review/critique-2026-10-09/`.
- **[2026-10-09]**: chore(governance): register graft's tool-owned surfaces in docs/self-managed-surfaces.json (copied from the workspace registry) so local audits skip graft-generated skill mirrors and helpers.
- **[2026-10-09]**: fix(web-next): **S1 market report no longer crashes on an incomplete matrix.** New `isCompleteSizingMatrix` guard in `lib/market-sizing.ts` is applied at the `MarketReport` entry; an incomplete stored matrix renders an "incomplete matrix — rerun required" notice with the existing rerun action as primary instead of throwing `matrix.regions.reduce`. `seed-s1-s12-demo.ts` (1.7.0) now seeds a structurally complete, demo-labelled `SizingMatrix` (2 regions, 2 segments, `competitive.structure`/`players` shape matching the S1 predicate and the D-6 route). New `tests/unit/sizing-matrix-guard.test.ts`.
- **[2026-10-08]**: refactor(scoring): **Wave D pure core-scoring package (spec 2026-10-08-wave-d-core-scoring-package-design, implemented).** New private `packages/core-scoring/` (`private: true`, never published) holds the pure scoring core with typed exports, a README and its own tests: `evaluateRag` and the RAG rules, the data-confidence ceiling (new `applyConfidenceCeiling` / `confidenceCeilingOf`, extracted from `assessRun`), data-confidence grading (`gradeDataConfidence`), proxy-chain grading, the ADR-0046 predicate AST, kill-criteria registry validation and snapshot coercion, and unit / proxy-chain conversion math (`convertBetweenUnits`, `applyProxyChain`). No DB, fs or env access in the package. `db/lib/rag-engine.ts`, `data-confidence.ts`, `kill-criteria.ts`, `chain-grading.ts`, `predicate.ts` and `scripts/co-newbiz/convert.ts` (1.1.0) are now thin adapters that call and re-export it, so no import path changes. Relative imports, no Bun workspace; `tsconfig.core.json` covers `packages/**` at 0 errors. Property-style tests (seeded and exhaustive) assert a grade never exceeds the confidence cap and an incomplete assessment is never graded. No behavior change: existing unit, E2E and offline eval results are identical.
- **[2026-10-08]**: fix(web-next): **Wave C-3 pilot-009 defects (D-2 to D-10, discrepancy 4.1).** Gate decision routes (opportunity and project, and so the proposal-confirm path) now refuse GO with 409 `gate_prerequisites_incomplete` until the gate's stages are complete (screening S1-S3; pre_fs S4 plus S5/S6; target_screening brownfield track plus S7; fs S8; dd S9; entity_ic/group_ic S10); HOLD and KILL stay allowed and existing rows are untouched. Offline or failed-LLM sizing is stamped `synthesized` and graded C (F-5 rule), and the S1 report shows the stored grade. `industry_id` is accepted on opportunity create and update (validated against `md_industry`) with a select in the S2 form and funnel rows. New `PUT /api/market-overview/analyses/{id}/competitive` and a competitive-tab form record the concentration summary and major players where the S1 predicate reads them. "Start from a known target" (`known_target` on `POST /api/opportunities`) pre-fills S6 and guides S1-S4 without skipping any predicate (no migration). Offline synthesis uses industry-agnostic segments. S1/S3 primary-action copy and the review-panel not-found state are fixed, and `/opportunities` renders the S2 funnel when no deal exists. `docs/pilots/pilot-009-system-rerun.md` gains "Re-run after C-3": S1 now completes and the run stops at the S2 attractiveness judgement, which is a legitimate human input.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(demo): **Wave C-2 internal demo and the pilot-009 UI re-run (spec 2026-10-08-wave-c-e2e-proof-design, now implemented).** New `bun run demo` (`scripts/co-newbiz/demo.ts`) boots the production build on a free port against a temp DB with the S1-S12 demo deals and offline fallbacks; `--capture` writes the S1-S12 screenshot sequence in `docs/evidence/demo/`. README gains an "Internal demo" section with an architecture diagram (web, API, DB, MCP stdio and HTTP, OAuth, review engine, RAG, eval). `docs/pilots/pilot-009-system-rerun.md` records the Emart/eBay Korea re-run through the UI with public facts only: it correctly halts at S2 (attractiveness is a human judgement the public record lacks), and it lists ten product defects (four High). Fixed here: "start a new review" looped between the board and an empty `/market` (S1 now renders inline when no deal exists), and the E2E harness offline mode still allowed web research (Tavily/ECOS/KRX keys now blanked, DuckDuckGo fallback off; temp dir removed on signal).
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(e2e): **Wave C-1 Playwright golden paths and axe gate (spec 2026-10-08-wave-c-e2e-proof-design).** `bun run test:e2e` now runs five UI-driven scenarios in `tests/scenarios/` (greenfield to `graduated_to_core`, brownfield M&A including the 25-item sub-track checklists to `divested`, KILL at a gate to `killed_at_gate`, mid-process `withdrawn` with `pipeline_run` never `completed`, and a real MCP stdio proposal confirmed on `/proposals/{id}/confirm`). Each scenario builds once into `.next/e2e` (`CO_NEWBIZ_DIST_DIR`), starts its own server on a free port against a temp DB, blanks the LLM/DART env, and tears down. axe-core 4.14.0 (pinned devDependency of `app/web-next`) gates the shell, stage page, gate panels, closure modal, inbox, confirm page and connected apps at zero serious/critical violations. Product gaps found by the run and fixed: the gate panel could not record a KILL (no learning-capture field vs the ADR-0152 422), the S12 form could not record four of five transitions (no ADR-0151 record column field), no document `<title>`, a menu button inside `role="tablist"`, accessible names missing visible text, and sub-4.5:1 accent/green/amber/sidebar-label contrast. New `e2e` job in `quality.yml`; the `scenarios` suite is sequential. `seed-s1-s12-demo.ts` gained `humanDecisions` and `subTrackDd` options (defaults unchanged).
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(eval): **Wave B-3 RAG calibration and committee promotion rule (spec 2026-10-08-wave-b-ai-eval-design, now implemented).** New `docs/methodology/rag-calibration.md` (v1.0.0): grade-to-probability mapping (Green 0.8 / Amber 0.5 / Red 0.2), Brier score and per-grade reliability table with fixed-seed bootstrap intervals, computed with and without the data-confidence cap, plus a sourcing plan for engine-ready pilot fixtures and public proxy-chain cases. New offline `calibration` suite in `bun run eval` (`scripts/eval/calibration.ts`; engine and pilot-record tracks; no pass threshold). Only pilot 010 is calibratable from pre-decision inputs (reference Brier 0.040, n = 1): insufficient for weight changes, and results never retune weights. Golden set 1.1.0: new `known_disagreement` field; the pilot-008 Screening kill expectation is marked with it because `preliminary_hhi_flags_certain_block` fires on the rebuttable 2023 presumption zone rather than a certain block (`docs/audits/2026-10-08-pilot-008-hhi-presumption-vs-certain-block.md` recommends a human ADR; rule unchanged). The `kill` threshold rises to 1.0. `DEC-20261008-01` records the committee LLM promotion thresholds: not promoted, because there is no priced model and no LLM baseline yet.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(eval): **Wave B-2 golden set and eval harness (spec 2026-10-08-wave-b-ai-eval-design).** `tests/eval/golden/` holds 14 pilot cases with cited expectations and a leakage-linted fixture per case. New `bun run eval` (`scripts/eval/`): deterministic suites `rag`, `kill` and `antitrust` run offline and in unit CI against recorded thresholds; expectations the fixtures cannot feed to an engine are listed as could-not-evaluate, never guessed. LLM suites (`review-ai2`, `stage-reco`, `committee`, `dd-tailor`, `business-case`) run only with `--llm` plus `CO_NEWBIZ_EVAL_LLM=1`, stop at `--max-cost-usd` (default 2.00) using B-1 usage, and use deterministic scoring with no LLM judge. Scoreboards (markdown and JSON, with model id, provider, prompt hashes, tokens, cost) go to `docs/reports/eval/`. `tests/eval/call-sites.yaml` plus an inventory check fail when an LLM call site has no suite or exemption. Migration of the hand-parsed JSON call sites to `completeStructured` is deferred with reasons in the inventory, because their suites have no LLM baseline yet.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(llm): **Wave B-1 LLM hardening and observability (ADR-0155; spec 2026-10-08-wave-b-ai-eval-design).** New `completeStructured` in `lib/llm.ts`: provider-native JSON schema output (Anthropic forced tool, OpenAI `response_format`, Gemini `responseJsonSchema`) when `CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_CAPS` declares `structured`, otherwise instruct-and-parse; every result is Zod-validated, gets one repair retry that names only failing paths, then an explicit synthesis fallback (`reason: validation_failed`). New `stream()` (SSE for all three shapes) and `cache_control` prompt caching, both gated by `CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_CAPS` (`stream`, `cache`), never by model name. Provider-reported token usage (input, output, cache read, cache write) is parsed for anthropic, openai, gemini and custom shapes; chars/4 remains only as a flagged `usage_estimated` fallback. `complete()` is unchanged; `completeDetailed()` returns the usage too. Gemini requests now use the native `contents`/`systemInstruction` shape. The review AI 2nd now uses `completeStructured` and records real provider tokens as its cost (the other JSON-parsing call sites keep their text contracts for now). `llm_call_log` gains tokens, `cost_usd`, `usage_estimated`, `project_id`, `function_name`, `prompt_hash` (migration 0130, additive); `config/llm-pricing.yaml` holds per-million prices and an unknown model records a null cost; `GET /api/projects/[id]/agent-runs` adds an `llm_usage` per-deal rollup. OpenTelemetry GenAI `gen_ai.chat` spans go out over OTLP/HTTP JSON only when `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is set (no SDK dependency, no prompt or completion content). Tests use mocked fetch only.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(ui): **Wave U-3 component decomposition, primary actions and usability re-run (spec 2026-10-08-wave-u-ux-overhaul-design, now implemented).** The seven files over 1,000 lines (`MarketAnalysisStage` 2,871, `OurCompanyStage`, `BrownfieldStage`, `SynergyStage`, `RouteDecisionStage`, `AdminConsole`, `brownfield/targets/[targetId]/page.tsx`) are split into hooks and panels of at most about 300 lines each under `components/stages/{market,our-company,brownfield,synergy,route-decision}/`, `components/admin/console/` and `components/brownfield-target/`; the old stage and admin paths stay as re-export shims and rendered output is unchanged (verified screen by screen against the parent build). Every screen in the design's primary-action table now has exactly one filled primary action in the page header (new `PrimaryAction`/`PrimarySlot` in `ui.tsx`, slot in `StageFrame` and beside the stage tabs) with the reason shown as text beside it when disabled; the S4 "propose route" and S6/S7 pre-check actions are included. More parameter and fact tables use `ParamTable` (S4 facts, WACC current values, simulation conditions, target costing), which stacks into cards at 390px; wide stage tables no longer scroll the page at 390px. Opening a deal from the board lands on its current stage and the WACC panel opens in CAPM mode. Usability re-run: median task clicks -33% and time -40% (per-task median rule), SUS proxy 40 to 67.5; pooled clicks on the three previously solvable tasks -27% (missed the -30% target, T4 is path-floor bound). `design-tokens.md` body and caption sizes now match the 14/13px floor.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(ui): **Wave U-2 readability and primary actions (spec 2026-10-08-wave-u-ux-overhaul-design).** 14px type floor: every sub-14px `font-size` in `styles.css` and Tailwind `text-[..rem]` class moved to the new `--fs-body` (14px) / `--fs-caption` (13px, secondary metadata only) tokens; spacing scale `--space-1..8` added; table cells forced to body size. New shared `ParamTable` (`ui.tsx`) puts label, value (right-aligned, tabular numerals), unit and source in separate columns with the source as a chip; the S8 parameter tab uses it (`60.0%기본값` becomes `60.0` / `%` / `기본값`) and the driver snapshot value column is right-aligned. One filled primary action per stage screen: row/section-level `btn-primary btn-sm` buttons demoted to ghost, the target filter is now `control-chip` with `aria-pressed`, S8 Generate, S3 confirm, S2 Register and S12 Record stay primary. `design:lint` rule 6 rejects font-size below 14px (CSS and Tailwind arbitrary values). No behavior or API changes.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(ui): **Wave U-1 navigation IA and inbox (spec 2026-10-08-wave-u-ux-overhaul-design).** Navigation cut to two layers: sidebar (Work: Inbox/Deals/Archive; Reference; Settings incl. Connected apps) and a keyboard-navigable stage stepper (`aria-current="step"`, compact at 390px). ~30 stage sub-links become per-stage tabs plus a More menu (`StageTabs` replaces `ProjectSubNav`); legacy "stage screens" label and `/brownfield-mockup` removed. New read-only `/inbox` aggregating gate/decision proposals, HOLDs, S12 blockers and notifications, each linking to the existing human-only control. ClosureModal reachable from S12. Dev indicator no longer overlaps the sidebar (layout test + `ux-layout-check.ts`). Usability baseline recorded: pending-decision and proposal-confirm tasks go from impossible to 2 and 5 clicks.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(mcp): **Wave M-3 stage-guide prompts, glossary/flow-spec resources and the REST/MCP parity test (ADR-0154; Wave M complete).** Both transports now advertise prompts `stage_guide_s1`..`stage_guide_s12` (objective, activities, procedure keys, required artifacts, gates, and the tools that serve each; they point to `get_procedure` and never restate procedure steps) and resources `glossary://index`, `glossary://term/{key}`, `flowspec://index`, `flowspec://stage/{id}`. The guides come from a generated projection (`mcp/co-newbiz/stage-guides.generated.ts`, emitted by `generate-spec-activities.ts` v1.1.0, `--check` drift-gated). New `tests/unit/mcp-parity.test.ts` drives one Green Field and one Brown Field deal to S12 and a typed closure through direct REST calls and through an in-memory MCP client (propose_* plus the human's confirm route), asserting equal `evaluateProcess` results at every step, human deciders only, no close tool, and no decision without the human confirm. Test hardening: `brownfield-llm-support.test.ts` no longer makes a live LLM call (the repo-root `.env` provider was injected and the call could outlast the 20s timeout under full-suite load); assertions unchanged. Wave M design marked implemented.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(mcp): **Wave M-2 full-stage tools and proposal-only decisions (ADR-0154).** The registry now serves one 102-tool set on stdio and `/api/mcp`: every flow.spec S1-S12 activity and requiredArtifact has a scoped tool (`read`/`write`/`propose`), each a thin wrapper over one REST route (`RestPort` gains `put`/`patch`). Decisions are proposals only: new `decision_proposal` table (migration 0129) and `propose_gate_decision`, `propose_route_decision`, `propose_case_adoption`, `propose_target_selection`, `propose_transition_decision`, `propose_approval` (charter baseline, DD checklist judgment, four-axis completion); a human confirms on the new `/proposals/{id}/confirm` page (single-use nonce, show-diff, 72h expiry, CSRF, session cookie only: tokens and agent identities cannot confirm), and that session runs the existing decision route so the decider of record is the human. MCP elicitation only points to the page. Closure and PMI kickoff have no tool; the in-process port refuses every human-only route. **Breaking:** `record_gate_decision` and `create_route_decision` no longer write decisions on either transport (the stdio exception is gone); they are deprecated aliases that create a proposal and return the confirm URL with a `deprecation` notice, removed next release. New `scripts/co-newbiz/mcp-coverage.ts` + `mcp-coverage.exclusions.yaml`, run by the variant audit hook `scripts/co-newbiz/audit-variant.ts`. New tests: coverage, row-count guards, confirm-as-human, expired/foreign/bearer/agent rejections, per-tool scope enforcement, elicitation, import-graph guard.
- **[2026-10-08]**: feat(mcp): **Wave M-1 remote MCP transport and OAuth 2.1 (ADR-0154).** Tool registry moved to `mcp/co-newbiz/registry.ts` behind a `RestPort` (stdio keeps the same 19 tools). New Streamable HTTP endpoint `/api/mcp` (Origin checks, in-process route calls as the user) and an in-app OAuth 2.1 server: PKCE S256, dynamic client registration, RFC 8414/9728 metadata, RFC 8707 audience binding, RFC 7009 revocation, 15-minute access and rotating 7-day refresh tokens with family revocation, scopes read/write/propose, no client_credentials, no agent accounts. Migration 0128; consent page and "Connected apps" revocation page; audit rows per token event and tool call (no token values). The two legacy decision-writing tools stay stdio-only until M-2 makes them proposal-only. 27 new tests. Also: `company-profile-status` test made hermetic (DuckDuckGo off in tests).

AI 1st is deterministic; AI 2nd is a different-domain LLM reviewer with Zod-validated output, a content-hash cache and a monthly token cap (`REVIEW_MONTHLY_TOKEN_CAP`, default 200k), recorded as "not performed" instead of silently skipped. - **[2026-10-02]**: feat(governance): **ADR-0151 accepted and implemented — dedicated transition-record columns; spec-activities generator; board pipeline-status 404 fix (spec 2026-10-02-followup-batch-design).** (1) ADR-0151 (Accepted 2026-10-02, human reviewer in chat): Option A — three nullable columns `tranche_condition` / `core_handoff` / `divest_plan` on `venture_transition_decision` via reversible additive migration `db/migrations/0121_venture_transition_record_columns.sql` (the design's "0080" number was already taken; erratum recorded in the ADR); Option B (single JSON record) rejected — audit-ledger records must be queryable and DB-enforceable; NO backfill — the transition-decision route requires the dedicated column for new decisions (400 with a named message when missing), while the S12 completion predicate grandfathers rows with `decided_at < 2026-10-02` (rationale-only suffices); SQLite cannot express transition-conditional CHECKs on nullable columns, so enforcement lives in the route + predicate (kill `learning_capture` rule unchanged).
