(2) New `scripts/co-newbiz/generate-spec-activities.ts` (@version 1.0.0, SCRIPTS.md registered) replaces the hand-maintained `spec-activities.generated.ts` workflow: deterministic regeneration from `process/flow.spec.yaml` with a `--check` drift gate wired through the unit test suite, mirroring `generate-process-flow.ts`; the regenerated body was byte-identical (no drift found). (3) `GET /api/opportunities/{id}/pipeline/status` now returns 200 with a default payload (`status: "none"`) for an existing opportunity with zero pipeline runs (was 404 — the cause of the board's console 404 noise); unknown ids keep their 404; `PipelineStatus` and the board handler treat "none" as a no-pipeline state. Rendered check at 1280px and 390px: /deals board loads with zero console errors for a viewer-role user.
- **[2026-10-02]**: feat(governance): **ADR-0149 and ADR-0150 accepted and implemented (spec 2026-10-02-adr-0149-0150-implementation-design).** (1) ADR-0149 (Accepted 2026-10-02, human reviewer in chat, Option A): the gate ladder in `process/stages.yaml` is renamed S1-S8 → G1-G8, ending the id collision with the S1-S12 business stages in `process/flow.spec.yaml`, which are unchanged and now the only meaning of S-ids. G3's `owner_agent` is `pm` (was TODO). `process/stage-aliases.yaml` alias targets follow the G ids (alias keys unchanged); `procedures/_validate.ts` now validates the gate axis against the declared stages.yaml id set fail-closed in both directions (no prefix hard-coding) instead of the `^S[0-9]+$` pattern; new unit tests cover the G registry and a no-collision invariant (no id in both `stages.yaml` and `flow.spec.yaml`). The DEG export (`scripts/co-newbiz/graph.ts`) maps G1-G8 → legacy `stage.co-newbiz.S<n>` wire ids at the emission points, so external DEG consumers (ADR-0083) keep their ids. (2) ADR-0150 (Accepted 2026-10-02): S12 gains six lifecycle completion criteria mapped to the five portfolio transitions; recorded decisions: IMMEDIATE full application to all in-flight projects (policy-pin query found 0 live pins, so nothing breaks) and criterion-6 data ownership (group-review-agent records captured learning, risk-officer feeds S1/S2 base rates). `flow.spec.yaml` S12 completionCriteria/requiredArtifacts updated; `portfolio-venture-monitoring` procedure 1.0.0 → 1.1.0 (no DB change needed — transition rationale is schema-required and `learning_capture` is route-required for kills); the web S12 completion predicate (ADR-0101 registry) now requires a fresh review cycle AND a human transition decision with rationale plus the transition-specific record, with a recorded limitation: criteria 3-5 have no dedicated columns, so the required rationale is treated as the record (a future migration can add columns). `seed-s1-s12-demo.ts` seeds a transition decision so the demo satisfies the new criteria. Docs regenerated (process-flow, graph, generated addendum); AGENTS.md/README/matrix verified accurate.
- **[2026-10-02]**: refactor(web-next): **PR B of the route cleanup — the five flat routes are now carrier-resolving redirect pages (spec 2026-10-02-route-cleanup-followup-design).** New shared `components/CarrierGate.tsx` implements the §4.2 rule: a query carrier (`?opportunity=` / `?deal=`) forwards straight to `/deals/{id}/sN` with other params preserved; otherwise the most recent in-progress opportunity is auto-resolved (track-mapped for /greenfield and /brownfield via the track APIs, any track for /market, /opportunities and /synergy via the selected+promoted opportunities API); exactly one carrier redirects, zero or multiple render a `/route-decision`-style picker with the first row keyboard-focused. `/market` `/opportunities` `/synergy` `/greenfield` `/brownfield` are thin CarrierGate wrappers now. Inbound links per §4.3: `StageFrame` S1-S7 links go carrier-form when a deal id is in context; `pauseLinkFor` emits carrier-form links when the run's carrier id is known; cross-links in RouteDecisionStage, OpportunityFunnelStage and the opportunity detail page are carrier-form. Deviations (recorded in the design's audit trail): sidebar and no-carrier CTAs keep the flat hrefs (they now resolve or pick); AppShell unchanged (routes still exist as stubs); the OurCompanyStage `?company=` deep link was dropped (no deal-carrier equivalent — company selection now happens inside the S3 tab); auto-resolution uses the API's created_at ordering as the recency proxy (no endpoint returns updated_at). `/route-decision` and `/opportunities/[id]` unchanged (§4.4). Rendered check (1280px + 390px): query-carrier direct redirect, single-carrier auto-redirect, multi-carrier picker with focused first row, picker click landing on the s1 tab, zero console errors on the flat routes; `/route-decision` unchanged.
- **[2026-10-02]**: refactor(web-next): **PR A of the route cleanup — dead stage-link maps removed (spec 2026-10-02-route-cleanup-followup-design, PR B flat-route redirects follow).** The `STAGE_LINK` map in `/deals/[id]/[stage]` was unreachable dead code (its only consumer, StageGuidanceTab, fires only for invalid stage ids) and is removed; StageGuidanceTab now renders stage status and blockers only. On the deal overview, the stage-work-screen link (previously built from the `STAGE_ROUTES` map with stale flat and `/projects/[id]/*` targets) is retargeted to the deal-carrier form `/deals/{id}/s{currentStage}`, which serves all of S1-S12. Design doc `docs/designs/2026-10-02-route-cleanup-followup-design.md` (approved): PR A dead-code removal, PR B unifies the five flat routes (/market, /opportunities, /synergy, /greenfield, /brownfield) behind deal-carrier redirects with a carrier-resolution rule; `/route-decision` and `/opportunities/[id]` unchanged. Verified: full unit suite, design:lint, validate:language pass; tsc unchanged from baseline (33 pre-existing errors, none under app/web-next/app).

- **[2026-10-01]**: feat(web-next): **ADR-0148 accepted and implemented — S9 completion counts unassessed standard checklist items (option A).** Until now `ddCompletionSummary` counted only existing state rows, so a project with at least one judged row could finish S9 while mandatory standard items were never assessed. It now also returns `unassessed` (standard items of the three DD checklists with no state row), and the S9 completion predicate blocks while `unassessed`, `pending` or `mandatoryUnmet` is non-zero. Applies only to projects created on or after 2026-10-01 (`DD_UNASSESSED_COUNTED_FROM`, keyed on `md_project.created_at`); earlier projects keep row-only counting and are not blocked retroactively. `pending` keeps its meaning, so the pipeline's `checklist_approval:S9` pause reason is unchanged. - **[2026-10-01]**: docs(glossary): **Register the `Revenue Quality` and `DSO` glossary terms.** `glossary/financial.yaml` v1.2.0 adds the two terms behind the cdd-08 four-axis assessment (owners `commercial-dd-agent` and `financial-analyst`); `glossary-data.generated.ts` refreshed by hand (no in-repo generator). `glossary:lint` passes.
- **[2026-10-01]**: test(web-next): **Drift guard for `library.generated.ts`, and consistency fixes in the revenue-quality change set.** New `tests/unit/library-projection-sync.test.ts` compares every projected checklist (version, item count, items) and every projected document's content with its source file; the repo has no generator for the projection, so a forgotten refresh used to be silent. The guard found 16 entries already stale before this work (two of them, `chk-brownfield-feasibility-checklist` and `chk-greenfield-feasibility-checklist`, also with outdated items and version), so those are listed as known-stale and not refreshed here — new drift fails at once, and a listed entry that becomes fresh fails until it is removed from the list. Consistency fixes in the cdd-08 / ADR-0147 set: `ma-08` and `fdd-05` no longer widen mandatory items through the now-recommended `cdd-08` (recurrence and customer-level supply apply only where `cdd-08` is performed); `mdd-06` asks for a benchmark only where one exists; `mdd-06` / `mdd-07` compute the figure themselves, and say so, when `cdd-01` / `cdd-02` was waived or not completed; the skill's revenue-quality output is conditional on `cdd-08` being performed.

- **[2026-10-01]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0147 accepted — revenue-quality four-axis assessment (cdd-08) and a single computation owner for customer concentration and churn; `brownfield-dd` wording aligned (v1.6.1).** Status ACCEPTED 2026-10-01 by the human reviewer: the ADR records the decision behind the cdd-08 checklist item, the `mdd-06`/`mdd-07` re-scoping, the optional `revenue_quality_norms` / `payment_practice` profile sections and the RAG non-change (ADR-0016 untouched), with OP-1~OP-3 resolved by the reviewer on 2026-10-01 (applies only to DD entered after this change; the project team fills the profile values while running cdd-08; cdd-08 is recommended, not mandatory); OP-4 (a row-less mandatory item does not block a gate) stays open for separate review. `procedures/_shared/brownfield-dd/schema.yaml` 1.6.1 changes step 1 and step 4 descriptions only — no step, agent key, skill key, output type or kill criterion changed. Impact analysis: the only derived copy of the step text is `library.generated.ts` (refreshed); `project_policy_pin` pins `policies/` only, so no in-flight project is re-pinned.
- **[2026-10-01]**: feat(governance): **Profile benchmarks for revenue quality, and one computation owner for customer concentration and churn.** (1) Industry profiles gain an optional `revenue_quality_norms` section (benchmark ranges for the four cdd-08 axes) and region profiles an optional `payment_practice` section (collection-period norms), schema_version 1.1. Both validators (`industry-profiles/_validate.ts`, `region-profiles/_validate.ts` v1.1.0) skip an absent section and hold a present one to the usual provenance rules (source, verified_on, maintainer, non-empty data); no existing profile was changed and no section was populated, because a sourced, human-confirmed value cannot be invented — until one exists `cdd-08` falls back to `peer-benchmarking`. New test `tests/unit/profile-optional-sections.test.ts` runs the real validators against scratch profiles. (2) `mdd-06` / `mdd-07` (market-dd-checklist v1.1.0) no longer recompute concentration and churn from the same data room as `cdd-01` / `cdd-02`: commercial DD is the single computation owner, market DD takes the figures as given, adds customer-voice evidence, switching costs, leading churn indicators and the industry/peer norm, and raises any gap as a finding. Item IDs are unchanged because `project_checklist_item_state` is keyed by item id. `commercial-due-diligence` skill text and platform mirrors updated. Not changed: `procedures/_shared/brownfield-dd/schema.yaml` step 4 still lists "customer concentration, churn" in its description — editing a procedure needs an ADR, so that wording is left for a follow-up.

- **[2026-10-01]**: feat(governance): **Commercial DD gains a four-axis revenue-quality assessment (cdd-08).** `checklists/commercial-dd-checklist.yaml` v1.1.0 adds recommended (`mandatory: false`, preferably completed; a skipped item records why) `cdd-08`: for each top customer and segment, concentration, margin, collection period and recurrence are read side by side on the same unit, with a verdict and evidence per axis — a floor of four axes, never collapsed into one score, and without restating `cdd-01`~`03`. `commercial-due-diligence` skill v1.2.0 adds the matching step and the typical `assumption_key` mapping; axis verdicts feed the Financial RAG evidence and the Data Confidence ceiling and are not averaged (ADR-0016). Cross-references: `fdd-05` and `quality-of-earnings` v1.2.0 (supply customer-level margin, DSO/aging and the recurring-revenue split), `dd-to-valuation-bridge` v1.2.0 (the margin axis must not double-count a QoE-normalized shortfall), `ma-08` (checklist v1.3.0) and `customer-attrition-risk` v1.2.0 (measure concentration and recurrence both pre- and post-close). `financial-dd-checklist` is now v1.1.0. Platform skill mirrors and `app/web-next/lib/library.generated.ts` refreshed. Industry and region profile fields for recurring share, typical DSO and payment practice are deferred (profile schema change). In-flight projects: check `project_policy_pin` before they pick up the new checklist version.

- **[2026-10-01]**: fix(web-next): **ADR-0146 PR4 follow-up — close the write-control holes found by live viewer-role verification.** The browser check with a viewer account found controls PR4's rollout missed. `OurCompanyStage` now disables the member-role select (fires an immediate PATCH on change) for non-analysts — the one control that produced a real 403 during verification. `SynergyStage`'s list component had no auth at all: it now gates the new-analysis button and form, list-row rerun/delete and result-header rerun behind `canCurate`, gates the capability-gap block's write buttons, and wraps its editable table in a disabled fieldset so stored values stay visible. `MarketAnalysisStage`'s list component gets the same treatment (pin toggle, row rerun/edit/delete, new-analysis button and form, link-modal run buttons, in-report rerun, linked-industry add), and `CalibrationPanel` gains its own gating for the reference form, escalation and accept/reject actions; read-only display is untouched in both. The static test `tests/unit/business-write-gating-static.test.ts` is extended to v1.1.0 (the `updateMemberRole` site must carry `!canAct` within 5 lines; each list component must contain at least 6 `canCurate` occurrences). UI only: server authorization is unchanged. Verified by audit, unit suite, tsc and design:lint; the fix itself was not re-checked in a browser.
- **[2026-10-01]**: chore(web-next): **Post-ADR-0146 cleanup — dead code removed, empty-state hints match the viewer's role.** Removed the unused `isAdminDealBlocked` from `lib/project-visibility.ts` and six never-called helpers plus four state hooks from `BrownfieldStage.tsx` (rendered behavior unchanged); the empty-state hints on the opportunity and company detail pages now show analysts the existing text and non-analysts a neutral read-only hint that does not mention hidden buttons. No authorization change. Verified by audit, unit suite, tsc and design:lint; not verified in a browser.
- **[2026-09-30]**: feat(web-next): **ADR-0146 PR4 — opportunity-carrier screens hide write controls for non-analysts; completes the ADR-0146 rollout.** New UI helpers `canWriteBusiness(role)` (analyst only) and `canReadBusiness(role)` (not admin, not null) in `app/web-next/lib/project-write-access.ts`. Greenfield, Brownfield, opportunity funnel, route decision and our-company stages, the company detail page, the brownfield target detail page and `opportunities/[id]` now hide write controls for viewers and admins (some form inputs are disabled via a disabled fieldset so stored values stay visible); CommitteePanel, SynergyStage, MarketAnalysisStage and the opportunity branch of GateDecisionPanel switched from the admin-or-analyst predicate to `canWriteBusiness`. The brownfield target page no longer fires its company-sync request on load for non-analysts. UI only: server authorization is unchanged (PR2 and PR3 already enforce it). New static test `tests/unit/business-write-gating-static.test.ts`. Not verified in a browser for any role.
- **[2026-09-30]**: feat(web-next): **ADR-0146 PR3 — admin gets 403 on business reads; admin Business Targets screen moved to new admin routes.** About 60 GET handlers in opportunities, brownfield, greenfield, companies, market-overview (except ksic and value-chain-graph), synergy and our-company now call `requireBusinessActor(ctx, "read")`: admin gets 403 `role_forbidden`, viewer and analyst unchanged. Former `isAdminDealBlocked` users (archive/deals, projects GET) move from `role_required` to `role_forbidden`. New admin-only routes `GET /api/admin/business-targets/groups` and `/groups/{id}` back the admin Business Targets screen. Data-changes and library stay open to admin. Docs: api-spec, mcp-integration, openapi. Authorization change; human security review required.
- **[2026-09-30]**: feat(web-next): **ADR-0146 PR2 — admin role no longer writes business content; central `requireBusinessActor` guard.** ADR-0146 is now Accepted. A new helper `requireBusinessActor(auth, mode, opts?)` in `app/web-next/lib/auth.ts` replaces duplicated inline viewer checks in about 72 write routes (opportunities, brownfield, market-overview, our-company, companies, synergy, greenfield, agents/query POST): write mode rejects viewer (403 `role_required`, per-route message kept) and admin (403 `role_forbidden`); analyst is unchanged. Behavior changes: comments POST now rejects both admin and viewer (viewers could previously post opportunity comments), tokens POST no longer lets admin issue write-scoped API tokens (403 `role_forbidden`; read-only tokens unchanged), and our-company/groups POST answers a viewer with 403 instead of 400. MCP write tools now need an analyst token. Reads (GET) are unchanged; PR3 (reads, admin-side routes, docs) and PR4 (UI) follow. Authorization change: human security review required.
- **[2026-09-30]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0146 proposed — admin role is system administration only, no business-content access.** Status PROPOSED pending human review and acceptance: the admin role would stop reading or writing business content (opportunities, brownfield, market-overview, our-company, companies, synergy, greenfield, agent queries, comments) outside the admin console, via a central helper next to `requireAdmin`, admin-side business-targets read routes, no write-scoped API tokens for admins, and analyst tokens for MCP write tools. Documentation only; no authorization code changed, and implementation follows in three later PRs after acceptance.
- **[2026-09-30]**: fix(web-next): **D4 PR-A — write controls on 8 project pages and 2 panels follow `canWriteProject` (analyst only).** Registration and action controls on the incentives, permits, sites, interviews, customer-coc, debt, share and proxy-chain project pages, `FinancialAnalysisPanel` and `WaccPanel` (gated inside the panel, covers both mounts) are now hidden, not disabled, unless `canWriteProject(me?.role)` holds; read-only display, analyst behavior and API authorization are unchanged, and controls stay hidden while auth is loading. New static test `tests/unit/project-write-gating-static.test.ts`. Not verified in a browser per role. Remaining: D4 PR-B/C (Greenfield, Brownfield, OurCompany stages and detail pages) wait on ADR-0146 (draft) on whether opportunity-family write APIs stop accepting admin; the proxy-chain empty-state hint still mentions calibration.
- **[2026-09-30]**: fix(web-next): **D4 bundle 1 — project write controls follow `canWriteProject` (analyst only).** Replaced the admin-or-analyst write-control predicate with `canWriteProject(me?.role)` (`app/web-next/lib/project-write-access.ts`) in 10 files that call only `/api/projects/[id]/...` routes: the DD, Deal Structure, IC Review and Portfolio stages, `WaiverPanel`, the charter, PMI, partnership and reports pages, and the project carrier of `GateDecisionPanel` (its opportunity carrier keeps admin-or-analyst because the opportunity API accepts admin today). Admins and viewers no longer see controls the API would reject. No read-only admin notice was added on these screens. API authorization is unchanged. Not yet covered: D4 bundle 2 (ungated screens), bundle 3 (other write surfaces) and bundle 4 (project close/delete lead check); `SynergyStage`, `MarketAnalysisStage`, `CommitteePanel`, `PromotionPanel` and opportunity pages are intentionally untouched. Visual check pending (login required).
- **[2026-09-30]**: feat(web-next): **Deferred-review remediations D2/D4 — calculation-engine provenance and admin read-only view.** (D2) `TargetComparisonRow` gains `engine` (`python-sim-engine` | `ts-fallback` | `mixed` | null), derived from the `engine=` note of the base-scenario simulation rows; `listBusinessCases` returns `enginesMixed`; `GET /api/projects/[id]/business-case` now passes it through as `engines_mixed` (previously dropped); `BusinessCaseStage` shows a per-row engine badge (engine column) and one footnote when engines differ. Ranking and recommendation logic are unchanged (`flagRecommendedTarget` stays non-mutating); runs whose notes lack `engine=` yield null. (D4) New `lib/project-write-access.ts` (`canWriteProject` / `isReadOnlyAdmin`; only analyst may write); `BusinessCaseStage` `canAct` now uses it and admins see a read-only notice instead of write controls. API authorization is unchanged. Tests: `business-case-targets.test.ts` extended, new `project-write-access.test.ts`. Follow-up: the D4 sweep of other project-surface stages is still open (grep `role === "admin"` and `canAct` in `components/`).
- **[2026-09-30]**: fix(web-next): **Deferred-review remediations D3/D5/D6/D8.** (D3) `business-case-generator.ts` hoists project-level inputs once per multi-target run via a new `caseInputsBaseForProject` — WACC, `bcParams`, `waccSubject`, `targetCost`, and the redundant growth-model resolve (including the DART company-facts path) are no longer re-resolved per subject; `caseInputsForTarget` gained an optional base param (2-arg callers unchanged). (D5) `business-case.ts` exports `NotFoundError`; `selectTarget`/`adoptBusinessCase` throw it and the `target-selection` / `business-case/adopt` route handlers map `instanceof NotFoundError` → `notFound()` instead of `msg.startsWith()` sniffing — HTTP responses byte-identical, new test covers the 404 vs 400 mapping. (D6) activity id prefix changed from target company name to `target.id` (ULID); zero-subject greenfield id format unchanged; UI verified display-only. (D8) `ci.yml` regains a live `test` job ("Unit + Script Tests", bun + web-next deps + python venv) — clobbered by the 2026-09-23 template resync (second occurrence); `VERSION_MANIFEST.md` regenerated. D2/D4/D7 remain open (design/product decisions needed).
- **[2026-09-30]**: docs(context): **Fill scaffold placeholders and add research References.** Filled the placeholders in `docs/context.md` and `docs/project.md` (clears the audit "Live context placeholder check" WARN) and added a `## References` section to `docs/research/energy-power-profiles-2026-09.md` and `docs/research/machinery-defense-digital-profiles-2026-09.md`, consolidating citations already present in the bodies (clears the "Research files missing ## References" WARN). `docs/context.md` is pipeline-maintained, so a later template sync may overwrite the placeholder edits.
- **[2026-09-30]**: fix(scripts): **Project review 2026-09-30 validator remediations.** `validate-templates.ts` 1.50.2 returns early when `templates/common` is absent (project context, not the L0 workspace); `agent-lifecycle-audit.ts` 1.4.0 discovers every `agents/*.md` and accepts a role or a description; `verify-scripts.ts` 1.10.0 requires exactly 8 columns per `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` registry row (new `tests/unit/script-registry-audit.test.ts`), and the `regenerate-agents-md.ts` row is corrected (layer L0+L1). Baseline recorded in `docs/reports/2026-09-30-project-review-baseline.md`.
- **[2026-09-30]**: chore(agents): **Agent frontmatter refresh across 41 `agents/*.md`.** Added `tier.gemini-cli` / `tier.antigravity` to 39 agents, refreshed `last_updated`, added the missing role to `pm.md`, and regenerated `docs/VERSION_MANIFEST.md`.

- **[2026-09-29]**: chore(upgrade): **template upgrade to v0.8.1 — Claude 5.5 models.** Registry High/Medium tiers move to `claude-opus-5-5` / `claude-sonnet-5-5`; AGENTS.md `tier-model-mapping` blocks refreshed (now delivered by the fixed upstream managed-block merge); agent tier lines carry the tier word only (stale `# <model-id>` comments removed — the registry resolves models).
- **[2026-09-28]**: chore(upgrade): **template upgrade — `Hermes.md` renamed to `HERMES.md` (upstream ADR-0093 Amendment 2).** Hermes Agent discovers its instruction file only as `.hermes.md`/`HERMES.md`, so the mixed-case file was skipped on case-sensitive Linux/Docker hosts. Also delivers the current template scripts, skills, and `.gitignore` (generated `.cursorrules`/`.clauderules` now ignored).

- **[2026-09-27]**: chore(upgrade): **Template sync v0.7.0 + COMMON-HERMES activation (PRs #418–#420; design `2026-09-27-template-upgrade-070`).** Three-landing upgrade round via `upgrade-project` (ADR-0073): (1) v0.7.0 catalog sync — 385 files across the five platform skill mirrors (`.hermes` ×155, `.codex` ×49, `.claude` ×46, `.gemini` ×45, `.agents` ×45), three governance docs, `design-foundation`/`meeting-facilitation` skill refreshes, and the validator set (`validate-templates`, `validate-docs-links`, `verify-platform-lifecycle`, `verify-readme-sync`, `verify-scripts`); (2) post-v0.7.0 tail — `audit.ts`, `upgrade-versions.ts`, `upgrade-policy.ts`, `validate-md-language.ts`, skill-graph/workspace-schema registries, `SCRIPTS.md`; (3) `Hermes.md` delivery (120 lines) after the L0 managed-block-merge 1.3.0 fix (workspace PR #1129) added the COMMON-HERMES marker pattern — the workspace decision is ADR-0093, this repo carries the delivered artifact. Design doc backfilled 2026-09-28 (the round predated this record).

- **[2026-09-25]**: docs(agents): add `## §6: Skills` as a pointer to the project registry (`skills/SKILLS.md`, 105+ skills) and align `## Governance` → `## §7: Governance`. Pre-variant-promotion alignment: co-newbiz is slated for `project-to-variant` promotion, where full §1–§7 skeleton alignment happens; the transitional §7 numbering lets the skill-table integrity check run (it requires a §6→§7 span).

- **[2026-09-23]**: docs(adr): **ADR-0144 (promotion naming rule) + ADR-0145 (sync/engine mutual exclusion) + index backfill.** ADR-0144 codifies the fix from #413 as a decision: the promoted project display name is the opportunity name as-is, the route `sub_track` stays a classification field (`route_decision.sub_track` / charter `subTrack`), and the previously-undocumented title-suffix behavior is removed and regression-tested — it regulates ADR-0104 §2's implementation without amending its decision, per the index's new-ADR-not-edit rule. ADR-0145 records the one-writer-per-working-tree principle after the 2026-09-23 incident where concurrent upgrade-engine resets silently replaced a sync commit's task content with engine artifacts (PR #411; re-landed as #413): no engine overlap around `/sync`, `SYNC_SCOPED_STAGING=1` for dirty-tree syncs, `pre-upgrade-snapshot` stashes (incl. the `^3` untracked parent) as the sanctioned recovery channel, and content-verification of pushed commits. `docs/adr/README.md` gains rows for 0143 (was missing from the index), 0144, and 0145.
- **[2026-09-23]**: fix(web-next): **Promoted project names no longer carry the sub-track suffix (spec-exempt E3).** `buildProjectCharter` appended ` — <sub_track>` (e.g. `— existing_business_rbd`, `— strategic_investment`) to the project display name at S7→S8 promotion, baking machine classification into the human-facing title. The name is now the opportunity name as-is; the deal shape stays recorded where it belongs (`route_decision.sub_track`, `charter.subTrack`). Regression test added to `tests/unit/project-promotion.test.ts`; the two affected live rows (PRJ-QGB2W5, PRJ-DSE4PW) were repaired in the dev database.
- **[2026-09-23]**: fix(web-next): **Center the login form inside its panel (spec-exempt E3).** The legacy `.login-form { width: 320px }` rule forced the form 10px wider than `.login-panel`'s content box (384px − 2×36px padding − 1px borders), and the block overflow all went right: the inputs, button, and heading sat 37px from the panel's left edge but only 27px from its right (user-reported "asymmetric margins" on the login screen; present in every browser, just subtle at 10px). Removing the fixed width lets the form follow the panel content box — measured insets are now 37px/37px in both Chromium and WebKit renders.
- **[2026-09-23]**: fix(ops): **ghcr image retention + restore `deploy-web.ts` (design `2026-09-23-docker-image-retention-deploy-restore`).** Two defects surfaced while diagnosing rapid host-disk growth after the Docker cutover. (1) `.github/workflows/docker-cleanup.yml` adds weekly (`workflow_dispatch` too) retention for the `co-newbiz-web` container package via `actions/delete-package-versions@v5` — keeps the newest 10 versions (each main push = one version carrying `latest` + `sha-<commit>`); the deployed `latest` is always the newest version, so the window always covers it. (2) `scripts/deploy-web.ts` was missing at HEAD — deleted as an unintended casualty of the 2026-09-21 fleet-resync wave (`b0af650f`) while the deployment runbook §8, the compose header, and the `2026-09-21-docker-prod-cicd` design (REQ-P4) all still instruct the operator to run it; restored byte-identical from `b0af650f^` (pull → up → health gate, `--build` local path) and re-verified against the current compose contract (service `web`, port 7748, `/api/health`). Host-local, outside the repo: the BuildKit cache was pruned and the Docker Desktop GC budget lowered 20GB→10GB in `~/.docker/daemon.json` (note: Docker Desktop rewrites that file on quit — edit it while Docker is stopped).
- **[2026-09-22]**: fix(web-next): **Allow the public domain in dev `allowedDevOrigins` (spec-exempt E3).** The Cloudflare tunnel serves the public site (`co-newbiz.bizknights.org`) from the dev server (7749) by operator choice, but Next.js dev blocks cross-origin dev resources (HMR websocket, static chunks) for hosts not in `allowedDevOrigins` — server-rendered HTML returned 200 while the browser got 403 on every chunk, leaving pages stuck on the loading shell. Added the domain beside the Tailscale and LAN IP entries; the dev server must be restarted for the config to apply.
- **[2026-09-22]**: feat(gate): **Agent persona integrity gate in `validate-agents.ts` 1.3.0 (design `2026-09-22-agent-persona-integrity-gate`).** Regression gate for the roster-artifact class repaired in `2026-09-22-agent-roster-generation-artifact-repair`: fails on `extends:` chains that do not resolve to an existing base file, on the known spilled-frontmatter body shapes (`  governance: …` / `examples: []`), and on self-contained definitions missing a frontmatter `description:` (resolving ADR-0033 extends-stubs downgrade to a warning — they inherit the description from their base). Raw-content checks run in both L0-root and L1 project contexts; the L0 copy (`ai_workspace/scripts/validate-agents.ts`) is kept byte-identical for the script-drift check. Negative fixtures confirmed all three checks fire; the gate now also detects the sibling-project defect found in the fleet survey (9 × `pm.md` carrying the template-relative `extends: ../../common/agents/pm.md` that dangles at the deployed location).
- **[2026-09-22]**: fix(agents): **Repair the 2026-09-19 roster generation artifacts across 39 `agents/*.md` definitions (design `2026-09-22-agent-roster-generation-artifact-repair`).** The roster wave corrupted all but `pm.md`/`i18n-specialist.md` three ways: a dangling `extends: ../../../agents/<name>.md` on 38 co-newbiz-specific agents (no L0 base exists — and the field made `validate-agents.ts` skip their schema validation entirely), spilled frontmatter fragments (`  governance: …` + `examples: []`) in the body tail that the persona generator inlined into every harness dispatch, and a lost `description:` field that made the generator silently skip regeneration. The repair removes the dangling extends, strips the body fragments, restores each description from the last-good generated `.claude/agents/*.md`, and regenerates the personas. `pm.md`/`docs-writer.md` keep their valid ADR-0033 L0 chains; no roster membership, semantics, or governance-record changes. Post-repair: `agent:verify` 41/41, `validate-agents` 82 files/0 errors (the 38 previously-skipped schemas now actually validate), full unit suite green.
- **[2026-09-22]**: feat(s8): **Brownfield per-target feasibility — target axis on one project carrier (ADR-0143, design `2026-09-22-s8-multi-target-feasibility`, flow.spec v2.5.0).** Brownfield S8 now produces one feasibility case set per shortlisted target (3 scenarios × N targets) instead of one per project: `business_case_model.target_id` (migration 0120), generation loops the ADR-0098 chain over the shortlist with seeds keyed `(projectId, targetId, scenario)` (engine `case_id` carries the subject so two targets never draw the same stream), the S8 completion predicate becomes track-aware (every shortlisted target analyzed + human selection recorded), and the fs gate consumes a two-step adoption — `POST /api/projects/{id}/target-selection` records the human selection (finalizes `md_project.company_id`; rank-1 binding at promotion is provisional) and constrains case adoption to the selected target. Spec gains `subjectScope` + `target-comparison`/`target-selection` activities; new `brownfield-fs-comparative` procedure; S9 narrows to selected-target DD; UI gains a target comparison/selection tab with per-target scenario filter. Greenfield paths byte-identical.
- **[2026-09-21]**: chore(web-next): **Untrack the generated `next-env.d.ts` (spec-exempt E3).** Next writes different contents per mode (dev points route types at `.next/dev/types`, build at `.next/types`), so the tracked file flip-flopped with every dev run or build and got swept into unrelated syncs (PR #398 carried it). Now gitignored and untracked — Next recreates the file on any dev/build run, and the container build never relied on the tracked copy.
- **[2026-09-21]**: fix(web-next): **Allow LAN host 192.168.0.44 in dev `allowedDevOrigins` (spec-exempt E3).** Browsing the dev server (7749) from another device via the workstation's LAN IP blocked the HMR/dev-resource cross-origin requests (`Blocked cross-origin request to /_next/webpack-hmr from "192.168.0.44"`) — only the Tailscale IP was allow-listed. Added the LAN IP beside it; the dev server must be restarted for the config to apply.
- **[2026-09-21]**: fix(skills): **Heal skill-graph drift and re-enforce the unit suite in CI (spec-exempt E3).** `tests/unit/skill-graph.test.ts` failed 2 cases on clean main: (1) four procedure→skill edges whose targets never declared the procedure — recent `procedures/_shared/` additions (`deal-shape-chain`, `jv-lifecycle-governance`, `portfolio-venture-monitoring` → project-review; `entity-establishment-execution` → k-law) shipped without updating the skills' `metadata.procedure_keys` self-description; (2) project-review's `metadata.related_skills` still listed `project-resync`, a skill that does not exist. Both skill frontmatters aligned (project-review 1.3.1, k-law 1.0.3) and the full unit suite is green again (122/122 files). First enforced CI runs then exposed a systemic environment gap: the scratch-SQLite tests' `beforeAll` (fresh-DB migration of 100+ migrations) exceeded bun's default 5s per-test/hook timeout on slower CI runners (adr-0104, then brownfield-eval-criteria — each passes locally in <1s). Fixed in `test-runner.ts` v1.6.0 rather than per-test: `bun test` now runs with `--timeout=30000` (the per-suite process timeout is unchanged). The CI unit-test steps recorded added on 2026-09-20 were found lost — `ci.yml` was back at the commented template stubs (likely reset by the scaffold-hardening wave) — so a real `test` job is configured: root deps + python venv (`python/requirements.txt`, needed by the venv-first analysis spawns) + web-next deps, running `test:unit` + `test:scripts` on every PR and main push.
- **[2026-09-21]**: chore(web-next): **Patch-bump framer-motion ^13.4.0 and lucide-react ^1.47.0 (spec-exempt E3).** Range-only dependency bumps found staged in the working tree after the Docker deployment session (originating outside the session — likely a local `bun update`); verified before landing: `bun install` clean against the updated lockfile, `bun run --cwd app/web-next build` green, unit suite unchanged (the one failing file, `skill-graph.test.ts`, fails identically on clean main and is unrelated — tracked separately).
- **[2026-09-21]**: fix(web-next): **Admin ADR route works in the containerized runtime (design `2026-09-21-docker-prod-cicd` follow-up).** `GET /api/admin/adr` 500'd inside the production container: Turbopack rewrites `import.meta.dirname` to a synthetic import-meta whose `dirname` is `undefined` at bundled runtime, so `path.resolve(undefined, …)` threw (`paths[0] must be of type string`). The route now locates `docs/adr` by walking up from cwd (the established `proc-resolve.ts` discipline), which also fixes a latent off-by-one — the route file sits 5 levels above the repo root, not 6, so the direct-process path silently scanned the wrong (nonexistent) directory and always returned an empty ADR list.
- **[2026-09-21]**: feat(deploy): **Docker production deployment + image CI/CD (design `2026-09-21-docker-prod-cicd`).** Production now runs as a Docker container on the operator workstation (host port 7748 → container 7749; dev stays a direct host process on 7749): multi-stage `oven/bun` image (non-root, whitelist `.dockerignore`, carries root `scripts/` + `node_modules` for runtime spawns, `--ignore-scripts` since nothing imports better-sqlite3 at runtime), SQLite persisted on a named volume with boot-time migrations + admin bootstrap, and `docker/entrypoint.sh` automating the ADR-0014 pre-migration backup. CI: `.github/workflows/docker.yml` builds the image on relevant PRs (no push) and publishes `ghcr.io/5throck/co-newbiz-web` (`latest` + `sha-*`) on main. CD: `scripts/deploy-web.ts` (manual by design) gates completion on the `/api/health` check with concrete rollback hints. Production config lives only in host-local `.env.prod` (`.env.prod.sample` template). Deployment runbook gains §8 (container production path, en/ko); known v1 parity gaps (Python-venv calibration subprocesses, playwright browsers) documented there.

- **[2026-09-20]**: feat(web-next): **Escalation downstream follow-ups — divergence warning + our-company escalation mark (design `2026-09-20-escalation-downstream-design` §4 Q2/Q3 resolved).** The S8 business-case GET returns `escalated_cagr_pct` for the project's industry (project -> source opportunity -> `market_scope` -> `md_industry` -> resolver), and the scenario table's historical rows show a `warn` chip when the DART historical CAGR deviates from the adjusted value by more than 15%p (simple threshold, documented; sigma-based threshold left as a tunable). The our-company profile surfaces escalation in the `산업 연계` table: the profile pipeline builder stamps `escalatedCagrPct` on newly analyzed profiles and the member-profile GET enriches stored payloads read-time via the resolver (payloads never rewritten); the S3 `산업 연계` table renders an escalation chip. Q1 (band MC re-centering) stays deferred until a new calibration loop opens.
- **[2026-09-20]**: feat(web-next): **Escalation downstream adoption — `resolveEffectiveCagr` resolver (design `2026-09-20-escalation-downstream-design` §3.1/§3.2, status implemented, stage 1).** The resolver returns the effective industry CAGR with provenance: the LATEST completed version's bottom-up value by default, overridden by the recorded deterministic-scaling decision when the escalation is newer than the version (recency rule — a newer rerun falls back to bottom-up and re-triggers judgment; version payloads stay untouched). Consumers adopted: the S8 `business-case-generator` market-forward growth now prefers the escalated value for the project's industry (via `opportunity.market_scope` -> `md_industry`, provenance stamped into the persisted `growth_source` note) and the S2 `insights` API returns `escalated_cagr_pct` beside `bottom_up_cagr_pct`, surfaced as an escalation chip (escalation label) on the opportunity-discovery industry chips. Band-MC adoption intentionally deferred — escalated industries have closed calibration loops, so sweeps no longer apply (revisit if a new loop opens). Tests: resolver recency rules added to `tests/unit/calibration-escalation.test.ts` (9/9).

GET `.../calibration` now returns `escalation`. Tests `tests/unit/calibration-escalation.test.ts` (exact 15% residual math, latest-version base, append-only re-apply, already-converged and missing-block rejections). - **[2026-09-20]**: feat(process): **Graph reconciliation for DEG stage axis (ADR-0083 PR2).** Enhanced `scripts/co-newbiz/graph.ts` (v0.5.0): new `loadStageAliases()` loader reads `process/stages.yaml` + `process/stage-aliases.yaml` with `existsSync` guards (fail-open: graph degrades gracefully if files missing); emits 8 stage nodes (`stage.co-newbiz.S1...S8` with `gate_alias` property), 7 `stage_follows` edges (order-adjacent stages), and 20 `in_stage` edges (procedure → stage via alias lookup); renames two edge types for DEG conformance (`executes_agent` → `step_by_agent`, `executes_skill` → `step_uses_skill`); declares `graph_profile: "deg/v1"` at top level (sibling of `meta`/`nodes`/`edges`); fixes version drift by using module-level `GENERATOR_VERSION` constant for both header comment and `meta.generator_version`. Enhanced `scripts/co-newbiz/graph-map.ts` (v1.2.0): added SA-5 projection-level validation — every procedure node with a non-empty `gate` must have exactly one outgoing `in_stage` edge (mirrors SA-2 registry check, catches generator emission failures). Regenerated `docs/graph/graph.{json,md}` with stage nodes, new edge types, and `unaliased_gates` warning array (currently 1: deal-shape-chain gate 'P0'). Updated `scripts/SCRIPTS.md` with v0.5.0 (graph.ts) and v1.2.0 (graph-map.ts) rows. PR1 (stage adapter files) merged 2026-09-20; PR2 depends on it and must follow.
- **[2026-09-19]**: feat(web-next): **Phase 2+3 — calibration sweep (reverse-feasibility) + cycle orchestrator (design `2026-09-19-sim-engine-runtime-separation` §2·§4·§5).** Sweep: `POST/GET .../calibration-sweep` samples accepted-driver impact combinations (±50% triangular distribution, seeded mulberry32 — deterministic per seed) with the v1 plausibility filter (combinations whose summed impact exceeds 2× the needed uplift are excluded; exclusion ratio preserved), producing the reverse-feasibility view — per-driver contribution quantiles, gap P10/P50/P90, feasible ratio vs the ≤15% convergence line, and a verdict (`feasible`/`infeasible`). Results store quantile summaries only (`calibration_sweep_result`, migration 0118) — raw samples are never persisted. Cycle orchestrator: `POST .../calibration-cycle` accepts pending proposals (a person's action, audited) and submits market-sizing rerun jobs to the parallel execution layer (phase 1) in one call. Band Monte Carlo: CAGR down/base/up sampling yields 2030-size P10/P50/P90 in the sweep response. Math tests `tests/unit/calibration-sweep.test.ts` (seeded determinism, triangular bounds, plausibility filter, band ordering, convergence constant 15%). Routes registered in `openapi.yaml`.
- **[2026-09-19]**: feat(web-next): **Phase 1 — independent parallel execution layer (design `2026-09-19-sim-engine-runtime-separation` §3).** A feature-agnostic job layer: migration 0117 `execution_job` (kind/payload, resource_class `llm|web|numeric`, status, attempt/max_retry, timeout, lock columns) replaces process-local job state — queued jobs survive restarts, and stale running locks are reclaimed (`reclaimStaleJobs`). Claiming is atomic under SQLite's single-writer constraint: `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transactions with a `WHERE status='queued'` guard (`claimNextJob` FIFO + class filter, `claimJobById` for re-runs); `db/client.ts` gains `busy_timeout=5000` so concurrent writers wait instead of failing. `scripts/co-newbiz/job-worker.ts` is the child-process entrypoint — claims one job (or drains with `--loop`), resolves the adapter from the handler registry, runs with timeout, records success/failure with retry (deterministic errors finalize immediately via `noRetry` — no busy spin); `--handlers` loads extension modules, `--class` enforces resource-class dispatch. Proof adapter: `market-sizing.rerun` enters the same ADR-0131 protected mode as the rerun route and runs the sizing pipeline. Parallelism = spawning N workers (orchestrator phase 3); class concurrency caps default `llm:3/web:2/numeric:2` with env overrides. Tests `tests/unit/execution-jobs.test.ts`: lifecycle/requeue-exhaustion/noRetry/cancel/stale-reclaim/caps plus an e2e where TWO real worker processes concurrently drain 4 jobs on one scratch DB — every job runs exactly once (`attempt=1`). Also carries the two-line gate-enum widening (`target_screening` in `lib/deliberation.ts` + the committee-sessions route) that PR #351 also contains — needed for a green `tsc` on this base; identical edits, merge-safe.
- **[2026-09-19]**: docs(designs): **sim-engine separation design rev.2 — review findings folded in.** AI review P1 (methodology: ±50% triangular sampling distribution with optional `spread_pct`, plausibility filter + correlation groups, sweep reframed as a reverse-feasibility view with reference dispersion as input uncertainty), P2 (GPU-suitability wording corrected — the sweep is CPU-scale; separation justified by the shared runtime; governance: L0/L1 propagation coexistence, optional `requirements-gpu-*` installs, CI runs CPU-only; storage contract: `calibration_sweep_result` stores quantile summaries only, never raw samples), and P3 (convergence line tightened to **≤15%** per user adjustment with a 15–20% hysteresis band against the unchanged ≥20% trigger, 3-cycle escalation to deterministic CAGR scaling as a human decision, sweep-before-rerun ordering, a verification section with sweep-math determinism tests, measured feasibility evidence) — plus a new §3: an **independent parallel execution layer** (feature-agnostic job table + resource-class concurrency + child-process workers; user architecture directive 2026-09-19) and a 3-phase rollout each gated on separate approval. Spec registered: `2026-09-19-sim-engine-runtime-separation`.
- **[2026-09-19]**: fix(web-next): **DDG fallback visibility + gate enum widening (0111 leftovers) + sim-engine separation draft.** `web-research.ts` logs one info line when the DuckDuckGo fallback engages (query count, Tavily quota flag) and when it returns 0 results — success was previously invisible, which read as "DDG not used" during the S1 calibration reruns (Tavily 432 quota exhaustion made the fallback the primary path). `lib/deliberation.ts` RunDeliberationOpts and the committee-sessions route's ConveneGate widen their gate enums to accept `target_screening` (0111 alignment — main-level type break surfaced after PR #343/#348 merges; the brownfield committee convenes the S7 gate). `docs/designs/2026-09-19-sim-engine-runtime-separation-design.md` drafts the separation of `python/sim_engine/device.py` into a shared GPU/CPU runtime with its industry-analysis connection points (probabilistic sweep of accepted driver-impact combinations, calibrated CAGR band Monte Carlo) — implementation is gated on review; the loop bottleneck remains the LLM call chain, stated explicitly.

- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(web-next): **S1 growth-gap calibration loop (systematic-market-sizing v1.2.0).** Closes the "re-analysis just repeats and the gap never shrinks" defect with a three-link loop: (1) recorded external references — migration 0113 `market_external_reference` stores an analyst-entered external CAGR with a mandatory source label (plus `captured_from` provenance for `llm_prior` forecasts, which are persisted once so reruns stop re-rolling prior knowledge); the sizing pipeline's step 5-B uses the recorded reference first; (2) a 20% trigger — when the relative CAGR gap (`|ours−external|/external`) reaches 20%, the version records `matrix.calibration {triggered, ourCagrPct, externalCagrPct, gapPct, previousGapPct, appliedDrivers}`, the report shows a `보정 필요` badge, and a new step 5-C asks the LLM for numeric driver attributions (direction + estimated CAGR contribution in %p + rationale) stored as PROPOSALS in `industry_driver_calibration` (previous undecided proposals superseded); (3) convergence — a person accepts/dismisses each proposal (audited, boundary #2), and accepted drivers plus the reference and previous gap are injected as constraints into the next rerun's step-2/4 prompts with a remaining-gap justification requirement, while the report's new `보정 루프` panel (trigger badge, reference editor, proposal cards, accepted-driver list, version gap trend) makes convergence visible; `insights` exposes `calibration` so S2 stays gap-aware. Methodology doc updated (`skills/systematic-market-sizing/SKILL.md` step 5-B + new 5-C); `flow.spec.yaml` S1 output annotated (comment only — no structure change). Tests: `tests/unit/market-calibration.test.ts` (trigger math, reference resolution order, constraint builder, decision+audit flow). Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-18-s1-growth-gap-calibration-design.md`. New routes registered in `openapi.yaml`.
- **[2026-09-18]**: docs(governance): **ADR-0142 + skill sync for the simulation engine work.** New ADR-0142 records the architecture decisions behind the standalone simulation engine (engine extraction to `python/sim_engine/`, PyTorch device layer with cuda>mps>xpu>rocm auto-detect, seed redefined as `fnv1a32(case_id + ":" + sha256(canonical_drivers))` amending ADR-0117's shared-seed clause for engine-generated runs, and the inputs-json snapshot contract) with the ADR README index updated. Skill sync per design section 8: `wacc-input-session` 1.4.0 documents the WACC application subject (promoter/target/project_newco with mandatory rationale) and the relevered-beta reference; `investment-feasibility-modeling` 1.2.0 documents the sim_engine job-spec contract and the BEP year/volume/discounted-payback outputs. Audit that surfaced these gaps: design docs (2, both implemented), openapi.yaml, process-flow.md, checklists (gf-12/bf-13), procedures (target_costing 6.96/7.96), and CHANGELOG were verified reflected on main; the two skill updates and the ADR were the outstanding items.
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Target-costing price anchor automation — web-search evidence drafts (spec 2026-09-18-target-costing-price-anchor, ADR-0118/0141 pattern).** New `POST /api/projects/{id}/target-costing/price-draft` runs the Tavily-to-DDG chain (`lib/web-research.ts`) over three subject query variants and extracts price candidates via the new `s8.price_evidence` prompt (prompts registry regenerated, 30 entries). Rules enforced in both the prompt and the parser: a candidate without explicit currency AND unit is not a price; a candidate without a source URL is dropped; zero candidates returns an explicit not-confirmed note - a missing price is reported as missing, never fabricated. PROPOSALS ONLY: the draft endpoint persists nothing (eval-draft contract); the human picks a candidate card in the target-costing tab (unit price + source link + quote + confidence), the panel fills the form, and confirmation lands through the existing target-costing POST with source=peer_benchmark and the adopted citation preserved in the new nullable `target_costing_analysis.price_evidence_json` (migration 0116). Deterministic fallbacks keep the flow unblocked without search keys or LLM configuration (verified live on the demo seed). Tests: query builder, unconfigured-search note, search-without-LLM fallback, and evidence persistence + no-citation rejection (target-costing suite 8/8); routes registered in `docs/architecture/openapi.yaml`.
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Target-costing computation service — REQ-W2 completed.** Migration 0115 `target_costing_analysis` (market price, target margin, allowable cost, estimated unit cost, gap, gap ratio, threshold flag, reduction levers); `lib/target-costing.ts` pure gap computation (`allowable = price x (1 - margin)`, gap beyond +10% of allowable is material) with persistence and `resolveDownsideOpexUplift`; `GET/POST /api/projects/{id}/target-costing` (analyst+); the S8 page gains a target-costing tab (gap card, live preview, reduction levers). Generation wiring: a beyond-threshold analysis uplifts ONLY the downside scenario's opex driver by the gap ratio (job-spec source label + `tc_gap_ratio`/`tc_downside_opex_uplift` note stamps; base/upside unchanged). Market-price anchoring starts user-entry/peer-benchmark-record based; external price data auto-linkage is a separate design. Tests: pure math incl. exact-threshold direction, validation errors, route roundtrip, and downside-only uplift wiring (4/4); routes registered in `docs/architecture/openapi.yaml`.
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Deterministic VERSION_MANIFEST ordering + manual sales-plan lines (follow-ups from the live review and CI).** `generate-version-manifest.ts` tie-breaks equal script basenames by location (template copy `scripts/design-lint.ts` v1.0.0 vs project copy `scripts/co-newbiz/design-lint.ts` v1.2.0 sorted opposite ways on different filesystems, flaking the CI Documentation Audit); the business-case line editor (POST/PUT) now accepts `sales_volume`/`unit_price_krw` (nullable, must be paired, non-negative) so the REQ-W1-1 sales-plan decomposition is manually enterable, with the columns exposed on GET; the new `business-case/params` routes are registered in `docs/architecture/openapi.yaml`.
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Design Phases 3-4 - WACC application subject rule, S8 parameter panel, and the live GUI review gate (spec 2026-09-18-s8-business-case-evaluation W4-W6, W9).** WACC subject (REQ-W4): migration 0114 adds `md_project.wacc_subject` (+rationale/decided_by/at; the subject is a string enum so it lives on the project row, not the numeric assumption table) with track-based defaults (greenfield=promoter, brownfield=target) resolved from the route decision; `resolveProjectWaccSubject`/`setProjectWaccSubject` (rationale mandatory) / `resolveReleveredBeta` (beta_unlevered x (1+(1-t) x D/E) from the session's components) land in `db/lib/wacc.ts`; the wacc API exposes subject+relevering on GET and accepts subject/subject_rationale on POST; WaccPanel gains the subject select+rationale form and the confirmed-value card shows the subject chip and relevered-beta reference; checklists add gf-12/bf-13; generation stamps `wacc_subject=` into notes and the job-spec discount_rate.subject. Parameters (REQ-W6): the full `bc.*` vocabulary resolves with provenance (opex/capex/initial-investment ratios, growth/sigma overrides, horizon, iterations - user > derived > default), a new `POST /api/projects/[id]/business-case/params` records user batches on a fresh run (rationale required), and the S8 page gains a parameter-snapshot tab (ParamsPanel) rendering the resolution table, the edit form, and the latest model's job-spec snapshot including the discount-rate subject. GUI review gate (REQ-W9): live browser review on the demo seed - one P0 found and fixed (legacy/placeholder snapshots crashed ParamsPanel; now defensive rendering with a regenerate hint, and the demo seed writes a real job-spec snapshot); functional, usability, and readability axes verified with screenshots (gui-review-screenshots/t20-t24).
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Design Phase 2 — sales-plan decomposition, BEP persistence, and the target-costing step (spec 2026-09-18-s8-business-case-evaluation W1–W3).** Migrations 0111–0113: `business_case_line` gains nullable `sales_volume`/`unit_price_krw` (revenue = volume × price when both are known), `valuation_simulation` gains `bep_year`/`bep_volume`/`payback_discounted_years` (p50 row), and `business_case_model` gains `inputs_json` — the persisted job-spec snapshot, so "which parameters produced this distribution" is structured data instead of a parsed note. The engine grew a `ramp_up` driver (utilization curve by year, holds its last value, applied to the whole FCF and the deterministic path alike); the TS fallback simulator ports the identical ramp and the identical discounted-payback/BEP-year math (`discountedPaybackYear`), so both generation paths satisfy the contract. Sales-plan parameters are user-resolvable as `bc.capture_rate` and `bc.ramp_up.y1..yN` user assumptions (defaults 0.5% / `[0.4,0.7,0.9,1.0,1.0]`, provenance stamped into every note and the job-spec). The fs spec's S8 outputs/requiredArtifacts/completionCriteria gain the BEP year + input snapshot (mirrored into `process-flow.ts` STAGES + the S8 completion check, `docs/process-flow.md` regenerated) — pre-Phase-2 models now block S8 completion with a regenerate hint. FS procedures (`greenfield-fs` step 6.96, `brownfield-fs` step 7.96) add the `target_costing` step (financial-analyst) backed by the new `target-costing-analysis` skill and the registered `target_cost_gap` output type. Test fixtures for downstream-stage predicates (deal-structure/ic-package/portfolio/seed demo) updated to the new artifact set; the ADR-0098 predicate test now documents the two-stage completion (bare simulation row still blocks until bep_year + inputs_json exist).
- **[2026-09-18]**: feat(sim): **Standalone Monte Carlo simulation engine (GPU/CPU hybrid) with the S8 generator demoted to a job-spec builder (design Phase 1).** New generic engine `python/sim_engine/` executes any driver-set job-spec (normal/uniform/triangular/constant distributions) with a PyTorch-single-abstraction device layer (auto-detect cuda > mps > xpu > rocm, numpy CPU fallback with recorded warnings, GPU payoff threshold for tiny jobs, optional `requirements-gpu-{cuda,rocm,mps,xpu}.txt` extras), case-level multiprocessing on CPU / batch streaming on GPU, and seed = `fnv1a32(case_id + sha256(canonical drivers))` so ADR-0052 §D.4 determinism survives parameter changes. Outputs NPV percentiles, IRR, discounted payback / BEP year, BEP volume, and echoes the full input job-spec — the snapshot IS the execution contract, ending note-string-only provenance at the engine boundary. `app/web-next/lib/sim-engine.ts` spawns it (cached availability probe, `CO_NEWBIZ_SIM_ENGINE=off` kill switch, 120s timeout); `business-case-generator.ts` keeps its constants but now builds job-specs and falls back to the legacy in-process simulator when the engine is unavailable, stamping `engine=`/`device=`/`precision=`/`bep_year=` into every valuation_simulation note, and step-5 reuses the step-4 simulation instead of re-running it. Tests: `tests/unit/sim-engine.test.ts` (closed-form parity, seeded determinism, TS-independent seed cross-check, BEP volume, device fallback, parallel==serial, contract errors) and `tests/unit/business-case-sim-engine.test.ts` (engine-path stamping, kill-switch fallback); the ADR-0098 regression is unchanged and passing. Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-18-s8-business-case-evaluation-design.md`.
- **[2026-09-17]**: feat(web-next): **Collapsible sidebar drawer with dual-home toggle, unified mobile drawer.** The permanently-docked 264px sidebar (`--sidebar-width` token) gains a standard two-mode collapse: desktop push-collapse animates `width → 0` in-flow so content reflows in sync (no scrim), and mobile (≤880px) replaces the old horizontal icon strip with a true overlay drawer (fixed panel slide-in over a fading `.drawer-backdrop` scrim, ESC/backdrop/route-change close, focus trap via a new `active` gate on `useModalFocus`, `inert` when closed). The collapse control follows the dual-home convention — it lives in the sidebar header (`.sidebar-head`) while the sidebar is visible and moves to the topbar (`.topbar-icon-btn`) only while the sidebar is hidden; both are quiet outline icon buttons calibrated through three review rounds (bordered pill → ghost → outline) after user feedback. Collapse preference persists in localStorage (`"cn-sidebar"`); `prefers-reduced-motion` disables the transitions. Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-17-sidebar-drawer-design.md`.

- **[2026-09-16]**: feat(web-next): **DuckDuckGo web-search fallback — provider chain in `web-research.ts` (Tavily primary, DDG when unconfigured/quota-exceeded/empty).** Fixes the silent-evidence-failure modes of the single-provider setup: no `TAVILY_API_KEY` or Tavily HTTP 432 now falls back to a best-effort DuckDuckGo HTML-endpoint search (browser UA, 15s timeout, pure exported parser `parseDdgResults` — `uddg` redirect decoding, tag/entity stripping, non-http(s) ads dropped, never blocks the flow), so screening/eval proposals, S6 web proposals, profile cross-check, and DD tailor keep their evidence without a paid key. `WEB_SEARCH_DDG_ENABLED` (default on) disables it; `isWebResearchConfigured()` now means "Tavily key OR DDG enabled". Regression tests `tests/unit/web-research-ddg.test.ts` cover parser decoding, 432→DDG fallback with `quotaExceeded` preserved, keyless+disabled = zero network calls, and Tavily-success skipping DDG. Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-16-ddg-web-search-fallback-design.md`.
- **[2026-09-16]**: fix(web-next): **S6/S7 screening pre-check restored — t-01~09 verdict recording and visibility for the committee basis.** The 2026-09-15 redesign had removed the t-01~09 verdict strip without removing its consumers, leaving the Short List `인수 가능성` excerpt permanently `미판정` and the committee (target-longlist-shortlist-committee) without its stated t-01~09 basis. Recording now lives on the candidate detail page as a new `사전 검토` profile tab (all 9 items, 4-value select, `판정 저장` via the existing `screening_json` merge route, `판정 초안 생성 (웹검색)` via the existing screening-draft route — pending items only); `GET /api/brownfield/targets/[targetId]` additionally returns `checklist` + `screening_summary`; the Long List table gains a `사전 검토` `assessed/total` column (red `knock-out 실패` state, tooltip lists pending mandatory items) using the already-computed-but-never-displayed `screening_summary`; the detail header card gains a matching chip; and `buildEvalBriefMd` switches the committee brief back to a per-candidate t-01~09 verdict table (eval combined score kept as a secondary context column with a role-separation legend). The Short List `인수 가능성` cards now show a `판정 n/9` progress chip, color-coded verdict chips with item short-labels (not bare `미판정` text lines), and a deep link to the candidate's pre-check tab (`?tab=screening` honored by the detail page). Same-day repositioning per user decision: **chk-target-screening is a Short-List final check with an integrated committee recommendation flow and evidence persistence** — the Short List section (`사전 검토 · 위원회 추천`) gains a primary `위원회 추천 검토` button that runs all promoted candidates through the enhanced screening-draft route: it reuses already-secured information (DART 5-year financial facts, company AI profile, recent disclosure titles — never re-searched; t-08 `재무적 매력도` is judged purely from secured facts when available) and web-searches only the missing items, returning per-item verdict proposals with rationale/source/kind (`secured`|`web`); each candidate card shows a deterministic `추천안` badge (`추천`/`보류`/`제외 검토` from Part-1 knock-outs t-01~t-05), the human reviews the selects and confirms via `판정 저장`, and the committee panel now sits inside the same section with its convening brief including the unconfirmed proposals. Evidence persistence: migration 0108 (`brownfield_target.screening_evidence_json` — per-item rationale/source/kind, merged by the verdict-save route's new `evidence` payload) and 0109 (`brownfield_target_evidence` — per-candidate timeline; `discovery` rows at registration with proposal rationale/source, `analysis` rows when the company profile is re-analyzed for linked targets, `screening` rows for pre-check runs and human confirmations); the candidate detail page gains a conditional read-only `사전 검토 근거` tab (confirmed verdict strip + per-item evidence + collection timeline) — recording stays exclusively on the /brownfield section. The Long List column and the old recording tab were removed (checklist has no Long-List role; transient detail-API fields no longer needed were re-scoped), and the `잠정 순위` column no longer loses the eval-based rank on promotion/exclusion (`evalRank` over all non-excluded candidates; shortlisted rows show their rank muted as reference). Dead code removed from `/brownfield`: `saveScreening`, `generateScreeningDraft`, `screeningDraft`/`screeningReco` state, unused `VERDICTS`/`EVAL_VERDICTS` constants. Promotion gating is unchanged (financial/technical evaluation completion, 2026-09-15 decision) — screening verdicts stay advisory. Pre-check surface re-arranged into a transposed table (items as column headers grouped Part 1 knock-out / Part 2 recommended, candidates as rows, one verdict select per cell) per user proposal; the duplicate per-row `초안 재생성` button was removed (`위원회 추천 검토` is the single idempotent trigger — re-runs fill only remaining pending items), item search queries rebuilt as `company + short label` + `company + first sub-clause` (the old 40-char sentence query had near-zero recall), all pending items are now judging candidates (LLM judges from the secured block even without per-item web evidence and omits only unsupported items — prompts-registry `s7.screening_draft` updated and regenerated), and the response note reports explicit proposal coverage (`n/m 제안 — 기확보 x + 웹검색 y, 나머지 근거 부족`). Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-16-s6s7-screening-precheck-restore-design.md`. Same-day verdict-scale overhaul per user decision: **chk-target-screening verdicts move from pass/fail/na/waived to a 5-level evidence-aware scale** — `confirmed`(`충족 — 근거 명확`) / `probable`(`충족 — 추정`) / `partial`(`부분 충족`) / `unfulfilled`(`미충족`) / `insufficient`(`근거 부족`) — with `insufficient` as a first-class honest verdict so the LLM assigns one of five to EVERY pending item (full proposal coverage compatible with the anti-fabrication boundary); legacy stored values are normalized on read (no migration) and the verdict-save route validates only the new scale. The committee's recommendation rule becomes 4-tier from Part-1 knock-outs (`추천` / `조건부 추천` / `보류 — 근거 부족` / `제외 검토`), matching its role as the recommendation decider on top of verdicts. Evidence gathering deepened to 3 query variants × up to 6 results per item with the LLM budget raised to justify all nine items; shared verdict helpers live in the client-safe `lib/brownfield-precheck.ts`, the prompts-registry `s7.screening_draft` system prompt was rewritten for the scale and regenerated, and `tests/unit/web-research-ddg.test.ts` clears the global research test-override so the provider chain is testable in combined runs.

- **[2026-09-16]**: feat(web-next): **S6/S7 evaluation auto-draft — deterministic financial rubric (ADR-0140) + evidence-based technical draft with explicit unverified marker (ADR-0141), candidate-detail UX refinements.** Financial: new `lib/brownfield-financial-rubric.ts` computes pass/partial/fail proposals from `fact_financial` via `deriveFinancialAnalysis` (no LLM — CAGR/black-year/debt-ratio rules with conservative fallbacks for undefined CAGR and insolvency); per-criterion thresholds live in `brownfield_eval_criterion.rubric_json` (migration 0107, NULL = code defaults) editable in the eval tab via extended `PATCH eval-criteria` (financial builtins only, audited); a 4th builtin `net_income_5y` (`당기순이익`) added with seeding switched to per-`data_ref` so existing opportunities are topped up. Technical: `lib/brownfield-technical-draft.ts` assembles evidence (last-3-years DART disclosure titles via a new optional `searchDisclosures` months param, company AI profile, per-criterion web search) and asks the LLM under the `s7.screening_draft` prompt; unverifiable items return an explicit `확인 안됨` status with reason, pre-filled into the note. Both drafts flow through `POST .../eval-draft {category}` with nothing persisted — humans confirm via the existing `PUT eval-state` (assessed_by stays a person, already-judged items never drafted over), individually or via `초안 저장`. UX: always-visible `재분석`/`정보 수정`/`후보 삭제` tab actions (`재분석` first), draft badges + editable notes in the eval tab, the redundant `재무 facts` card removed from the financials tab (load-time auto-sync retained — it feeds the draft), overview/SWOT `[n]` research citations now link to the actual external sources with used-research recorded inline and in the sources tab, profile-tab font sizes aligned to the SWOT standard (body 0.88rem / headers 0.9rem), and the `목표 시장 정의`/`후보 발굴` grid rebalanced 1:1 → 2:3. Design registered: `docs/designs/2026-09-16-brownfield-eval-auto-draft-design.md`.
- **[2026-09-16]**: feat(web-next): **Brownfield candidate profile pipeline generalized to `md_company`, S1 industry-name display fixes.** Generalized `company-profile-pipeline.ts` from a `company_group_member`-only pipeline to a `ProfileSubject` union (`member` | `company`) via migrations 0105/0106 (`company_profile_version.company_id` column, `member_id` relaxed to nullable with a CHECK constraint), so the brownfield candidate detail page (`/brownfield/targets/[targetId]`) can run the same LLM research/SWOT/DART-financials pipeline as the group/affiliate screen; added `/api/companies/[companyId]/analyze` and `/profile` routes plus a richer candidate detail screen (DART account-level chart+table, a dedicated sources tab, a restructured strengths/weaknesses SWOT tab per iterative UX feedback, redundant candidate-info list removed). Also: brownfield list-page UI consistency pass (`SectionToolbar` everywhere, `btn-premium` primary action, unified DART search+registration form, 1:1 section grid), and S1 market-analysis industry-name column widened with LLM citation markers (`[2]`, `[11]`) stripped from industry names at both proposal-sanitize and persistence time (2 pre-existing bad rows cleaned up in the dev DB). Backfilled `openapi.yaml`/`navigation.ts` registrations for routes/pages added this session.

- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **S6/S7 screen restructured to the baseline-v5 single-flow mockup — no tabs, eval-driven ranking, committee grounded in t-01~09.** Removed the universe/longlist/shortlist tab machinery (`?tab=`, `TabNav`) in favor of one continuous page matching `docs/designs/mockups/baseline-v5.html`'s S6/S7 step-flow, with a clickable ①~⑥ flow banner that smooth-scrolls to anchored sections; `목표 시장 정의` and `후보 발굴` now sit side-by-side (1:2 grid) with the manual-entry form always visible and a dismiss (`무시`) button on web-search suggestions. Ranking flipped to the financial/technical evaluation layer: new `combinedEvalScore()` (average of the two 100-point weighted scores), `eval_summary` attached per target on `GET /api/brownfield/[oppId]/targets`, a `잠정 순위` column that pre-promotion-sorts the Long List by combined score, and the old checklist-derived `targetScore()` removed. Committee deliberation (`target-longlist-shortlist-committee`) is now grounded in the t-01~09 screening checklist via a new `briefMd` field threaded through `ConveneOpts`/`SessionMeta`/the committee-sessions API/`CommitteePanel` (persisted so round 2+ rebuilds the same enriched topic), replacing the former generic topic string; the committee panel renders above the gate-decision panel and the separate screening-column/funnel-chip surfaces were removed as redundant. Also: remaining user-facing `유니버스` strings renamed to `목표 시장(정의)`, and `chk-target-screening` checklist item texts translated to Korean in `checklists/target-screening-checklist.yaml` (mirrored into `library.generated.ts`).
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **S6 candidate registration now provisions the company master and evaluation catalog eagerly, and S7 financial criteria are concrete 5-year benchmarks.** `createTarget()` (both the manual `/targets` POST and the web-search `/target-proposals` accept path, previously a duplicate inline INSERT — now consolidated) wraps target creation, `resolveOrCreateCompany()` (extracted from the Short List promotion route), and `listOrSeedCriteria()` in one transaction, so a Long List candidate has a resolvable `md_company` and a seeded eval catalog from the moment it's confirmed rather than only after Short List promotion. The 5 generic builtin financial criteria (`매출성장률`/`영업이익률`/`순이익률`/`부채비율`/`영업부문`) are replaced with 3 concrete 5-year criteria (`매출액`/`영업이익`/`부채 규모·추이`, with margin ratios kept as reference lines rather than dropped) via `scripts/co-newbiz/backfill-brownfield-financial-criteria.ts` for already-seeded opportunities. `/brownfield`'s Universe tab now hosts candidate search/confirm (moved from Long List), Long List rows show a `재무`/`기술` evaluation-progress badge plus an inline DART reference panel (multi-year revenue/operating-profit/debt + ratios, with a sync CTA and a <5-year data warning), and Short List gained an advisory `인수 가능성` excerpt of the t-01/t-02/t-08 checklist items. A companion static review mockup (`/brownfield-mockup`, non-menu route) implements a ux-designer-agent redesign proposal (search-first Universe table, a drawer-based Long List evaluation workspace replacing the deeply-nested expanded row, and consolidated Short List decision cards) for review before implementation.
- **[2026-09-15]**: chore(upgrade): synced template infrastructure via `upgrade-project.ts` (workspace common v0.6.0) — refreshed scripts, agent/skill mirrors (`.claude/`, `.gemini/`, `.codex/`, `.agents/`), `docs/context.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `.githooks/`, and `docs/workspace-schema.json` to pick up governance and lifecycle fixes landed upstream since the last upgrade at the same recorded template version.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(llm): **Prompt registry — system prompts, owning agents, and ledger purposes centralized in a generated SSOT.** New `app/web-next/prompts/prompts.json` (29 entries: stage-scoped keys, agent, ledger purpose, system text) projected by `scripts/co-newbiz/generate-prompt-registry.ts` into typed `lib/prompts.generated.ts`, accessed only via `lib/prompts.ts` helpers (`promptSystem`/`systemMessage`/`promptAgent` with runtime-agent override). 24 files migrated off inline prompt/agent string literals — prompt edits are now single-file SSOT diffs that still pass regeneration → tests → /sync governance; the synergy askJson wrapper now takes a `PromptKey` (fixing a pre-existing S3-pipeline purpose mislabel), and an integrity test enforces SSOT↔generated sync, roster-valid agent keys, and the override contract. Round-trip verification guaranteed zero prompt-text drift; unit suite, audit, and the web-next production build all pass.
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(llm): **Runtime LLM orchestration hardening (scoped project review).** Closed three ADR-0118/0128 contract escapes: (1) run-ledger coverage — `complete()` now records to `llm_call_log` itself (single-write; `completeOrFallback` llm-path logging moved there) and previously unledgered calls (synergy FA summary, market-sizing summarize + KSIC classify, business-case executors with per-step agent keys, DD tailor → technical-dd-agent, committee-llm) now declare `agent` meta; (2) `GET /api/synergy/web-search` enforces the analyst+ floor (viewer 403) like its sibling routes; (3) S6 web-search proposal ledger key aligned to the flow.spec owner (market-intelligence-agent). Also: LLM-chosen KSIC fills now append `md_industry.ksic_llm_fill` audit provenance, Tavily failures log their reason instead of silently returning empty, the root-`.env` loader strips surrounding quotes, and AGENTS.md cites workspace-root ADR-0078 unambiguously. Backfilled and registered the missing ADR-0128 design doc plus this remediation design. Report: docs/reports/2026-09-15-project-review-llm-orchestration.md.
- **[2026-09-15]**: chore(governance): **Post-upgrade project review remediation.** Removed the unmarkerized duplicate 3-tier model-mapping block in GEMINI.md (MM-01), added the missing "Project Boundary Policy" section to GEMINI.md (P-01 parity with CLAUDE.md), retargeted inherited dead anchors in CLAUDE.md/GEMINI.md (AGENTS.md §3.6/§5.1/§5.1.1 links now point at the workspace-root AGENTS.md) and the ADR-0036/0054 links in scripts/SCRIPTS.md, raised agents/pm.md tier to high to match docs/workspace-schema.json and the AGENTS.md roster plus added its missing `role` field, refreshed stale `last_updated` frontmatter on 40 agent files to their last git commit date, created the two missing governance records (docs/lifecycle/agents/design-system-agent.md, i18n-specialist.md), added `domain: _shared` to i18n-specialist, corrected the stale roster counts in docs/meta/co-newbiz.context.md (41 agents / functional 25 / technical 11 / _shared 2) and the AGENTS.md specialist count (40), filled the scaffold placeholders in docs/context.md, and reconciled the local language-policy exception wording with the COMMON-CONSTITUTION block. audit.ts all-pass, validate-templates project-relevant errors 12→0, agent-lifecycle-audit 41→0 warnings, agent:verify 41/41.
