The upgraded `validate-md-language.ts` (v1.11.0) now scans `docs/adr/` and `docs/decisions/`, surfacing 61 pre-existing Korean-content files without declarations — declared `lang: ko` + `lang_reason: source-material` on 56 ADRs and 4 decision records (matching the established docs/designs precedent) and wrapped the VERSION_MANIFEST Skills table in the generated-region allowlist marker (T-20260912-015 pattern).

### Added
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **S6/S7 LLM-assisted discovery — web-search-grounded Long List proposals, universe criteria draft, screening verdict draft (ADR-0118 §5 unblocked).** ADR-0118 deferred S6 candidate-company suggestion until live-search evidence existed; `lib/web-research.ts` (Tavily) already serving DD tailoring makes it implementable, and `brownfield_target.discovery_source='web_proposal'` (migration 0087) finally gets written. All three features follow the ADR-0118 contract (on-demand POST, analyst+ floor, `completeOrFallback` whitelist normalization, never-fabricate deterministic fallback, audited, human-click-to-apply): (B) `POST /api/brownfield/[oppId]/target-proposals/web-search` grounds candidate companies in real Tavily evidence — each proposal carries cited source URLs and an A/B/C reliability grade, evidence-citation-less candidates are discarded, and acceptance reuses the existing registration route; (D) `POST .../universe-criteria-draft` drafts Target Universe selection criteria from a disclosed S1+S3 manifest and its `"적용"` fills the form only (saving stays human); (E) `POST .../targets/[targetId]/screening-draft` drafts t-01~t-09 verdicts per checklist item from item-specific web evidence — ungrounded items stay `미판정` with an explicit `"판정은 사람이 직접"` note, drafted selects are tagged `"LLM 제안 · 미확정"` until edited, and the save/score/knock-out/promotion path is untouched. `/brownfield` is also split into universe/longlist/shortlist tabs with `?tab=` deep-link sync, moving the gate/committee/promotion panels out from under a 600-line scroll onto the decision-focused Short List tab. Mockup-approved process (interactive mockup: `docs/designs/mockups/2026-09-15-s6s7-brownfield-tabs-redesign.html`); design doc spec-registered.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **S6 proposal discovery was silently dead for its entire lifecycle, and `"판정 저장"` locked the promotion button.** (1) The target-proposals route was keyed by PROJECT id and resolved the opportunity through `md_project.source_opportunity_id` — before S7→S8 promotion (exactly when S6 runs) GET always 404'd and POST failed with `not_promoted`, so the deterministic `"후보 발굴 제안"` section on `/brownfield` was permanently empty (the only test passed because it promoted first). Moved to `GET/POST /api/brownfield/[oppId]/target-proposals`, opportunity-keyed, with a pre-promotion regression test; (2) the screening-judgment POST returned `screening_summary` as a sibling of `target`, so the client's row merge overwrote the summary with undefined and left `"Short List 승격"` disabled after a fully-passed `"판정 저장"` until page reload — the route now embeds the summary in the returned row (matching GET).
### Added
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **System mode (production/development) gates deal-data deletion (ADR-0133, migration 0100).** A new `system_setting` table seeded `system_mode = 'production'` (fail-closed) — `DELETE /api/projects/[id]` and `DELETE /api/opportunities/[id]` (tombstone deletes) return `403 production_mode_delete_blocked` in production; an admin-only `GET/POST /api/admin/system-mode` (audited `system_mode_set`) plus `/admin/system-mode` page in `ADMIN_NAV` switch modes with a confirmation naming the risk. Existing tombstone-delete semantics are unchanged and no hard-delete capability was added; all other DELETE routes (tokens, share-links, PMI sub-records) are deliberately out of scope. Development DBs start in production after migration and must be switched once via the admin page.
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **Capability-level register (`구` `역량 갭`) relocated to S3 and auto-derived from synergy areas; wired into S4 as scoring evidence.** The register moved from the S2 opportunity detail's third tab to `/synergy`'s `연계 시너지` tab (right after the synergy-area table), renamed `역량 갭` → `필요 역량 수준` across every user-facing surface (S4 advisory labels, gate briefs, stage-handoff copy, API error messages; code identifiers unchanged). The synergy pipeline now derives needed-capability proposals from the analysis's own synergy areas (Short List first, ≤6, `source: "synergy_area"`, unevaluated) and merges them into the linked opportunity's register on every new/re-run — add-only, deduped by capability name, human judgment untouched (boundary #2). S4's `평가 근거` tab consumes the full register read-only via the advisory API (collapsible scaffold + summary chips inline with the heading), and the three capability-related 5-criteria rows (`역량 결정성·통합 역량·역량확보 속도`) show a live `역량 참고` evidence line while scoring; the reworked `필요 역량 추가 제안` button (ex `LLM 보유 판정 초안`) covers additional proposals, auto-saving unsaved rows first so the draft endpoint can see them.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-15]**: feat(web-next): **S3 synergy score becomes a human-confirmed record (boundary #2 closure).** The S4 route advisory voted brownfield ×2 on "`시너지 권고` 67" — but `opportunity.synergy_score` was written automatically by the synergy pipeline (ADR-0090 sanctions the write as a recommendation) and no human confirmation path existed anywhere: the opportunity form has only attractiveness, the PUT API's synergy field had no caller, and the value propagated to the S2 list, opportunity detail, and gate briefs as an indistinguishable fact. Migration 0099 adds `synergy_score_confirmed_at/by`; `GET/POST/DELETE /api/opportunities/{id}/synergy-confirm` (analyst+, audited `synergy_score.confirm`/`retract`) is the only confirmation path, with score+rationale override; `recordSynergyRecommendation` (extracted, unit-tested) invalidates a confirmation only when a rerun writes a different score. The S4 advisory now votes only on confirmed scores — an unconfirmed recommendation is a neutral info signal with the shared note; S3 renders an always-visible confirm editor (score/rationale prefilled, primary `확정 저장`, `철회`) after the collapsed toggle went unnoticed; S2 list, opportunity detail, and gate briefs carry `확정`/`권고(미확정)` markers; the S4 rules explainer documents the split.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S4 route decision screen — full mockup-approved redesign of RouteDecisionStage.tsx.** Five prior incremental edits each missed because the problem was the screen's overall information architecture, not any one section — a "deterministic signal card" and an "LLM recommendation card" competed as two recommendation surfaces while decision/scoring/history/committee were spread across four tabs with no clear "what do I look at now". A static mockup was built, reviewed live in-browser, and approved before implementation. Now: one unified `진출방식 추천` card (rule summary + AI narrative draft, both labeled `제안`, boundary #2 copy) replaces the two competing cards; tabs reduced to `결정` → `경쟁 구조` → `평가 근거` → `위원회 심의` with decision history moved into a <details> inside the `결정` tab; the scoring tab's judgment-rule modal is replaced by an inline <details> reference block; competitive structure is shown unconditionally instead of behind a collapsed scaffold.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **`/deals` replaces the old KPI-chart dashboard as the app's landing page.** The old `/` dashboard (two abstract aggregate bar charts) duplicated information already on the deals board without any of its actionable detail (clickable deal cards, real HOLD/aging state). `/` is now a server-side redirect to `/deals` (same pattern as the existing `/pipeline` stub, ADR-0120) so old bookmarks/links keep working; the sidebar nav entry and brand-logo link now point at `/deals` directly. `components/PipelineStatus.tsx` is now orphaned pending a follow-up cleanup pass.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **S4 flow-order, terminology, and S4/S3 duplication cleanups.** The standalone strategic-fit verdict (`fit_verdict`) is removed as a user-facing concept (ADR-0134, no migration — columns left inert): it duplicated the `screening 진행 권고` Go/Hold/Kill shape without its own screen and had never been wired to any UI; `gateRecommendation()` now combines only attractiveness+synergy (same thresholds minus the fit clause), the orphaned `POST /screening {kind:"fit"}` handler is gone, and context builders no longer cite it. S4 tabs reordered to `경쟁 구조` → `평가 근거` → `위원회 심의` → `결정` (default first tab = `경쟁 구조`) so evidence precedes decision; the decision tab's fact grid drops the redundant `시너지 권고` card and gives `발굴 근거` a 1:3 width share; a stage-inverted re-review trigger (`brownfield 권고이나 쇼트리스트가 비어 있습니다`) that fired on every brownfield recommendation was removed outright; the `평가 근거` tab gained an inline intent selector with a warning because `route_decision_score.intent` NOT NULL made the server silently discard scores saved without one.
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **S4 synergy-rationale contamination and readability fixes.** The S3 confirm editor no longer prefills its `확정 근거` field with the pipeline's raw calculation dump (`규칙 기반 산출(69×0.35 + …) LLM 보정 +3p`) for unconfirmed recommendations — clicking `확정 저장` used to record the AI's working notes as if a human had authored them; the pipeline rationale itself is now qualitative narrative (no arithmetic notation), unconfirmed records display it labeled `AI 산출 근거 (초안 — 계산 과정 그대로)`, and an explicit `AI 초안 불러오기` button pulls it in only on demand. The /synergy list table fixed three column-width bugs (company name collapsed to 0px, long opportunity names and rerun-status chips wrapping) and the S4 `/deals` board disambiguated HOLD from natural next-stage waiting (`진행 대기` filter renamed `HOLD`; only real gate-HOLDs stay amber, `S4 대기` renders gray).
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **`/deals` `진행 에이징` table scoped and labeled honestly.** The table collects HOLD/KILL decisions from all seven gates across both entities but sat under the S1–S7/S8–S12 funnel columns with no explanation; a clarifying line states it is not scoped to either funnel group, the `진행` column is renamed `게이트` and renders via the shared `GATE_LABEL` map (`screening` → `Screening` matching the stage rail), and the stage-rail gate chips gained a tooltip explaining that one gate (e.g. Screening) is shared across S1–S4 rather than being a per-stage review.
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **S4 advisory's shortlist signal was stage-inverted — an unconditional greenfield vote for every opportunity.** "`결합 후보 없음` → greenfield ×2" read `brownfield_target` (the S6/S7 shortlist), which is downstream state of the very track S4 decides: at first-pass S4 the shortlist is structurally always empty, so the absence branch cast a constant pro-greenfield vote unrelated to any evidence (and the presence branch leaked post-S4 state into the gate). Removed from the vote (the count still feeds the forward-looking B3 next-step trigger); the S4 rules-explainer rows are gone; `adr-0104.test.ts` regression-asserts the key never appears in signals.
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **List tables standardized — one `.list-table` implementation for all row-action tables (design-lint enforced).** The S1 `산업 목록` and S2 `기회 퍼널` lists rendered the same data differently, and a survey of all 7 row-action tables found each improvising: the unconstrained label column absorbed all leftover width (pushing scores/actions to the far edge), action columns had missing, empty (`sr-only` `관리`), or inconsistent (`액션` + inline right-align) headers, and 6rem date columns folded `2026-09-14` onto two lines. Standard (docs/design-system/design-components.md EN+KO): `.list-table` = fixed layout capped at 64rem with explicit widths on every non-label column, long status chips wrap instead of overflowing, dates use the new `col-date` class (7rem + nowrap), and every column is headed in the accessibility tree — the actions header is `sr-only` `작업` (visible label removed per readability report). Applied to all 7 tables; design-lint v1.2.0 fails any row-action table without the class or the actions header; market `분석 상태` widened 13→15rem so the longest rerun-progress chip fits on one line (2026-09-15).
- **[2026-09-15]**: fix(web-next): **S1 report's `바텀업` vs `탑다운` CAGR bars were clipped to full width whenever CAGR exceeded 8%.** `ConsistencyChart` hardcoded the x-axis maximum at 8 — a seed-era value — so a 22%/12% analysis rendered both bars overflowing the 0–8% axis with labels contradicting the scale. The axis ceiling is now derived from the plotted CAGRs (max × 1.1 rounded up to a 5% tick step, floor 8); the two `max: 100` composition charts are legitimate and unchanged.
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **`/deals` board silently dropped every HOLD opportunity and showed duplicate cards for promoted deals.** `GET /api/portfolio/pipeline`'s visibility filter (listGateAging rows) matched only project-carrier rows against visible projects, so every opportunity-carrier HOLD/KILL row — including a live `데이터센터·무정전 전원용 고정식 연료전지` HOLD — was filtered out before it ever reached the board, and the same bug meant `유압식 브레이커` showed both an opportunity card and a project card simultaneously once promoted (project-visibility check ran on presence of project_id rather than actual project visibility). Also fixed a same-day-only aging filter that made the board's `진행 에이징` table contradict its own HOLD badges (aging field dropped entirely in favor of one currentHolds list, minAgeDays: 0, sorted by real elapsed days).
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **Archiving a project left its origin opportunity permanently stuck mid-flow with no path forward.** `휴머노이드 로봇 사업 진출`'s project was correctly archived, but the linked opportunity kept `status="selected"` and a stale `project_id`, so it rendered on the deals board as `발굴/평가 단계 필요` while actually being done — neither cleaned up nor left usable for a future attempt. Retroactively corrected via a traced, audited data fix (root cause: archival predates a cascade rule that doesn't exist yet for this path); the underlying gap is left for a follow-up ADR rather than papered over here.
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **Protected re-run of an already-completed S1 market-sizing or S3 synergy analysis gave no progress feedback and left every mutating button clickable mid-run.** `rerun_in_progress` (ADR-0131/0132) intentionally leaves `status`/completed_at pointed at the last good version so "latest completed" queries never regress — but the UI read only status, so a rerun looked identical to steady-state `완료` with no step indicator, and `재분석/수정/삭제` stayed enabled, letting a second rerun or a delete race the first. Both /market and /synergy now derive busy = inProgress || rerun_in_progress, show `재분석 중` (N/8`단계, 이전` v{n} `유지`) / (N/5`단계`), gate all three buttons on busy, and the delete/rerun API routes reject with 409 analysis_running while a rerun is in flight (synergy's delete route had no such guard at all before this).
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **Redundant `열기` buttons and a dead-end single-connection click target on S1/S3.** /market's industry rows already opened detail via the industry-name click — the adjacent `열기` button was a pure duplicate, removed. /synergy's opportunity-linkage cell required clicking the *company* name when a synergy area had exactly one connected opportunity (any other count required the separate `열기` button instead) — the opportunity cell is now unconditionally the click target (company name is always plain text) and `열기` is removed as now-redundant.
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **GateDecisionPanel's `인간 승인 기록` heading dropped the redundant `인간`.** Cosmetic copy fix across all six consumers (/greenfield, `/synergy`, `/brownfield`, and three `/projects/[id]/*` gate screens) — now reads `의사결정` — `승인 기록 (경계` #2).
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **S2 opportunity list's `매력도 평가/선별/제외` row-shortcut buttons bypassed real evaluation and were mostly non-functional.** The `발굴` row's `매력도 평가` button flipped status straight to `평가` with no review step, silently rubber-stamping an AI auto-discovery draft score as a completed human assessment; `선별/제외` required `synergy_score` + fit_verdict, which in practice are only ever set via /synergy's own, more complete S4`로 진행` promotion action, so they sat disabled for most rows. All three removed; every row now shows a single `비즈니스 모델 검토` link (deep-links to the opportunity detail page's matching tab via ?tab=sketch) plus `삭제`. An AI-drafted (not yet human-reviewed) attractiveness score is now labeled `제안` in the list. `제외`(dropped) status is retained for existing historical rows but is no longer creatable from the UI — `삭제` is now the only decline path, per user decision (soft-decline history judged not worth keeping as a separate action from hard delete). Also renamed the `다음 단계` column header to `작업` (no longer a funnel-progression column) and promoted the `기회 발굴 (요인 자동 도출`) button from btn-ghost to btn-primary — it is this screen's actual primary action but was barely visible.
### Added
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S3 capability-gap register — market requirements vs group/affiliate held capabilities.** The "required capability list derived in S1/S3" referenced by S5/S6 inputs and chk-target-screening t-05 existed nowhere as data. Migration 0098 adds `opportunity.capability_gap_json` (resolution vocabulary `build|partner|hire|invest` matches S5's `capability_gaps_json` so the register hands off); `GET/PUT /api/opportunities/{id}/capability-gap` (GET seeds in-memory rows from the S2 sketch's legacy requiredCapabilities; PUT is the human save, audited) and `POST .../capability-gap/llm-draft` (drafts a held-status per row from group/affiliate profile strengths·industryExposure — proposal only, saving stays human). The opportunity detail page gains a `역량 갭` tab (status/holders/evidence/resolution editing, LLM draft, add/remove, coverage summary). Every surface carries the caveat that the held side is group/affiliate-profile-based and the internal (standalone) capability assessment is a documented follow-up.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S4 route decision now grounds the track advisory in competitive structure and capability coverage (flow.spec v2.4.0).** S4's declared inputs covered only the synergy/fit assessment — the S1 outputs it implicitly consumed were undeclared, S1 never required a competitive artifact, and the deterministic advisory (ADR-0104) voted on growth alone (high CAGR ⇒ greenfield, even in a locked-up oligopoly). Now: S1 `경쟁 구조` becomes a required artifact with a soft advisory blocker (7 of 14 live completed analyses predate the competitive record — completion is preserved, re-analysis is nudged); S3 outputs declare `필요 역량 목록·역량 갭 분석`; S4 inputs declare the S1 outputs + the capability gap. The advisory gains `competitive_structure` (concentrated market → brownfield ×2; fragmented votes nothing so growth alone cannot drag the recommendation) and `capability_coverage` (≥70% → greenfield, ≤40% → brownfield, group/affiliate basis stated) plus a competitive-record re-review trigger. `GET route-advisory` returns `s1Evidence` (industry, version, market size, CAGR, competitive structure + players) and a capability-gap summary, and the S4 decision tab renders a market/competitive/capability evidence card — per-signal rationale trail, expandable competitive structure table, gap summary with empty-state links. `screening`/`pre_fs` gate briefs gain a competitive line and an S3 `역량 갭 요약` block so committee deliberation sees the same evidence.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S7 shortlisting funnel — Long List → Short List narrowing is now visible on `/brownfield`.** The S7 screen already had the machinery (knock-out checklist, promote/exclude APIs, committee, target_screening gate) but nothing showed the *process*: one flat table mixed pending/shortlisted/excluded rows, progress was a bare `판정` 3/9, promotion hid behind row expansion, and flow.spec S7's Target Ranking/Score output and "every candidate has a score or an exclusion reason" completion criterion had no UI surface at all. The screen now leads with a selection funnel (Long List N → `판정 완료` K → `승격 가능` J → Short List S · `제외` X) plus a remaining-work line; each row renders its t-01~t-09 verdicts as nine chips (`충/미/예`/N/·) with per-item tooltips; a deterministic selection score (waiver-eligible items: pass/na 1, waived 0.5, fail 0; gates t-01~t-02 stay promotion conditions, not scored) shows as a percentage once fully judged; promotion-ready rows promote inline without expanding; Short List rows get ↑/↓ rank controls backed by a new POST /api/brownfield/{oppId}/targets/{targetId}/rerank endpoint (neighbor swap, ranks re-normalized 1..N, audited brownfield_target.rerank); and status filter chips (`전체/심사 중`/Short List/`제외`) narrow the table.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S1 pinning reaches linked industries, and expanded groups read as hierarchy.** The ★ pin button rendered only on root (anchor) industries, so a linked industry like `유압식 브레이커` could not be favorited at all (live user report — the same-day pinning design had assumed "pin state only makes sense for something that can appear at root", which real usage disproved). Stars now render on every row (linked rows keep their ↳ + relation badge after the star); ★ `핵심만 보기` promotes a pinned linked industry to its own top-level row when its anchor isn't pinned (a pinned anchor still brings its whole group along, unchanged); child-row indent deepened 1rem → 2rem so expanded groups no longer read as a flush same-level list. Supersedes part of the same-day pinning design.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S1 industry list explicit curation — pin industries, `핵심만 보기` toggle.** As linked-industry grouping catches more cases, the root-level list itself keeps growing. `md_industry` gains a `pinned` column (migration 0097); a ★/☆ toggle on each root row (`PATCH /api/market-overview/industry/{id}` with {pinned}, handled separately from the existing name/KSIC edit path) lets users mark their actual focus industries. A new `전체 보기`/★ `핵심만 보기` chip filters the root-level list to only pinned industries when active — their linked-industry groups still come along underneath regardless of the children's own pinned state. Defaults to showing everything (pinnedOnly=false) so the list isn't empty before anyone has pinned anything.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S1 industry list grouping now also reads directly-registered `가치사슬 연계` links, and groups collapse by default.** The previous grouping only read market_sizing_analysis.after_analysis_id/linked_relation (set when an industry is queued via the linked-industry serial queue, ADR-0130) — a pair of already-analyzed industries linked afterward via the `가치사슬 연계` registration form (proxy_link.relation, ADR-0129) never appeared grouped, as reported live for `연료전지/수소 충전소 구축 서비스업`. GET /api/market-overview now also returns active, relation-tagged proxy_link edges, and the frontend merges both sources (`전방/전전방` → from_industry_id is the anchor, `후방/후후방` → to_industry_id is the anchor, per the existing chain-links registration convention). Groups with linked industries now render collapsed by default with a `▸`/`연계` N`건` toggle, so the list stays scannable as it grows — expanding shows the same indented, relation-badged rows as before.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S1 industry list groups linked industries under their anchor.** With 21+ industries in the list, `전방/전전방/후방/후후방` (forward/2nd-forward/backward/2nd-backward) linked industries were indistinguishable from independently-registered ones — all rendered as flat, equal rows. The list now nests each linked industry directly under its anchor (indented, with a colored relation badge — blue for demand-side `전방/전전방`, amber for supply-side `후방/후후방`), sorted forward-before-backward within the group. No schema change — this reuses `market_sizing_analysis.after_analysis_id`/`linked_relation`, already recorded when an industry is registered via the linked-industry queue (ADR-0130); purely a rendering change in `market/page.tsx`.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **S1 industry deletion no longer permanently blocked by retired or invisible references.** Deleting an industry relied on the FK constraint to refuse with a generic 409, but the UI's link "removal" is a soft retire (ADR-0129) — 9 retired proxy_link rows still referenced `유압식 브레이커`; one active ADR-0126-era relation IS NULL legacy row never appears in the removable relation list; and the project-domain references (`project_industry` binding, project `value_chain_link` edge) have no removal API anywhere, making the block a permanent dead end. `DELETE /api/market-overview/industry/{id}` now cascades every direct `md_industry` reference inside its transaction (`proxy_link` (retired/active/legacy alike), `value_chain_link`, `project_industry` with `superseded_by` self-reference cleanup, `proxy_chain`, `fact_industry`, `chain_node_sensitivity`, `structure_simulation_result`, `pipeline_run`), appends an audited `industry_delete` entry with per-table row counts, and keeps promoted opportunities' history protected. Verified on a `.backup` copy with `PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON` (live DB untouched); the delete confirm dialog now states the real scope.
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **Dev-server restarts left S1 market-sizing analyses stuck at `분석 중` forever.** `runSizingPipeline` runs as an in-process fire-and-forget promise; killing/restarting the dev server mid-run abandons it with no process left to finish it, and the existing recovery mechanism (`recoverQueuePass`) only runs via an OS-scheduled `watch:run` cycle that local dev never runs — so the row stayed in_progress indefinitely with no automatic path to `실패`. instrumentation.ts now calls recoverQueuePass once on every server boot, so a restart-orphaned analysis surfaces as `분석 실패` (with a retry path) instead of hanging forever. Root-caused live: 4 analyses orphaned by a dev-server restart mid-session were confirmed via llm_call_log (all activity stopped at the exact restart timestamp) and manually recovered the same way this fix now automates.
### Added
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **Six S8-S12 navigation/data fixes.** (1) Sidebar's dynamic S8-S12 submenu (AppShell.tsx projectStageChildren) was missing `타임라인`·SLA/`리스크 히트맵` even after they were added to ProjectSubNav — they're two different menus; added both. (2) Removed the dashboard's `포트폴리오 전체 뷰` button per request (note: /portfolio-view has no other UI entry point now, reachable only by direct URL). (3) /projects list: project names are now links to the detail screen, matching the row's existing link style. (4) WACC and `재무분석` folded into S8 business-case's tab set (?tab=wacc/?tab=financial-analysis) instead of being separate S8 nav entries — extracted into shared `components/wacc/WaccPanel.tsx` and `components/FinancialAnalysisPanel.tsx`; the standalone `/wacc`//financial-analysis routes are kept alive as thin wrapper pages rendering the same panels, for deep-link compatibility. (6) Site-selection tools (`부지·시설, 인허가·인증, 인센티브`) moved out of the generic `도구` catch-all into their own `입지·인허가` group in ProjectSubNav, since no pipeline stage owns site selection.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **S9 `가치사슬 연계` inherited nothing from S1's curated industry linkage.** `GET /api/projects/[id]/proxy-chain` only ever queried `value_chain_link` rows the analyst re-created from scratch inside S9, never joining through `md_project.source_opportunity_id → opportunity.market_scope → md_industry` to pull in the `proxy_link`/`proxy_chain` rows S1 already curated for the project's origin industry (ADR-0126/0129). Now resolves the origin industry once and unions it into both queries — verified live: a project with zero S9-authored links now correctly shows 4 inherited `proxy_link` rows from its S1 industry.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **Timeline·SLA and risk-heatmap screens registered in the project sub-nav.** `timeline/page.tsx` (ADR-0122 #1) and `risk-heatmap/page.tsx` (ADR-0122 #2) were both fully implemented already but missing from `ProjectSubNav`'s `SUB_PAGES` list, so they were reachable only by typing the URL directly — invisible in the menu. Both added to the `tools` group.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-14]**: fix(web-next): **`GET /api/projects/[id]/timeline` 500'd on every call.** Two queries referenced nonexistent columns: `opportunity.updated_at` (opportunity has no `updated_at` column at all — the S3 milestone now reads `synergy_analysis.completed_at` for the linked opportunity, the actual source of a synergy-completion timestamp) and `deal_structure_hypothesis.created_at` (the real column is `hypothesized_at`). Found while registering the timeline screen in the nav (previously unreachable from the UI, so the bug had never surfaced).
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S10 deal-structure screen split into in-page tabs (Phase 3 continued).** `deal-structure/page.tsx` packed the LLM structure recommendation, investment-structure hypothesis registration, and sources-&-uses financing plan into one scroll. Split via TabNav into LLM `추천` (viewer-role hidden, matching the section's existing canAct gate), `투자구조 가설` (count-badged, default-active), and `자금조달 계획`. Active tab syncs to ?tab= the same way as the business-case split.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S8 business-case screen split into in-page tabs (Phase 3 of the tab-readability plan).** business-case/page.tsx packed an explainer table, generation controls, run log, LLM adoption recommendation, and three scenario comparison tables into one continuous 450-line scroll. Now split via the shared TabNav into `설명·생성·권고` (explainer + generate + LLM recommendation) and `시나리오 비교` (the three scenario tables, default-active since it's the main content) — the error banner and editing/gate-decision panels stay outside the tabs since they're triggered from within either tab's content. Active tab syncs to a ?tab= URL query param (wrapped in Suspense per Next.js App Router requirements) so it survives refresh and is deep-linkable.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(web-next): **S8-S12 project sub-nav grouped by stage (Phase 1+2 of the tab-readability plan).** ProjectSubNav's 23-item flat link list was one undifferentiated row with no indication of which of the five project stages (S8-S12) each link belonged to. Each entry now carries a group field (local to ProjectSubNav.tsx, not added to lib/stage-meta.ts's flow-spec-conformance-tested shape) and renders under a small stage heading (S8 · `사업성 분석`, ..., plus a `도구` heading for non-stage utility tabs) — no routes or links changed, purely a rendering grouping. Also adds a shared TabNav component (components/TabNav.tsx) formalizing the .tab-nav pattern named in docs/design-system/design-guide.md's Component Standard table (previously hand-rolled once, only in `dd/page.tsx`), with keyboard arrow-key navigation and an optional count badge; `dd/page.tsx`'s DD-checklist domain tabs are migrated onto it as the first consumer. Reviewed by ux-designer-agent, design-system-agent, and frontend-engineer before implementation — their finding that `StageFrame`'s existing S1-S12 `StageRail` (ADR-0086) already solves stage wayfinding on 10 of these pages avoided building a duplicate second stage-rail component.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(s1-market): **S1 industry list surfaces synthesis-degraded reports, not just the report detail view.** `GET /api/market-overview` now classifies each industry's latest completed version as `all_synthesis`/`partial_synthesis`/clean by inspecting `stepSummaries[].source`, and the list's status chip shows a distinct red `분석 완료` — `합성 데이터` or amber `분석 완료` — `일부 합성` chip instead of the same green `분석 완료` as a genuinely LLM-derived report. Closes the gap where a partially-degraded report (e.g. `건설·광산 중장비 제조 산업`, steps 1-6 genuine but 7-8 synthesis fallback) showed no indication of a problem anywhere in the list screen, even though the report detail view's ADR-0131 warning banner already flagged it.
- **[2026-09-14]**: feat(app-wide): **Extend "LLM failure preserves prior data" beyond S1 market sizing (ADR-0132).** Applies ADR-0131's exact protection — a *genuine* LLM failure (provider configured, call still failed) must not silently overwrite already-good data, and the user must be told — to S2 auto-derived opportunity factors, S2 fit-screening verdicts (new `fit_source` column), S2 business-model sketch regeneration, and S3 synergy analysis reruns (new `rerun_in_progress`/`error_message` columns on `synergy_analysis`, migration 0096, mirroring ADR-0131's `synergy-pipeline.ts` `askJsonTracked` mechanism). The group/affiliate company-profile pipeline reuses its existing versioned `degraded` field and job-status mechanism instead of new schema. Compute-and-return recommendation/gate-brief endpoints already reported `source: "llm"|"synthesis"` and needed no persistence-layer change.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Re-analysis no longer silently overwrites a good prior version on LLM failure (ADR-0131).** A new `rerun_in_progress` column (migration 0095) tracks an in-flight rerun of an already-completed analysis independently of `status`/`completed_at`, so every existing "latest completed version" query keeps serving the prior good version for the whole duration of the run. `market-sizing-pipeline.ts` now distinguishes a genuine mid-run LLM failure (the provider was configured but a call still errored) from the pre-existing, accepted not-configured synthesis mode, and skips the version swap entirely on a genuine failure — the prior version and its completion date are left untouched, and the analysis is surfaced with a "rerun failed, prior version kept" chip instead of quietly advancing. The S1 report also gains an explicit red warning banner when any step's output came from the deterministic fallback rather than the real LLM, instead of silently omitting the positive "all steps used LLM" note as before.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s1-market): **CAGR displayed/consumed at 100x its true value in six places.** `market-sizing-pipeline.ts` persists `matrix.consistency.bottomUpCagr` already scaled to a percent number (e.g. `36` means 36%, not the fraction `0.36`), but six downstream consumers multiplied it by 100 again as if it were still a fraction — the S1 report's "growth rate" summary card (root-reported bug, showing -10000.0% instead of -100.0%), the attractiveness/route/greenfield LLM-recommendation prompt builders, `gate-brief.ts`'s gate summary text, and `track-advisory.ts`'s greenfield/brownfield market-growth signal (whose `cagr >= 0.15` threshold was also comparing a percent-scale number against a fraction-scale constant, so it was effectively always true for any positive growth rate — corrected to `>= 15`). Updated the one test fixture (`adr-0104.test.ts`) that seeded the old fraction-scale value to match the actual pipeline output format.
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s1-market): **Retired demand-industry links permanently blocked re-registration.** The "already linked" checks in both the proposal/manual-registration list (`existingLinkedIds`) and the chain-links create endpoint's duplicate guard matched `proxy_link` rows regardless of `status`, so once a link between two industries was retired (e.g. corrected via the ADR-0129/0130 retire+re-register contract, or manually retired by a reviewer) that industry pair could never be re-registered — it silently disappeared from both the approval-proposal list and the manual dropdown, and a direct registration attempt failed with `already_linked`. Both queries now require `status = 'active'`, so a retired link only withdraws that specific relation claim and no longer blocks a corrected or renewed one.
### Added
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Linked-industry serial analysis queue (ADR-0130).** Selected linked industries no longer spawn in parallel with the target analysis — they are created as `pending` rows anchored via after_analysis_id (migration 0094: status CHECK gains 'pending', partial unique index enforces one active analysis per industry, queue index) and start ONE AT A TIME after the anchor completes, demand-side (forward/second-forward) first. A failed anchor cancels its pending group (audited queue_cancel) while an individual linked failure lets the queue continue; watch:run gains a recovery pass (runMarketSizingQueuePass) that heals stuck runs and claims ready pendings so the queue never depends on anyone opening the UI. Rerunning a root cancels leftover pendings (`루트 재분석으로 대기 취소`) — no zombie generations. Queue state is audited (queue_claim/queue_cancel/queue_recover with system actor market-sizing-queue).
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(skills): **Committee deliberation split from the common meeting skill.** The engine-backed co-newbiz `meeting-facilitation` skill is renamed `committee-deliberation` (v1.6.0) so the common `meeting-facilitation` (v1.4.1, adopted from templates/common) keeps its name across template upgrades — no more name collision between the collaborative `/meeting` flow and the gate-review deliberation CLI. The deliberation engine itself (`runDeliberationRounds`/`buildSynthesis`) is split out of `agent-orchestrator.ts` into the dedicated `app/web-next/lib/deliberation.ts`, and the committee CLI is renamed `scripts/co-newbiz/committee-deliberation.ts` (transcripts now `memory/committee-*.md`).
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s1-market): **Hydraulic-breaker link mislabeled forward + operator-perspective proposal prompt (ADR-0130).** The live proxy_link row for `유압식 브레이커` → `건설기계(굴삭기·스키드로더) 장비 운용` carried relation='`전방`' with placeholder evidence ('.') — from the equipment-operator perspective the breaker is a procured attachment, i.e. `후방`. Corrected per the ADR-0129 retire+re-register contract (scripts/co-newbiz/fix-construction-machinery-link.ts, audited, idempotent, with a placeholder-evidence sweep). The first-pass proposal prompt now states the operator-perspective judgment rule explicitly (procure=`후방`, demand=`전방`, actor-type injected from the industry name) and the new proposal sanitizer enforces the stored relation taxonomy while routing `대체/보완` findings to a visible `대체 위협 (검토 필요`) surface instead of silently dropping them (6-axis linkage territory per ADR-0025).
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s1-market): **First analysis of a new industry no longer invites parallel linked spend.** The new-analysis candidate modal is preview-only (read-only proposals, `이 산업만 분석`); linked industries are added post-completion via the report's `연계 산업 이어서 분석` CTA or the re-analysis modal (selection cap 5), queue behind the anchor as `연계 대기` · N`번째`, and the list polls pending rows so queue progress is visible. The proposal chip is relabeled `제안 (검증 전`) and a missing-LLM-role 403 gets its own message instead of the misleading "no candidates".
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(docs): **OpenAPI drift gate failing on two undocumented routes.** `GET /api/market-overview/value-chain-graph` and `PATCH /api/market-overview/industry/{industryId}/chain-links/{linkId}` (added with ADR-0126/0129) were missing from `docs/architecture/openapi.yaml`; both registered, drift gate green.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Human-curated demand-industry linkage (ADR-0129).** `proxy_link` (migration 0093) gains `relation` (forward/2nd-forward/backward/2nd-backward), `hop_distance`, `source` (llm_proposed/user_added/approved), and `status` (active/retired) columns, extending ADR-0126's structural hop into a first-class, human-curatable demand-industry edge. Legacy untagged rows surface in a dedicated review queue instead of being silently treated as confirmed. The market-sizing pipeline now derives segments and pulls anchor/CAGR evidence from these curated forward/2nd-forward industries instead of free LLM invention or fragile `name_ko` string-matching, and feeds the same curated set into the demand-pipeline, qualitative, and regulatory-comparison steps. Chain links can now be retired (soft-delete with rationale) via a new `PATCH .../chain-links/{linkId}` endpoint — closing a prior gap where structural links could only be created, never removed.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Industry-to-industry value-chain relationship diagram.** New `GET /api/market-overview/value-chain-graph` aggregates all active, relation-tagged `proxy_link` edges workspace-wide and Kahn-layers them (cycle-tolerant — stray cycles are dumped into a trailing layer instead of throwing) into forward/backward columns. Rendered as an SVG diagram in a modal on the S1 industry-list screen, opened via a button placed beside the screen's intro text; analyzed industries are highlighted.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s1-market): **Legacy (unreviewed) demand-industry links were silently treated as confirmed.** `related-candidates` and the S1 re-analysis modal's "already-registered — locked" logic checked only `proxy_link.status = 'active'`, not whether `relation` had been human-reviewed — so a pre-ADR-0129 structural link with no relation tag (e.g. a stale industry pairing) was force-included in every re-analysis and could never be deselected. Added `AND relation IS NOT NULL` to the candidate query; unreviewed links now stay confined to the legacy-review queue until a human tags them.
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(admin): **Data-source "key configured" status always showed unset.** `/admin/data-sources` derived its env-var name as `CO_NEWBIZ_<CODE>_API_KEY`, which does not match any of this project's actual credential env vars (`DART_API_KEY`, `KOSIS_API_KEY`, etc., per `scripts/co-newbiz/source-adapters.ts`). Now reuses each adapter's real `credentialEnv` instead of reconstructing the name.
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(ui): **Stage handoff strip ("input" / "output" labels) rendered as unstyled run-on text on every S1–S12 screen.** `StageFrame`'s `.handoff`/`.handoff-chip`/`.handoff-hl`/`.handoff-out`/`.handoff-cta` classes had no CSS definitions anywhere. Added styles (chip pills in a tinted banner, accent CTA button) using existing design tokens — fixes the strip workspace-wide since `StageFrame` is shared by every stage screen.
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(build): **Production build (`next build`) failed on an unrelated pre-existing TS error.** `scripts/lib/ssrf.ts` carried two `@ts-expect-error` directives that Node's current `LookupFunction` type overloads no longer need, which `tsc` flags as an error ("Unused '@ts-expect-error' directive"). Removed the stale directives; `bun run build` completes again.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-13]**: refactor(s2-opportunities): **Detail-screen tabs + decoupled scenario regeneration.** The opportunity detail screen's opportunity/risk-factors and business-model-review sections are now tabs instead of always-stacked (funnel/list-view iteration also reverted from a kanban board back to the original flat table per follow-up feedback). Scenario premises (optimistic/base/pessimistic) moved out of the business-model-review tab into the factors tab, and regenerating the business-model canvas no longer overwrites them (and vice versa) — a dedicated scenario-regenerate button next to the scenario heading regenerates only the scenarios, preserving the canvas/market-entry/key-risks, and surfaces a reminder to review factors afterward instead of auto-triggering factor re-derivation.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(nav): **Topbar back button stuck on query-param detail screens.** Clicking the topbar "back to list" button from `/market?industry=`, `/synergy?company=`, `/route-decision?opportunity=`, `/brownfield?opportunity=`, or `/greenfield?opportunity=` silently failed to return to the list — traced to a Next.js App Router internal behavior where `router.push`/`replace` to the same pathname with cleared search params gets its own `history.replaceState` call immediately reverted by the router's cache reconciliation back to the prior full URL. Fixed by falling back to a hard navigation (`window.location.assign`) specifically when the target href equals the current pathname and search params are present; cross-path back navigation (e.g. deal-stage → deal-overview) is unaffected and still uses soft navigation.
### Added
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Demand-side grounding for anchor/CAGR estimation + segment ceiling raise.** The market-sizing pipeline now reads completed analyses of the linked downstream (forward/second-forward) industries and injects their size/growth as demand-side evidence into the Step 2 (anchor) and Step 4 (CAGR) prompts, aligning quantitative estimation with the proxy-chain logic that downstream demand drives upstream revenue. Segment ceiling raised from 4 to 6 (linked-driver cap 24 → 36) to address the fixed-count constraint; `askJson` token budget raised 2000 → 3200 to accommodate the larger prompts. The growth-rate tab now flags a >=20% gap between our estimate and the external forecast with a one-click re-analysis action. First-pass proxy analysis (S1 scope modal) token budget raised 1200 → 2200 with tolerant partial-JSON parsing after a real truncation was caught via the new LLM call ledger.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(agents): **Runtime PM-agent orchestration (ADR-0128).** Every LLM call is now ledgered in the append-only `llm_call_log` (migration 0092) with an agent key, purpose, source (llm/synthesis), provider/model, and latency — 15 direct call sites stamped. A runtime PM router (`lib/pm-router.ts`) classifies natural-language queries (LLM with deterministic-rules fallback) and dispatches either a single roster agent (system prompts projected from `agents/*.md` via the new generated registry) or the `opportunity_evaluation` chain (factors → attractiveness → fit → PM synthesis). New `/agents` surface with roster browser, `/api/agents/query` + `/api/agents/registry` endpoints. Outputs remain proposal-only — decisions stay on the human gate UIs.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s1-market): **Analysis-scope modal with first-pass proxy industry analysis.** Both analysis start and re-analysis now open a value-chain linkage industry-selection modal: stored candidates (approved links + past payload chain) or an on-demand first-pass proxy analysis proposes related industries with linkage evidence; selected industries run analyses together and unregistered ones auto-register. KSIC master expanded from a 13-entry curated seed to the full official KSIC 10th-revision classification (2,000 codes) with profile entries prioritized; search result selection no longer sticks after cancel.
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s2-opportunities): **Opportunity detail screen + business-model review history (ADR-0127).** Opportunity rows become a lightweight funnel (attractiveness-evaluate, select, drop, business-model review, delete only); the name navigates to `/opportunities/{id}` with factors editing, attractiveness evaluation, business-model review with append-only version history (`opportunity_sketch_version`, migration 0091), and the promotion panel. S3 synergy/fit runs moved off S2 — the detail screen links to the S3 surface, and inline synergy scoring is rejected (`synergy_requires_s3`). "sketch" UI label renamed to "business-model review".
### Changed
- **[2026-09-13]**: refactor(s3-synergy): **Value-chain linkage rename + company-grouped list.** "proxy chain" UI naming unified to "value-chain linkage" (market page section, project subnav, committee stance copy, pipeline pendingInputs); technical identifiers unchanged. The synergy analysis list groups analyses by company (rowSpan bundle) with opportunities listed per row as the detail entry points; DART corp-code column replaced by a target-type column; S3-only synergy gate enforced by clearing synergy scores on opportunities with no linked completed analysis.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(s3-synergy): **Human curation layer for synergy screens (ADR-0125).** The S3 market-linkage tab gains a human add-area form and per-row origin badges (LLM-proposed / human-added); the priority-synergy-area tab gains inline verdict/reason editing plus new-area creation, with three-state origin badges (LLM-proposed / LLM-proposed-human-edited / human-added). Backed by two new tables (`synergy_linkage_item`, `synergy_shortlist_curation`) overlaid on the immutable append-only `synergy_version` payload at GET time, and two new analyst+ endpoints (`POST .../linkage-items`, `PUT .../shortlist-items`) registered in the OpenAPI spec. The GateDecisionPanel on the shortlist tab now explains that recording GO moves to S4 and offers a direct link (absorbing the removed S4-proceed button).
### Changed
- **[2026-09-13]**: feat(process): **S7 stage renamed from target screening to target selection** across flow.spec.yaml (governance SSOT, docs/process-flow.md regenerated), stage-meta, process-flow, stage-handoff, gate-brief, track-advisory, PromotionPanel, and stage screen copy. Technical identifiers (`target_screening`, `chk-target-screening`) unchanged.
- **[2026-09-13]**: fix(s4): **S4 picker promotion split now keys on project_id, not deleted_at.** The opportunity picker's active/promoted grouping and its year/month filter (now decided_at-based) correctly treat promotion (project_id) and archival (deleted_at) as independent lifecycle signals; the single-opportunity S4 screen shows separate promoted/archived banners. Fixed a data inconsistency where a deleted project still held opportunity.project_id without any route_decision.
- **[2026-09-13]**: refactor(gates): **GateDecisionPanel single-gate dedup + decision button rename.** Single-gate panels (all current call sites) render a compact one-line status instead of a gate/decision/rationale table duplicating the form labels below; the redundant gate selector is hidden; the submit button reads "record decision". New optional `onRecorded` callback lets surfaces react to recorded decisions (S3 uses it to absorb the removed S4-proceed handoff).
### Changed
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(r1-s4): **S4 route-decision rebuilt to the v5 baseline tab structure (R1 first screen).** The 8-section single scroll is now 5 tabs — decision form (opportunity context, decision evidence, track/route selects, rationale, save) / recommendation (deterministic advisor + LLM recommendation) / scoring (Stage-1 intent + 5-criteria scoring) / decision history (decision table + committee sessions) / gates (GateDecisionPanel + steering committee). Tab sections stay mounted (display toggle) so form and scoring state survive tab switches; the old anchor-jump flow list is retired. Logic and API calls unchanged.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(restyle3): **Primary-action batch pass across stage surfaces (backlog execution — batch strategy).** The gate-record submit in GateDecisionPanel (8 stage surfaces at once), the business-case adoption action, and the portfolio transition-record submit are now btn-primary — completing the single-primary-action hierarchy on the remaining purpose screens. Combined with the earlier passes (chip conversion, linkage strips), every stage screen now carries the v5 visual vocabulary; the remaining legacy-surface work is the per-screen workload migration into /deals tabs (tracked in the implementation matrix), not visual restyle.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(restyle2): **Primary action hierarchy + control-chip semantics on legacy screens (restyle pass part 2, ADR-0119).** Chip-buttons that carried aria-pressed (filters, sorts, scorecard selectors) across nine legacy screens are now control-chip (visually distinct from read-only status chips). Each stage screen's purpose action is now the single btn-primary: S1 analysis start, S2 assess move / S4 assessment proceed (row-level), S4 decision save, S5 assessment save, S3  analysis . The opportunities toolbar gains a primary ＋ opportunity register toggle (registration moves to the toolbar per ADR-0082/0083).
### Changed
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(restyle): **Stage linkage strip on every stage screen (ADR-0120 restyle pass — the visual upgrade users were missing).** StageFrame now renders an input←output linkage strip on ALL 14+ stage surfaces automatically: the stage's inputs (from flow.spec inputs, presentation strings in `lib/stage-handoff.ts`) as chips, and an output→ next-stage CTA linking via the stage route map (project-context and track aware). This upgrades the legacy working screens (market/opportunities/synergy/route-decision/greenfield/brownfield + all project tabs) without per-page edits. styles.css adds the .handoff strip styles.
### Added
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(cycle3): **Covenant scenario compute view (final P3 backlog item).** The debt page gains a scenario panel: annual debt service is computed per instrument with the annuity formula from the persisted principal/rate/maturity (remaining term derived; unstated maturity shown as a 5-year assumption), optional +2%p rate stress, and DSCR = scenario operating cash flow / debt service compared against parsed covenant thresholds (both "DSCR >= 1.3" prose and JSON forms accepted). The scenario cash flow is an explicit user assumption — nothing invented; official covenant tests remain simulation-run-confirmed (covenant_test), with simulation integration noted on the backlog. Threshold display now parses both prose and JSON covenant notation.
### Added
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(cycle2): **Backlog P2 items — business-targets admin page + deal paused banners (quality-review cycle 2).** /admin/business-targets wires the previously unwired BusinessTargetsSection component (ADR-0114 standards, admin-only; ADMIN_NAV + route registered). /deals cards show a paused-pipeline banner (paused_reason + deep link context) fetched for selected/assessed opportunities. S1 staleness badge verified as already implemented (90-day "re-analysis recommended" chip). Decorative emojis stripped from six legacy screens' headings (design-system single-icon-vocabulary decision).
### Added
- **[2026-09-12]**: fix(wave5): **Quality-review revision cycle — P1 fixes from the all-agent review (M6).** Walked four journeys against the running site with weighted scoring (79/100). P1 fixes: (1) backtest membership gate — per-project rows member-visible only, non-members receive an anonymous org aggregate (counts + avg |deviation|%), closing a project-name exposure; (2) onboarding reachability — /deals board shows the 4-step guide CTA when the workspace is empty; (3) LLM proposal reliability score (ADR-0123 #2) — per-gate committee-PROPOSAL vs human-decision agreement surfaced in agent-runs (display-only); (4) target-proposal suggestions UI on the brownfield screen with discovery_source provenance. Re-scored projection ~84/100. Minutes: docs/designs/2026-09-12-quality-review-minutes.md.
### Changed
- **[2026-09-12]**: refactor(wave4): **/pipeline redirects to /deals — board consolidation sweep (construction plan Wave 4, ADR-0120).** The /pipeline screen (funnel + gate aging) was subsumed by the /deals board in Wave 1; the legacy route is now a server-side redirect so bookmarks never break, its nav entry is retired, and the redirect stub is registered. Legacy stage screens (/market, /opportunities, etc.) intentionally remain as working surfaces — the workload migration into /deals stage tabs is a separate wave and the screens are linked from the deal workspace and board.
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(wave3b): **Oversight surfaces — stage SLA timeline, risk heatmap, portfolio view, onboarding wizard (construction plan Wave 3 part 2, ADR-0122).** New `GET /api/projects/{id}/timeline` computes per-stage milestone dates from each stage's primary-artifact timestamps (never stored) against the admin-managed `stage_sla` policy (unset policy degrades honestly with an unset-policy marker); the timeline page renders plan-vs-elapsed bars with SLA-breach highlighting. Risk heatmap aggregates DD findings, customer concentration, and incentive clawback into one likelihood-x-impact grid from real records only (no invented values, ADR-0112). Portfolio view (/portfolio-view) detects cross-deal market duplication at the group level (Group IC judgment stays human). Onboarding wizard (4-step empty-workspace guide) implements the ux-designer-agent empty-state standard. All registered (NON_MENU_ROUTES + OpenAPI timeline entry).
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(wave3): **Transparency and learning surfaces — agent-run viewer, backtest, external safe-share (construction plan Wave 3 part 1, ADR-0123).** Project sub-nav grows to 24 tabs: agent history (agent_run/activity_run timeline + committee PROPOSAL records — read-only, proposals visually distinct from human records per boundary #2), backtest (org-wide forecast vs actual comparison with honest insufficient_evidence degradation and outlier highlighting for the learning loop), safe-share (expiring/watermarked/logged snapshot link management — UI note marks the security-engineer design sign-off pre-condition). ToastProvider mounted app-wide in the layout provider tree (completing ADR-0121's toast standard for all screens).
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(wave2): **Seven new deal-tool UIs over the Wave 0 REST layer (construction plan Wave 2, round-2/3 review surfaces).** Project sub-nav grows from 14 to 21 tabs: incentives/clawback (incentive sourcing + clawback rules), sites (score comparison), permits/certifications (separate permit/certification lists per flow.spec), debt (instruments with rate/maturity/covenants), interviews (evidence_ref mandatory), customer-CoC (>=30% concentration auto-flag), comp-parity (aggregate-only, k-anonymity + exec-ref exposure per SEC-05). Each screen follows the ux-designer-agent acceptance checklist — search/registration surfaces, single primary action, PageState loading/empty/error, toast confirmation, ADR-0088 table alignment. Navigation registered (NON_MENU_ROUTES + ProjectSubNav).
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(wave1): **Deal-centric skeleton — /deals board + workspace + stage tabs (ADR-0120, construction Wave 1).** New `/deals` board merges the opportunity funnel and project pipeline into one four-column view with search/status filter control-chips, gate aging, and a new-deal entry to S1. `/deals/{id}` overview resolves either carrier through the process API (opportunity first, project fallback) and renders the current stage, blockers/artifacts checklist, gate status chips, and an input←output linkage strip. `/deals/{id}/s[stage]` stage tabs render per-stage status with track-applicability awareness and link to working surfaces (full workload integration lands in Wave 2). Shared UI primitives (components/ui.tsx): Button (btn-primary/ghost/danger — ADR-0119 single-primary hierarchy), ControlChip (status/control separation), URL-state Tabs, PageState (skeleton/empty/error with retry), ToastProvider (role=status/alert mutation feedback). styles.css gains btn-primary, control-chip, skeleton (reduced-motion aware), toast, pager per design-tokens v1.2.0. Navigation re-planed (ADR-0105 SSOT + test updated): the explore group leads with the deal board, legacy stage screens move to a "legacy stage group" group until Wave 4 redirects; /deals dynamic routes registered. Legacy deep links (pauseLinkFor) intentionally unchanged until Wave 4.
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(wave0): **Backend foundation for the v5 mockup surfaces (construction plan Wave 0, ADR-0122/0123).** Migration 0087 lands the data contract: brownfield_target discovery provenance (`discovery_source` + `proposal_accepted_at` — S6/S7 candidate suggestions become first-class), antitrust screening, site/facility assessment, contract CoC, dd_interview, deal_comment (collaboration annotations), stage_sla policy, `gate_decision.erp_ref` (e-approval cross-reference), and external safe-share tables (`share_link` + `share_access`). Fourteen REST surfaces (all envelope-wrapped, role-gated analyst+, member-visible, audit-logged, OpenAPI-registered): antitrust, incentives (legacy NOT NULL columns honored), sites, permits+certifications (tracked separately), debt instruments, interviews (evidence_ref mandatory), customer-CoC (≥30% concentration auto-flag), comp-parity (aggregate-only, exec refs), target proposals (deterministic master-data suggestions + provenance accept), agent-runs (transparency timeline + PROPOSAL counts — read-only, boundary #2), safe-share links (≤30-day expiry, immediate revoke, public indistinguishable-dead access route that logs every grant), forecast-actual backtest (adopted-case p50 vs scorecard attainment; insufficient evidence degrades honestly), and collaboration comments (project entities membership-gated). 14-test contract suite covers role boundaries, validation, provenance, the share lifecycle, and honest degradation.
- **[2026-09-12]**: docs(design): **Deal-centric UI redesign package — v5 development baseline, ADR-0119..0123, ux-designer-agent, construction plan.** Three all-agent design reviews converged into a frozen development baseline: full-service mockup v5 (56 screens — dashboard, auth, deal board/list/overview, S1-S12 stages with input-output linkage strips, 15 deal tools incl. new incentive/site/permits/debt/interviews/customer-CoC/comp-parity surfaces, agent-run viewer with LLM reliability scores, forecast-actual backtest, external safe-share, committee session viewer, risk heatmap, stage SLA timeline, portfolio view + resource calendar, onboarding wizard, design-system gallery) frozen as `mockups/baseline-v5.html` + 56 per-screen previews + an implementation matrix (baseline preview vs implemented route tracking for post-build comparison). New ADRs: 0119 (btn-primary action hierarchy + status/control chip separation), 0120 (deal-centric /deals workspace IA), 0121 (PageState/Toast/DataTable/Tabs primitives), 0122 (round-2 outcomes — SLA timeline, risk heatmap, data trust overlay, portfolio oversight, onboarding, collaboration annotations, audit immutability visibility, scenario snapshots), 0123 (round-3 outcomes — agent-run transparency, flow.spec version badge, forecast-actual backtest, LLM reliability score, external safe-share with security sign-off gate, e-approval cross-reference/hub, source health, group isolation, copy dictionary). New agent `ux-designer-agent` (technical/design) owns screen design under the Onyx 3.0 doctrine with a design acceptance checklist. Construction plan (PM-led waves 0-4 + all-agent quality review with weighted scoring and mockup-vs-build comparison protocol) accepted; data contract documents every schema/API gap before implementation. Design tokens v1.2.0.
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(web-next): **S7→S8 promotion surface + pipeline spec-conformance hardening (P1/P2 linkage repair).** The S1–S12 chain was organically linked in the spec and the evaluateProcess engine but dead-ended in the UI: no screen called POST /api/opportunities/[id]/promote, so a deal finishing S5 (greenfield) or S7 (brownfield) could never become a project from the UI. New PromotionPanel (mounted on /greenfield, /brownfield, and selected opportunities' expanded rows) shows track-final-stage readiness from the process API and records the promotion as an explicit human action (need-to-know basis required, origin/project-name optional, admin hidden per ADR-0091, lands on the new project). Spec conformance tightened to v2.3.0: greenfield S4 now requires intent + 5-criteria scoring on both tracks (ADR-0104); promotion preconditions additionally require every track-relevant gate GO (screening + pre_fs, +target_screening for brownfield — aligning the direct path with the auto-pipeline's firstPendingGate ordering, an explicit amendment of ADR-0095's fact-checks-only posture); S11 completion now checks the adopted business case and the charter baseline — with a new POST /api/projects/{id}/charter/confirm endpoint + charter-page baseline-confirmation action giving the confirmation surface its missing writer (OpenAPI entry added); the opportunity gate-decisions route rejects project-carrier gates; the project-stage default predicate flipped fail-closed. New parity gates lock the two untested spec copies (process-flow STAGES, spec-activities.generated.ts — regenerated verbatim from the YAML) alongside the already-locked stage-meta copy.
- **[2026-09-12]**: feat(web-next): **StageRail navigation + deep-link repairs.** StageRail steps now link to their owning stage screens (project stages when rendered under /projects/[id]); the /pipeline header's /projects/undefined/process link (useParams misuse on a static route) is removed; /opportunities reads ?opportunity= to auto-expand the deep-linked row and /projects' view-opportunity link points at it.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Stage-scoped LLM decision support across S1-S12 (ADR-0118).** The boundary-#2 amendment requires every human-judgment surface to actively offer LLM-drafted information and recommendations, and an audit found S5/S10 fully LLM-blind, six gate surfaces taking freehand rationales, and S2/S8/S12 judgments with no at-decision-point proposal. A governing constraint ships with the features: **each stage's LLM feature reads only the artifacts flow.spec declares as that stage's inputs plus its own stage state** — an explicit per-feature context manifest enforced in code and displayed in the UI, never a bulk dump. Six on-demand endpoints (nothing persisted, analyst+, whitelist-normalized LLM JSON, deterministic fallback that never fabricates values, audit-logged): shared gate brief (`lib/gate-brief.ts` + opportunity/project routes) covering screening·pre_fs·target_screening·fs·dd·entity_ic/group_ic via GateDecisionPanel (recommendation chip, evidence, apply-to-rationale prefill — the decision select stays untouched); S5 capability-gap + Build/Partner/Hire/Invest strategy draft with form apply; S10 deal-structure hypothesis draft (vehicle/stake/instrument + four-axis notes); S12 monitoring brief (assessment, risk flags, 5-vocab transition suggestion, learning capture); S8 adoption-recommendation summary including what-would-change; S2 attractiveness score draft. Deferred with rationale: S6 candidate suggestions (hallucination risk until the Tavily evidence adapter lands), S7 per-item briefs, S1 confirm UX. Side-fix: the screening run route now writes the audit trail like its siblings. Design doc `docs/designs/2026-09-11-stage-llm-decision-support-design_ko.md`; 13 new synthesis-path tests; OpenAPI entries for all six endpoints.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **S4 LLM route recommendation + selection-to-evaluation flow (boundary #2 amendment).** The S4 route-decision screen left the "screened opportunity → evaluation" path unwired — selected opportunities offered only a sketch action on the opportunities board, and the decision form sat below the fold with no explicit entry — and the decision-support principle (boundary #2, CLAUDE.md/GEMINI.md/AGENTS.md amendment) now requires decision surfaces to actively offer LLM-drafted information and recommendations while humans record the final decision. The opportunities board gains a "proceed to S4 evaluation" action for selected opportunities (next-step cell + expanded row), and /route-decision gains a "proceed with evaluation" CTA under the opportunity chips (scrolls to the new `#route-form` anchor), repointed flow-guide anchors, and an empty state linking the assessed filter of the opportunities board. New `POST /api/opportunities/{id}/route-recommendation` (analyst+, selected-only) drafts a route recommendation from the S1~S3 artifacts already in the DB plus the deterministic track advisory (ADR-0104): recommended track/route/intent, 5-criteria draft scores with evidence notes, rationale/alternatives drafts, key evidence, and cautions — LLM output is whitelist-normalized (never trusted raw), the response is source-tagged (`llm` vs `synthesis`), the deterministic fallback echoes the advisory and never fabricates scores, nothing is persisted (no schema change, no ADR trigger), and each generation is audit-logged. The screen renders the proposal in a new LLM-recommendation section with provenance chips and an apply-to-form action behind a confirm dialog — apply fills the form, the recorded decision stays the human "save decision" click. 6 new unit tests; OpenAPI entry added.
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-12]**: fix(repo): **Restore roster/skill files clobbered by the db271ba upgrade + unblock the web-next build.** The 2026-09-12 "fleet sync" upgrade deleted 39 agents/*.md roster files and 57 skills/*/SKILL.md (the declared source of truth of sync-agent-personas.ts and AGENTS.md — same clobber class as the CI workflow restored in 7dd396d), leaving tests/unit red (process-flow, committee-boundary, skill-graph); the deleted paths are selectively restored from db271ba^ (agent:verify 40/40). Seven pre-existing web-next build/type failures on main are repaired: companies page import depth, a spurious actor option in the DART sync route, EnvelopeStatus missing 503, WACC suggestion narrowing + missing field, dart-financial-facts missing getDb import + optional yearly handling, report-composer's nonexistent dd.complete field, wacc-reference not returning its typed spread_pct, and the wacc-session hardcoded asOf time-bomb (as_of DESC ordering flips once the stamped date slips into the past).
- **[2026-09-11]**: test(web-next): **wacc-session time-bomb defused.** The ADR-0115 suite's direct-mode session recorded at a fixed `now` (2026-09-11T02:00Z) while the HTTP capm tests in the same file record at real time — once real time passed the fixed stamp, resolveProjectWacc's latest-assumption ordering flipped and the suite went permanently red (reproduced on clean main, unrelated to any in-flight PR). The fixed timestamp is dropped; the session now records at real time like its siblings.
- **[2026-09-11]**: chore(skills): **Governance record for value-chain-linkage-analysis v1.1.0** (`docs/lifecycle/skills/value-chain-linkage-analysis.md`) — skill-session-review flagged the skill as modified without a lifecycle record after its persistence surface (value-chain-links REST, topology endpoint) shipped in ADR-0116 Phase 5.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Proxy-chain REST write surface + calibrate trigger (ADR-0116 Phase 5).** The S9 proxy-chain tab was a permanent empty state: `value_chain_link` (created by 0011) had zero writers anywhere and the ADR-0056 project-industry binding repository had zero callers, so agents could not land judged links through ADR-0011's REST-only rule. New `value-chain-link.repository.ts` (first writer; judgment numerics only — β/R² stay calibrate-exclusive per ADR-0025; evidence_ref validated per ADR-0058; audit-logged), `GET/POST /api/projects/{id}/value-chain-links`, `GET/POST/DELETE /api/projects/{id}/industries` (wiring the previously unreachable ADR-0056 bind/retire repository with mandatory rationale), `POST /api/projects/{id}/proxy-chain/calibrate` (spawns the ADR-0006 calibrate methodology per pair; honest guidance listing the exact pairs and the observation-series dependency instead of a silent no-op), `GET /api/projects/{id}/value-chain/topology` (the endpoint value-chain-stage-financials has referenced since it was written), and `GET /api/industries` search. Tab rewritten to the approved mockup (binding table with retire, link registration form, calibrate button, chain grades); `value-chain-linkage-analysis` skill updated with the real REST paths. 7 new repository/validation tests; OpenAPI entries added.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Reports composer + renderer downloads (ADR-0116 Phase 6).** The reports tab was a hand-typed Markdown box and the docx/pdf/hwpx renderer path was unreachable (the UI never sent `formats`) with rendered files stranded on disk. New `POST /api/projects/{id}/reports/compose` builds an IC-report draft from the IC-package surface (adopted business case with dual-variant comparison, DD completion, RAG proposal, deal structure, gate history) plus the pipeline briefing pack (`lib/report-composer.ts`, deterministic); `report_render` (migration 0086) persists where each rendered file lives and the new guarded download route (`GET .../reports/{snapshotId}/render/{format}`) streams it after format whitelisting and cwd-independent path-containment checks. Fixed two latent bugs the new flow exposed: the renderer summary parser JSON-parsed only the first line of a pretty-printed array inside a promise executor (uncaught throw left the request hanging forever — now full-stdout parse with reject), and `useConfirm`'s confirm button kept the previous invocation's label (an adopt dialog offered the delete label). Tab rewritten to the approved mockup (compose button, format checkboxes, download links, CTA empty state).
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Tab hygiene — orphan tab retired, in-frame stage guidance, empty-state CTAs (ADR-0116 Phase 7).** Deleted the greenfield-feasibility page (a read-only orphan: zero inbound links, absent from ProjectSubNav, duplicating the dd GET — S9's completion predicate already judges the dd checklist track-agnostically) and its navigation registration. `StageFrame` now renders a stage-guidance strip on every project tab (S8–S12): the stage's remaining blockers and required artifacts from the existing process API, or a completion chip — no per-page duplication. Empty states across ic-review/dd gained next-action CTAs, and TargetCheckPanel renders a failure notice instead of silently disappearing.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Dual-perspective business-case valuation — market_forward vs historical side by side (ADR-0117, migration 0085).** S8 cases grew from a single growth anchor (opportunity growth_rate, fallback 5%) with a hardcoded 2% annual dispersion, so neither the subject company's own trajectory nor real volatility moved the numbers. Each scenario case now exists in two variants from the same seeded engine: **market_forward** (opportunity growth; forward volatility profile blending historical revenue volatility with the WACC session's `wacc.beta_vol_asset`, clamped 2–25%) remains the gate/kill-criteria/RECOMMENDED primary, and **historical** (bound-company DART revenue CAGR + YoY stdev) is comparison evidence — generated only when company facts exist, never fabricated (N/A with a connect+sync CTA otherwise). Parameters and their sources persist in line notes and render in a per-scenario comparison table (perspective | growth (source) | sigma (source) | NPV p05/p50/p95 | IRR | delta-p50); the IC package carries the sibling-variant rows. Legacy rows default to market_forward so existing projects keep their semantics; activity ids gain the variant segment (`scenario:variant:step`). ADR-0117; regression tests cover parameter bounds, seeded determinism, and the market_forward-only recommendation rule.
- **[2026-09-11]**: feat(web-next): **Company financial facts — DART ingestion, company screen with commentary layer, and project bridge (ADR-0116, migrations 0083–0084).** `fact_financial` existed schema-only with zero writers, leaving the financial-analysis tab a permanent empty state. New `lib/dart-financial-facts.ts` resolves/validates the company's DART corp code (persisting it back), pulls 5 business years through the shared dartFetch gate, seeds `md_account` (migration 0083) and year periods, and lands `fact_financial` rows append-only — unchanged values skip, changed values append a revision (ensureFresh discipline; never overwrite). The binding that GET reads (`md_project.company_id`) was only set by brownfield promotion, so `POST /api/projects/{id}/financial-analysis/company` binds any subject company with a mandatory rationale (`md_project.subject_company_rationale`, migration 0084) — `project_company` was deliberately not resurrected (dropped by 0073 as superseded by brownfield_target). New company screen `/companies/{companyId}` renders the 5-year statements with a deterministic commentary layer (headline cards, per-metric plain-language readings, threshold callouts), and the project tab becomes a bridge: bind + summary card + link out. Renderer-summary JSON parsing and `useConfirm` stale-label bugs fixed along the way; WACC reference prefill lands with Phase 3/4 (ECOS). ADR-0116; route/writer tests included.
