- **[2026-09-10]**: feat(web-next): **Business-target standards as group/affiliate master data + non-blocking fs-gate evidence panel (ADR-0114, migration 0082, flow.spec v2.3.0).** New business proposals had no pre-defined, auditable success criteria; `md_business_target` (migration 0082, tombstone deletion per 0081, partial unique index = one active row per group/scope/metric) now stores group-common and affiliate-specific standards over an extensible metric list (presets: final-year revenue, operating margin, plan period, NPV, IRR; plus user-defined custom KPIs), managed admin-only at the Master-data console section with 100M-KRW / % / year input units converted to storage units (ADR-0061). A project's scope resolves via `md_project.company_id` → the group registry, affiliate rows shadowing group-common ones; the new read-only `/api/projects/[id]/target-check` compares the resolved standards against the adopted Business Case (final-year line compounding matching the S8 rendering, margin excluding capex, recommended-scenario p50 NPV/IRR) and `TargetCheckPanel` renders met/missed/indeterminate/manual chips above the fs `GateDecisionPanel`. `flow.spec.yaml` v2.3.0 adds one fs decision criterion asking the human to check this evidence in the base scenario — display only, never a blocker or an automatic verdict (ADR-0053/0094 boundary). Projects without an initiating affiliate and custom KPIs degrade honestly; unit tests cover resolution precedence, evaluation formulas, tombstone behavior, and deny-by-default visibility.
- **[2026-09-10]**: feat(web-next): **WACC CAPM decomposition — market-rate citation, beta volatility evidence, and a persisted derivation trail (ADR-0115).** The WACC input session persisted only the confirmed scalar, so reviewers could not see how the discount rate was built and the S5 sample carried beta as `assumed` because no price series existed in the pipeline. A CAPM component layer now records every input as its own assumption row under the `wacc.*` namespace on the confirmed run (`wacc.risk_free`, `wacc.erp`, `wacc.size_premium`, `wacc.beta_levered`/`wacc.beta_unlevered`, `wacc.observed_de`, `wacc.target_de`, `wacc.tax_rate`, `wacc.cost_of_debt`/`wacc.debt_spread`, plus `wacc.beta_r2`/`wacc.beta_alpha`/`wacc.beta_vol_asset`/`wacc.beta_vol_market` regression stats) and re-derives the build-up — formulas and substituted numbers — on read via `db/lib/wacc-components.ts` `assembleDerivation()` (pure TS, imported client-side so the screen and the registry cannot drift). Market figures are cited from an authoritative publication (AlphaSpread, Bank of Korea ECOS, Damodaran) as `kind='live'` with source name, `https://` anchor_ref (ADR-0058 §2) and as-of date; judgment values stay `kind='user'`; components never write `kind='assumed'`. Beta takes two paths: peer-cited levered betas Hamada-unlevered to a median, or an OLS regression over a pasted `date, price, index` series (`db/lib/beta-regression.ts`) reporting beta, R², and annualized volatility of asset vs market (×√252) persisted as stat rows. Cost of debt follows the company's credit rating (Rf + spread) or the actual loan rate. `POST /api/projects/{id}/wacc` with `mode=capm` validates the component set (`capmComponentGaps`) and returns the server-computed WACC as `wacc_computed`, flagging `computed_mismatch` when the human-confirmed value diverges; GET returns the persisted components plus the re-assembled derivation. The reworked WACC page (approved mockup `docs/designs/mockups/2026-09-10-wacc-capm-ui-mockup_ko.html`) adds mode tabs, cited component inputs, a beta panel with regression scatter and volatility comparison bars, the derivation table, CAPM build-up and WACC-blend waterfalls (`RoicWaterfallChart` gains `numberFormat="percent"`), and a saved-session ledger with per-figure `SourceTooltip`. New CLI `wacc:derivation --project <id>` emits the same tables as markdown for S5/S8 deliverables. `wacc-input-session` skill v1.3.0 (mirrors synced), glossary WACC entry, ADR-0115, and the 2026-09-09 engine-seams design doc's deferred-scope footnote updated. 21 new unit tests; the kill-criteria exact-key contract (`wacc`/`hurdle_irr`) is untouched.

The governance summary now lives in AGENTS.md "Deal Pipeline Orchestration" (a project-owned region the upgrade merge never touches), pm.md keeps only a thin pointer matching the common template's thin-pm design ("detail lives in AGENTS.md"), the operational detail stays in the `deal-pipeline-orchestration` skill (a verified strict superset of the removed text), and the cross-references in pm.md (orchestration-surfaces bullet, committee-PROPOSAL rule), CLAUDE.md §5, and the skill description were re-pointed. Personas regenerated via sync-agent-personas; agent:verify and validate-agents green.
- **[2026-09-10]**: feat(web-next): **S3 competitive-positioning disclosure cross-check (ADR-0113, payload schemaVersion 3).** The competition tab's positioning was an LLM estimate from web-research excerpts alone behind a permanent pre-verification disclaimer; a new pipeline step 5 (`lib/company-profile-crosscheck.ts`) now checks each claim against the latest DART business-report original text — `searchDisclosures` gains a `business` preset plus `selectBusinessReport` (latest rcept_dt, non-amended filing preferred), the previously uncalled `fetchDisclosureText` is wired with deterministic keyword-window context extraction (≤6 merged 4k-char windows around competition/product/market-share/revenue-mix hits) to fit the 500k-char filing into one bounded LLM call — plus one targeted Tavily query per competitor (≤5) as independent triangulation. Per-claim verdicts (confirmed / directional / contradicted / unverified, with evidence quote and dart/research source class) render in the competition tab (claim table, per-competitor chip, DART original-text link, dynamic footnote) and never auto-rewrite the estimate — contradictions surface to the analyst, never auto-scored. Section confidence upgrades C→B- only on ≥1 DART-confirmed claim; the verification ledger gains per-competitor rows it previously lacked entirely; HWP-primary filings and unlisted members degrade honestly to `dart_unavailable`/`research_only` with the reason recorded. 15 new unit tests; stored profiles pick this up on re-analyze (append-only payloads).
- **[2026-09-10]**: feat(web-next): **Business-model sketch v2 — BMC canvas with integrated Blue Ocean ERRC actions.** The sketch was a text list with poor readability; it is now a visual canvas (`schema: 2` in `opportunity.sketch_json`, no migration): 9-block Business Model Canvas grid, per-item ERRC badges (Eliminate/Reduce/Raise/Create) with a one-line rationale, market-entry flow, ERRC summary strip, and scenario/risk cards — tab-switchable with the legacy text view kept for old rows and as the parse-failure fallback. Generation uses one LLM call with a v2 JSON template, lossless normalization (string items, unknown actions dropped, block caps), and a deterministic fallback sketch instead of a 400. `components/SketchCanvas.tsx` renders it full-width on /opportunities (no tab chrome — legacy v1 rows fall back to the legacy text list directly).
- **[2026-09-10]**: feat(pipeline): **KSIC auto-classification on market-sizing runs.** Re-running an analysis no longer leaves a NULL industry KSIC: the sizing pipeline classifies the industry against the curated KSIC master (LLM, with a deterministic keyword-match fallback when only one candidate matches) and fills the code only when it is NULL — analyst-confirmed codes are never overwritten, master-unknown codes and codes held by other industries are rejected, and unresolved classifications surface as a step-1 pending input. The humanoid-robot industry is classified to 29280 (industrial robot manufacturing).

Leads get a row-level delete action on /projects that marks the whole deal chain (project + source opportunity, synergy analyses guarded with 409 while live) and writes a `deal_archive` manifest (member allowlist + market version freeze) served by the member-only `/api/archive/deals` endpoints; lead-only close/archive records the same manifest. Shipped with the operational documentation set (archive manuals en/ko, adopted deletion playbook en/ko, runbook registry + `runbooks:validate` validator, evidence-file policy) and the proposed follow-ups plan (un-delete, deletion-reason dialog, archive export, retention engine — Phase 1-5, not yet required).

- **[2026-09-06]**: feat(api): **Envelope-guaranteed route handlers — `route()` wraps every API handler (ADR-0109).** api-spec §9's `{data, meta, error}` envelope previously held only on the happy path: an uncaught exception fell through to Next.js's default error handling and returned an HTML 500 that `lib/api.ts` and the MCP REST layer cannot parse. New `app/web-next/lib/route.ts` exports `route(handler)` — try/catch around the handler, server-side error log, `fail(500, "internal_error", <message>)` in the standard envelope on escape. All 16 handlers across the 10 existing route files retrofitted (llm-status, committee-sessions/[sessionId], establishment, factors, gate-decisions, pipeline/status, route-advisory, route-decision, series, series/[id]/observations) and all new routes born wrapped — 34 handlers across 20 route files total; handler-returned 401/403/404/409 Responses pass through untouched. - **[2026-09-06]**: feat(process,scoring,s11): **Registry-driven extensibility — stage-completion predicate maps, scoring-weights data module, and the JV/PMI/partnership DD workspaces (ADR-0110).** (A) `lib/process-flow.ts` stage completion moved from if/else chains inside the evaluator bodies to two predicate registries — `OPPORTUNITY_STAGE_COMPLETION` (S1–S7) and `PROJECT_STAGE_COMPLETION` (S8–S12) — keyed by stage id; a new stage/case type registers a predicate instead of editing the engine body. Behavior unchanged (each predicate is the previous branch verbatim; unmapped opportunity stages default not-complete, unmapped project stages default complete, matching the old fall-through) and current_stage remains derived, never stored. (B) New `lib/scoring-weights.ts` — the hybrid scoring methodology as DATA (ADR-0102): `SYNERGY_FACTOR_WEIGHTS` (0.35/0.3/0.2/0.15), `MARKET_ATTRACTIVENESS_CURVE` (0%→0, 8%→50, +0.27 span→100), `FINANCIAL_CAPACITY_PARAMS` (base 45, debt floor 0.4, slope 180, ±40 penalty, ROE 350, margin 300, 0.5/0.3 contributions); `lib/synergy-score.ts` imports them instead of inlining literals. Deliberately computation-free; declared as the merge seam for the future industry-profile/region-profile override per the ADR-0024/0044 profile-first principle. (C) First functional surfaces for the five previously dead ADR-0052 §C/§D models (`partner_candidate`, `partner_dd_finding`, `jv_term`, `pmi_key_talent`, `pmi_synergy_certification`): 10 project-keyed route files under `app/api/projects/[id]/partners` (candidates + nested DD findings), `jv-terms`, and `pmi/key-talent` + `pmi/synergy-certifications` — 18 operations registered in openapi.yaml, every route member-authenticated, role-gated, audit-logged, and ADR-0109-wrapped — plus two project-detail pages `/projects/[id]/partnership` and `/projects/[id]/pmi` registered in ProjectSubNav and navigation.ts NON_MENU_ROUTES per the ADR-0105 contract. - **[2026-09-04]**: fix(review): **Comprehensive project-review fixes (5-domain audit, 67-test suite green).** Critical: .githooks were committed non-executable so pre-commit/commit-msg/pre-push silently never ran (chmod +x, index 100755); a committed SQLite backup (app/web-next/db/data/_archived-duplicate/) removed from the index and app/web-next/db/data fully gitignored. Security: CSRF double-submit no longer skipped when the csrf cookie is absent (cookie-auth mutations now require both halves — csrf_missing 403); viewer accounts can no longer issue write-scoped API tokens; Gemini provider key moved from URL query to the x-goog-api-key header; upload-text reader validates stored names against path traversal. Engine: opportunity evaluation now surfaces HOLD on ALL current-stage gates (S4 pre_fs HOLD was silently ignored — only screening was checked); flow.spec entity_lifecycles corrected to the ADR-0094/0095 reality (opportunity S1-S7, project S8-S11) and docs/process-flow.md regenerated. Audit: brownfield target exclusion, business-case DELETE, and committee human-position submits now appendAudit. Registries/docs: skills/SKILLS.md regenerated (138 rows, ~20 missing skills registered, version drift + malformed status column fixed); ADR index completed (0074/0075/0095-0102 added, mid-table prose fixed); memory/MEMORY.md broken links repointed; standing-procedure memory renumbered to flow.spec v2; MCP get_project_dd_workspace registered (was an orphaned module) and S1-S7 labels updated to S1-S11; CI gained a bun unit-tests job; README Recharts->ECharts + Python venv Quick Start + stale placeholder removed; /projects/[id]/process cross-linked from all project pages.
- **[2026-09-04]**: fix(synergy): **Scoring-engine calibration fixes from the hydraulic-breaker sample run.** (1) Market-attractiveness normalization was piecewise-linear from a 10% CAGR floor, which flattened mature markets (a 5.7% CAGR industry scored 0/100) — now 0%->0, 8%->50, 35%+->100. (2) The group-rollup path (targetType=group) did not feed scoring inputs, so industry-match and financial-capacity factors were silently dropped — the rollup now carries the union of member industry exposures and the representative profile's DART ratios into the engine. Verified live on the Sosan group run: rule score 30->49, final hybrid 41->53 after LLM narrative (both directions of adjustment exercised across runs: +3/+8/-8 with recorded justifications).
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(synergy,s9): **Hybrid synergy scoring (ADR-0102) + live env probes + full humanoid-robot-to-DD sample run.** (A) New lib/synergy-score.ts — deterministic factor engine (market attractiveness from S1 CAGR, industry/exposure match, DART financial capacity, scope match; renormalized weights; fail-closed null below 2 factors) producing an explainable 0-100 recommendation with per-factor breakdown. (B) Synergy pipeline step 5 rewritten as hybrid: the rule engine anchors the score, the LLM adds a narrative + bounded ±10 adjustment (reason required) when available; on LLM failure the run still yields a rule-based score + factor breakdown instead of losing the recommendation. Payload gains scoringMethod/synergyScoreFactors/synergyScoreLimitations. (C) Research/evaluation separation: the ADR-0090 profile-reuse path now runs Tavily research independently of the LLM (was silently 0 sources). (D) env-check --probe: live 1-request validity probes for LLM/DART/Tavily — key presence was never validity (the expired LLM key returned 401 while presence checks passed; the probe now flags it). (E) ADR-0091 conformance: /promote refuses admin accounts (an admin promotion made the promoter a lead whom every deal route 404-blocks — a dead end by design). (F) Sample chain executed end-to-end via the live REST API (also a website-operability pass): humanoid-robot market S1 (8-step run + analyst-researched v2 matrix seeded with a GS/MS/Fortune cross-checked anchor $3.2B 2026E, 35-39% CAGR), S2 opportunity registered+selected, Samsung Electronics profile (DART 2021-2025 real financials) + hybrid synergy 63/100 rule-only then 78/100 after key replacement, S4 brownfield strategic_investment with 5-criteria scoring, S6 universe + 5-candidate long list (DART codes), S7 screening → shortlist Rainbow Robotics #1 / Robotis #2 + 3 reasoned exclusions, promotion to project PRJ-QGB2W5, S8 3-scenario valuation + Monte Carlo quantiles (base p05 ≈ KRW 60.0B, positive), fs GO, S9 DD 29/37 desk-research judgments → dd HOLD with the 8 data-room items listed explicitly. Also fixed en route: the S7 checklist silently empty (library id mismatch chk-target-screening → chk-target-screening-checklist) and S6 coverage regressing on legitimate post-screening exclusions (now counts all discovered candidates).
### Fixed
- **[2026-09-11]**: fix(web-next): **WACC input rows no longer overlap on md–lg viewports — fixed grid tracks demoted to xl, form controls allowed to shrink.** The CAPM WACC page's input rows (step-4 capital-structure/tax rows, CitedRow rows) and BetaPanel's βU row pinned their grid to fixed-width tracks (`9.5rem/7rem/…`) from `md` up, so on md–lg widths the numeric inputs overflowed their tracks into the adjacent fields; each row now collapses to `grid-cols-2` below `xl` and applies the fixed template only at `xl`. Form controls also gain `min-width: 0` (styles.css) so they can shrink inside fixed tracks instead of forcing overflow (`min-width: auto` was the default blocker).
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(web-next): **Editor rows sized by flex-basis instead of fixed widths — clipped values readable in the greenfield capability-gap and Business Case lines plus similar entry fields.** The capability-gap rows and Business Case lines on the S5 greenfield screen pinned their inputs to fixed 6-10rem widths, so real values (multi-clause capability names like the EU-certification and dealer-network entries) rendered truncated while the wide panel left the rest of each row empty; the same fixed-width pattern cramped free-text entry fields on /opportunities (opportunity/risk factor drafts at 9rem) and /brownfield (exclusion reason at 12rem). Editor inputs in these rows now size via flex-basis with grow so they share the row width and wrap naturally; inputs whose content is bounded (attractiveness score draft, BC amount, deal-structure numeric entry) and the KSIC search picker keep their compact fixed sizes.
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(web-next): **S3 synergy opportunity dropdown populated again — the page parsed the opportunity-list envelope as `{ rows }` while the route returns the array directly.** The linked-opportunity selector on /synergy offered only the opportunity-less analysis mode since its 2026-09-02 rewrite: `api.get<{ rows }>("/api/opportunities")` read `r.data.rows` off a `data` payload that is the rows array itself (`ok(rows, ...)` — the same shape every other consumer parses correctly, e.g. app/page.tsx and route-decision), so `.map` threw on undefined and the `.catch(() => setOpps([]))` silently swallowed it into an always-empty list. Now parses `r.data` as the array like the other pages; the four registered opportunities render again and can be linked to a synergy analysis for the ADR-0090 synergy_score record.
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(agents): **Deal Pipeline Orchestration restored in pm.md after the working-tree sweep clobbered it.** The 51e1cb9 sweep commit carried a stale pre-c506408 copy of `agents/pm.md`, silently reverting the deliberate "PM learns the deal pipeline" fix and leaving references to AGENTS.md §-anchors that do not exist (AGENTS.md itself still points at the section). The canonical file is restored from 60ac19f and the `.claude` persona regenerated, so `agents:personas --check` passes with the section intact instead of the drift gate laundering the loss into the harness persona.
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(governance): **audit-workspace skill created, design-doc language exceptions declared, sketch-schema str helper added.** The `audit-workspace` skill that phase-definitions (Phase-6 QA), project-review, and ci-triage all reference as the machine-battery wrapper never existed, failing the skill-graph gate (`related_skills entry 'audit-workspace' has no SKILL.md`); it is now created as the thin deterministic battery wrapper those docs describe and distributed to all three platform skill copies, with the skill graph regenerated (307 nodes, `verify-skill-graph` green). Five docs/designs files with undeclared Korean content declared the sanctioned `lang: ko` / `lang_reason: source-material` frontmatter exception (same as the passing `_ko` precedent) — `validate:language` exits 0 again. `lib/sketch-schema.ts` gained the missing `str` scalar-coercion helper, a ReferenceError on any object-form sketch item at runtime and a tsc error on build.
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(web-next): **First table column no longer monopolizes the table's surplus width.** Follow-up to the col-* alignment fix: auto table layout treats a column whose only constraint is `min-width: 12rem` (`col-label`) as carrying that width into the surplus-distribution pass, so with rigid `nowrap` numeric columns the first column absorbed nearly all leftover space (the year column rendered ~3x its floor). `.report-table .col-label` now declares `width: 1%` alongside the 12rem floor — the column converges to content size (bounded below by 12rem) and the surplus redistributes to the numeric/text columns. Applies uniformly to every report-table (financial ratio/summary, industry matrix, competitors, market competition/regulation, synergy areas).
- **[2026-09-10]**: fix(web-next): **Table th/td col-* alignment made symmetric across pages, with a design-lint guard; DART loss-variant account names no longer dropped.** The financial ratio/trend table (our-company) rendered right-aligned `col-num` headers over left-aligned cells because the ADR-0088 col-* classes were applied to `<th>` only; the same asymmetry existed in the DART summary, industry-exposure matrix, competitor, market competition/regulation tables, and in reverse (classed `<td>` under unclassed `<th>`) in WaiverPanel and the project list — where the classes were additionally inert because those tables lack `report-table`. All touched tables now mirror col-* on th and td; `.data-table .col-num` applies the alignment outside report-tables too (width-only col-label/col-type/col-text stay report-table-scoped to avoid layout shifts); new design-lint rule R9 `table-align` fails the lint when a table's th/td col-* class sets are asymmetric. Separately, operating margin / net margin / ROE showed "—" for every year because the DART ingestion whitelist dropped loss-variant account titles: `영업이익(손실), 당기순이익(손실), 영업수익, 반기순이익, 분기순이익` now normalize to their standard accounts at fetch time (parity with the derive-side alias table); `지배기업 소유주지분` subtotals are deliberately NOT normalized so a subtotal can never shadow the statement total under first-wins dedup. Stored profiles need a re-analyze per member to pick this up (profile payloads are append-only).
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **KSIC picker shows official 11th-revision names — profile aliases demoted to tooltips (ADR-0111 revision 2026-09-10).** User report: code 74100 rendered as the profile-internal label (plant O&M service) instead of its official name. Every dataset `name_ko` is now the verified official 11th-revision name (verified 2026-09-10 against the categoryofbusiness.com KSIC-11 DB, cross-checked with official renumbering examples): 74100 = facilities maintenance service, 35114 = solar power, 35119 = other power generation, 63999 = other information services. Profile labels moved to the detail field as explicit aliases (still searchable, shown in the tooltip). Four dataset entries whose profile anchors conflict with the 11th revision were EXCLUDED rather than displayed with false names: 264 (semiconductors - 11th-rev 264 is communication/broadcast equipment, semiconductors live at 261/26111/26112), 29192 (robots - 11th-rev 29192 is extinguisher manufacturing, industrial robots are 29280), 30111 (shipbuilding - 11th-rev ocean-going ships are 31111), 30303 (space - no such 11th-rev code, spacecraft under 3131). Correcting those four profile anchors is owner-approved content and is flagged for the normal profile-governance path. Curated list is now 9 entries; tests updated with a 74100 official-name regression.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **KSIC search button uses the lucide Search icon instead of the emoji glyph.** On some render environments the U+1F50D emoji in the button label and the create-form hint degraded to a bare circle glyph, making the shipped picker look unrendered. Replaced with the lucide `Search` icon (16px inline-action size, per the design icon vocabulary where search/lookup maps to Search, not an emoji) and dropped the emoji from the hint sentence. SVG renders identically everywhere; no behavior change.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **KSIC search now queries by industry name when the code field is empty.** The search button was disabled until the KSIC input had text, so the natural flow - type the industry name, click the search button - silently did nothing (a disabled button gives no feedback). The search query now prefers the KSIC field (code prefix or name substring) and falls back to the industry name, mirroring the DART picker's search-by-name flow; the button enables as soon as either field has text and the form hint states the behavior. Selecting a result fills only the KSIC code - the user-authored industry name is not overwritten (unlike DART, where the registry name is authoritative).
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **KSIC picker interaction aligned with the standard search pattern (design-guide section 6, ADR-0111 revision note).** The first cut used a debounced auto-suggest combobox (query per keystroke, list auto-opened on focus) plus a bespoke `.ksic-suggest-list` CSS component, which the design review checklist forbids (no implicit new components/tokens; every on-screen element must trace to a standard pattern). Reworked to the app's established explicit search-button precedent: input + magnifier search button (query fires on click, results cleared when the input changes), results rendered in the existing `sizing-note-list` listbox with the same selection semantics (aria-pressed + check-mark + semibold), and the ad-hoc CSS removed entirely. The table's inline KSIC editor reverts to the plain ADR-0082 row-edit input (Enter-to-save standard); the search picker lives in the create form where look-up belongs. No new tokens or components; design-lint green.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **The /market KSIC column no longer shows fabricated `NEW-xxxxxx` placeholder codes (ADR-0111, migration 0078).** The create route stored `NEW-<ULID-last-6>` into `md_industry.ksic_code` whenever the optional field was left blank (the column was NOT NULL + UNIQUE), so the analysis list rendered internal ID fragments like `NEW-FXENCH` as if they were KSIC codes. Migration 0078 (`@notransaction` table rebuild, auto-applied at server boot) makes `ksic_code` nullable (SQLite's unique index allows multiple NULLs), nulls every existing `NEW-*` value, and keeps the load-bearing `HYD-BRK` freeze sentinel untouched (insights bottom-up CAGR supplement and the /market frozen banner depend on it). Unspecified industries now render an em-dash; a same-name industry no longer silently drops the newly typed code (fill-or-update); Prisma schema aligned to `String?`.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **S3 company-profile analysis failures are now reported instead of hanging or failing silently.** The pipeline ran fire-and-forget with only a console.error on failure while the profile GET derived status from version existence alone, so a crashed run left the client polling `분석 중`... forever with no diagnosis path. An in-process job registry (startCompanyProfilePipeline: running/failed + reason, cleared on success) is now recorded at analyze time and surfaced by the profile GET as status:"failed" + `error`; the `/our-company` polling reports it as an error banner and releases the stuck analyzing state. `completeOrFallback` now logs the swallowed LLM error before falling back (a live run had both LLM calls fail transiently while DART + 12 research sources succeeded, leaving zero diagnostic trace). GET empty-body retries now back off (400ms/1.2s) so dev recompile windows are actually skipped, and the analyze POST retries once on `invalid_response` (starting the pipeline is idempotent). 4 new route-handler tests pin the in_progress/failed/completed/crash registry contract; unit suite + tsc green.
- **[2026-09-09]**: fix(web-next): **API client no longer surfaces raw browser JSON-parse errors on empty responses.** After the Bun 1.4.0→1.4.2 upgrade a recurring Turbopack persistent-cache panic forced dev-server restarts, and a request killed inside the cold-compile window came back with an empty body; `lib/api.ts` unconditionally called `res.json()`, so the raw "Unexpected end of JSON input" TypeError was surfaced verbatim in page error banners. `request()` now converts empty/malformed bodies into a readable `invalid_response` ApiError ("Server response is empty or corrupted - please try again shortly.") and retries once for idempotent GETs. Also fixed the `/our-company` analyze flow leaving the member button stuck on the analyzing label forever when the analyze POST fails (loading state is now cleared in the catch; button label was stuck on the Korean "analyzing" text). Verified all nine `/our-company` API endpoints return well-formed envelopes (cold compiles included); tsc green.
- **[2026-09-04]**: fix(nav): **Projects nav item relocated into the process flow, empty Operations section removed.** Per feedback, `/projects` moved from a standalone Operations group to the Discovery nav section, immediately after Target Candidate Discovery (S7) — the natural next step once a target is promoted to a project (S8+). The now-empty Operations section was removed and the header comment updated accordingly.
- **[2026-09-04]**: fix(nav): **Left navigation panel had no entry point to the S8–S11 project screens.** The Operations nav section rendered empty and the project-keyed screens (business-case, dd, ic-review, portfolio) were reachable only via deep links buried on the /opportunities page. Added `GET /api/projects` (deny-by-default via `listVisibleProjects`) and a new `/projects` list page (project number, track chip, source-opportunity link, quick links to all four S8–S11 screens per row); added a Projects nav item under Operations in `AppShell.tsx` pointing to it, and corrected the stale NAV_SECTIONS header comment that still described the S4+ surface as pending rebuild. 3 new tests (anon 401, member sees own project, non-member sees none). Unit suite 69 files, tsc, design:lint, audit green.
- **[2026-09-04]**: fix(review): **Comprehensive S8–S11 verification pass — 19 findings fixed (4 major integrity defects + design conformance) and documentation reconciled.** Major: (1) the auto pipeline's dd-gate rule was dead code (`currentStage === "S9"` can never hold on a completed stage) — dd gate is now correctly surfaced before entity_ic (regression test added); (2) `growth_rate` was double-converted (ratio convention `/100`) silently understating every S8 valuation anchor; (3) committee-session routes now bind sessionId to the path opportunity (404), reject agent-seat impersonation (403) and duplicate same-round positions (409); (4) business-case POST/PUT line writes are transactional. Minor: pipeline start/advance race guards, waiver_id preserved on re-judgment (COALESCE), scoped line queries, track-aware hybrid committee rounds, gate_evaluation provenance bound to the entity, idempotent generate/tailor (`alreadyRun`), Phase 0 role-403 test. Design (Onyx 3.0 audit): `window.prompt` in CommitteePanel replaced with a proper form + success notices, missing S9 deep link added, 3px focus ring restored, StageFrame applied to business-case, `.mono-num` on financial figures, NPV unit label unified to Korean million-KRW on both pages, scenario chips neutral (not failure-red), `.btn-danger` de-hardcoded to tokens. Docs: `docs/process-flow.md` regenerated with an addendum covering the engine, per-stage automation surfaces, committee system, pipeline, and deferred/env-seamed capabilities (generator 1.2.0; byte-equality drift test green). All found via a 3-agent verification pass (opus code review, web runtime + design audit, plan-fidelity audit) after PRs #191–#199.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(pipeline): **End-to-end auto pipeline — industry+company selection drives the process, pausing only at human gates (ADR-0102, Phase 7, final phase of the plan).** Migration 0072 adds `pipeline_run` (entity-addressed, industry/company FKs, status running/paused_for_human/completed/failed/killed) and row-preservingly rebuilds `notification` (project_id nullable; `trigger_type` gains `pipeline_paused`). New `lib/pipeline.ts`: `startPipeline` (validates industry+company; one active run per entity) and `advancePipeline` — an explicit ordered rule table mapping evaluateProcess blockers to the existing automation entry points (`generateBusinessCases`, `tailorDdChecklist`, agent_team committee convene as PROPOSAL, `promoteOpportunity`) with an ineffective-automation guard and a 10-iteration cap; any human-confirmation blocker pauses with `paused_reason` + a `pipeline_paused` notification deep-linked to the action screen. Promotion closes the opportunity segment (completed at S7) and opens the project segment append-only. Resume rides the existing watch-run cycle (`runPipelineResumePass`, no new scheduler; "no change = no action"). `POST .../pipeline/start` + `GET .../pipeline/status` (analyst+; deny-by-default), `PipelineStatus` widget on the dashboard (auto-progress / pending-confirmation badges with deep links), read-only MCP `get_pipeline_status`. Boundary #2 by construction and regression-tested (15 tests): the rule table contains only generation functions — a full run writes zero gate/adoption/approval rows. Notable fix: `evaluateProcess` walks S1–S11 for opportunities too, so gate rules key on pre-gate artifact relevance and promotion requires the full opportunity scope settled. Unit suite 66 files, tsc, design:lint, audit green.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(s11): **S11 post-management — portfolio scorecard view and venture transition decisions complete the S8–S11 chain (ADR-0101, Phase 6, final phase of the plan).** Migration 0071 adds append-only `venture_transition_decision` (5-vocabulary CHECK matching flow.spec/PORTFOLIO_TRANSITIONS, `decided_by` pinned to the human role `venture-portfolio-ic` per ADR-0053, mandatory rationale, kill requires `learning_capture`, `run_id` recorder lineage — no recorded_by column; recorder tracked via run.created_by). `GET /api/projects/[id]/scorecard` serves the repository's `getPortfolioRollup` directly (no extraction/duplication) with `?review_cycle` pinning, per-cycle trend, and decision history; `POST .../transition-decision` is the human-only recorder surface (analyst+, server-side vocabulary + kill-capture validation, audit + access logs). New `/projects/[id]/portfolio` StageFrame page — quarterly trend, class rollup with insufficient-evidence exceptions, append-only decision ledger, and an explicitly labeled venture-portfolio-IC decision panel (useConfirm; Onyx 3.0 tokens, design:lint clean). process-flow.ts S11 predicate upgraded to latest-cycle freshness (current or previous quarter — documented choice), retiring the LAST REFINEMENT placeholder: **all S8–S11 stage predicates are now real**. MCP read wrapper `get_portfolio_scorecard` (POST deliberately unexposed). 10+ new tests (rollup, 403/404, vocabulary enforcement, kill-capture rule, append-only, freshness completion); unit suite 65 files, tsc, design:lint, audit green.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(s10): **S10 investment screening & execution — IC package, dual IC gates, and project-keyed establishment (ADR-0100, Phase 5 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0070 backfills `entity_establishment_step.project_id` from the opportunity backlink (additive; opportunity_id stays the origin of record). New shared `establishment.ts` read/write model (backlink fallback for pre-backfill rows; step-5 sequence enforcement; the upsert ADOPTS a matching legacy row in place rather than duplicating it, so the opportunity view's 5-step completion cannot double-count). `GET /api/projects/[id]/ic-package` aggregates the IC reviewer's package — adopted business case + simulations, DD completion + findings, RAG surfaced as `{ grade, isProposal: true }` (boundary #2/#4: proposal, never a PASS stamp), entity_ic/group_ic history, establishment n/5. `GET/PUT .../establishment` mirrors the opportunity route (analyst+, sequence enforcement, audit). New `/projects/[id]/ic-review` StageFrame page — IC summary, GateDecisionPanel ×2 (entity_ic, group_ic, project entity), 5-step table with P5 signal chips and useConfirm-gated step updates (Onyx 3.0 tokens, design:lint clean); S10 deep link added. process-flow.ts S10 predicate now reads establishment progress via direct project_id (backlink fallback). Legacy `investment_decision`/`portfolio_entry` (0057) confirmed write-less since ADR-0078 and formally deprecated in ADR-0100 (no drop — additive-only). MCP read wrapper `get_project_ic_package`. 11 new tests (backfill, aggregation, sequence enforcement, RAG-as-proposal, completion flip); unit suite 64 files, tsc, design:lint, audit green.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(s9): **S9 due diligence with external-search tailored DD checklists (ADR-0099, Phase 4 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0069 adds `project_checklist_item_custom` (domain commercial/technical/legal, provenance `source_url` + `source_reliability` A/B/C reusing the md_source vocabulary, status proposed/approved/rejected). New `dd-checklist-tailor.ts` runs a per-domain `runActivityChain` (commercial-dd-agent / technical-dd-agent / legal-counsel) proposing 5–10 industry-tailored items per domain under one auditable `process_run`; **honesty constraint**: the deterministic default sets `source_url=null`/reliability `C` — an unsourced proposal renders as an explicit "unsourced (C)" chip, never as a sourced one (no fabricated URLs; WebSearch-backed executor is an env seam `CO_NEWBIZ_DD_TAILOR_AGENTS`). Boundary #2: items land only in `proposed`; `PUT .../dd-checklist/tailor/[itemId]/review` (analyst+) is the sole approval path, and approval inserts the `project_checklist_item_state` row (`custom:<domain>`) in one transaction so approved items ride the ordinary waiver/judgment tracking. New `/projects/[id]/dd` StageFrame page (3 standard checklist tabs with P5 signal chips, tailored-proposal panel, dd_finding list, GateDecisionPanel gate=dd; Onyx 3.0 tokens only — design:lint clean), plus judgment-state and aggregate dd APIs and a read-only MCP tool. process-flow.ts S9 placeholder replaced with the flow.spec-verbatim predicate (all items judged AND mandatory-unmet 0, counting approved customs through the state table). Also: propagated workspace skills (handbook, k-opendata, handbook-sync-audit, meeting, platform lifecycle, commit-PR skills) registered and metadata-completed (SCRIPTS.md +26 scripts, status frontmatter, relates_to/owner fixes) so the audit battery is green across the board. Unit suite 63 files, tsc, validators, design:lint, audit all pass.
- **[2026-09-04]**: feat(committee): **Standing functional-expert seats on both steering committees (ADR-0097 Amendment).** Six domain seats added per user direction — financing (`financing-agent`, new), site & facility (`site-facility-agent`, new), legal (`legal-counsel`, existing), HR/labor (`hr-integration-agent`, existing), IR (`ir-agent`, new), patent/IP (`ip-agent`, new). Four new agent definitions (Medium tier, functional domain, referencing skills without restating steps) plus their lifecycle governance records; both committees in `procedures/_committees.yaml` extended (route-steering 12 members, target-longlist-shortlist 11 — brownfield-only skeleton has no greenfield track seat, documented); risk-officer remains the sole `veto_dissent`; deterministic executor gained templates for all new keys (and the previously missing legal-counsel/hr-integration-agent). AGENTS.md roster 34→38; committee composition regression tests added; round-1-only participation for functional seats deferred (engine semantics, noted in ADR amendment). Validators, graph-map, unit suite, tsc, audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(s8): **S8 feasibility analysis with automatic multi-business-case generation (ADR-0098, Phase 3 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0068 adds `valuation_simulation` (project/model FK, scenario base/upside/downside, percentile, NPV, IRR) — persistence for sensitivity output. New `business-case-generator.ts` runs ADR-0096's `runActivityChain` per scenario (financial-analyst growth_projection → valuation-agent valuation_model → quant-methodologist distributional_valuation + scenario_risk_assessment; 12 activities under one auditable `process_run`) with a seeded deterministic Monte Carlo default (FNV-1a seed per project:scenario, ±20% growth/dispersion deltas — the 3 cases carry genuinely distinct NPV distributions, seeds recorded in notes per ADR-0052 §D.4) and an env-seamed pluggable executor. quant-methodologist flags a RECOMMENDED case, but adoption is human-only: `POST .../business-case/adopt` (analyst+) is the sole adoption path (boundary #2, regression-tested: generation writes no adoption/fs-gate records). New project-keyed surface — `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE .../business-case` (manual CRUD coexists), `.../generate` + `.../generate/[runId]` progress, project-entity `.../gate-decisions` (GateDecisionPanel gains an `entityType` prop), and `/projects/[id]/business-case` comparison page (3 cards, RECOMMENDED/adopted badges, useConfirm-gated adopt, GateDecisionPanel gates=["fs"]); opportunity page deep-links when promoted. process-flow.ts S8 placeholder replaced with the flow.spec-verbatim check (case ≥1 AND matching valuation_simulation ≥1). Unit suite 62 files, tsc, migrations, and audit green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(committee): **Steering Committee deliberation — systematic agent-team review for S4 route decision and S6/S7 Long List→Short List, in three decision modes (ADR-0097, Phase 2 of the S8–S11 plan).** New `procedures/_committees.yaml` governance registry (sibling of _human-roles.yaml) encodes the 5-role committee skeleton derived from the 4-domain lead system and the cross-validation matrix — domain expert (track agent), industry-lead, region-lead, quantitative rigor (market-intelligence/valuation-agent), red team (risk-officer, `veto_dissent: true`, mandatory on every committee — omission is a validator ERROR), and chair (process-governance-manager, recorder pattern: synthesizes, never decides). Two committees registered (`route-steering-committee`, `target-longlist-shortlist-committee`), both rounds 2, modes human_only/agent_team/hybrid. Committee sessions ride ADR-0096's generalized `process_run` (+ additive `meta_json`, migration 0067) with `agent_position` per-round transcripts; synthesis produces a PROPOSED `gate_evaluation` with risk-officer dissent preserved verbatim in `dissent_json`. New committee-sessions API (convene analyst+ / positions / advance / transcript), `CommitteePanel` mounted beside GateDecisionPanel on /route-decision and /brownfield (embedded human confirm form, agent_team stops at PROPOSED), optional `gate_evaluation_id` back-compat linkage on the existing decision routes, a real `/meeting` implementation over the same engine (transcript to memory/), and read-only MCP wrappers. Boundary #2 regression suite (6 tests): an agent_team session end-to-end writes zero gate_decision/route_decision/brownfield_target rows until a human confirms with `gate_evaluation_id`. LLM execution is a pluggable seam (deterministic role-templated default; `CO_NEWBIZ_COMMITTEE_AGENTS` env). Unit suite 61 files, tsc, procedure validator, graph-map --check, and audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(orchestration): **Orchestration engine generalization — one shared deliberation/generation layer for the S4–S9 automation consumers (ADR-0096, Phase 1 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0064 generalizes `process_run` addressing from project-only to `entity_type`('opportunity'|'project')+`entity_id` via row-preserving rebuild (legacy rows preserved as project rows, `project_id` kept nullable for compatibility); 0065 adds human seats to `agent_position` (`member_type`, `human_user_id`, `agent_key` nullable — exactly-one-of enforced app-layer); 0066 adds `gate_evaluation.dissent_json` plus `gate_evaluation_id` provenance links on `gate_decision`/`route_decision`/`brownfield_target`. `agent-orchestrator.ts` is rewritten from dead code into two layers: `runActivityChain()` (sequential generation — future S8 case / S9 checklist consumers) and `runDeliberationRounds()` (multi-round rebuttal debate; round 2+ agents receive the full verbatim prior-round positions of all other members; positions recorded per round). Chair synthesis produces a PROPOSED `gate_evaluation` only — the 0045 `decision` table stays unused (gate_decision remains the sole human-confirmation ledger per ADR-0094; boundary #2 untouched), and veto-dissent positions are preserved verbatim in `dissent_json`, never averaged away. LLM execution is an injected `callAgent` strategy so Phases 2–4 supply real implementations. 17 new unit tests (incl. round-2 cross-context assertion and legacy-row preservation); unit suite, tsc, migrations, and audit all green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(promotion): **Project promotion foundation — opportunities can now enter the S8–S11 project lifecycle (ADR-0095, Phase 0 of the S8–S11 plan).** Migration 0063 adds `md_project.origin` / `source_opportunity_id` / `track` (snapshot from the latest route_decision) and `opportunity.promoted_at`; `promoteOpportunity()` in project.repository.ts performs the whole transition in one transaction — project insert, opportunity backlink, mandatory lead `grantProjectMembership` (a zero-member project is permanently invisible under the deny-by-default model), and audit. New `POST /api/opportunities/[id]/promote` (analyst+, mandatory need_to_know_basis) enforces the preconditions: greenfield requires a completed S5 assessment, brownfield requires an S7 Short List. process-flow.ts extends StageId/STAGES to S8–S11 with flow.spec definitions ported verbatim and implements the project branch of evaluateProcess (simple schema checks, refined per-phase later). Covered by 7 new unit tests (success, double-promotion, precondition failures, empty basis); full unit suite, tsc, and audit green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(db): **Single database regardless of working directory.** The default DB path was process-cwd-relative (`db/data/co-newbiz.sqlite`), so running the web server from `app/web-next` while CLI scripts ran from the repo root silently maintained two divergent databases (the indirect cause of a data-loss incident on 2026-09-03). db/client.ts now anchors the default to the repository layout — resolving from this file's location with fallbacks for Next/Turbopack bundling (import.meta.dirname → dir → file URL → walk-up for db/migrations) — and exports DB_DIR, which db/migrate.ts reuses so MIGRATIONS_DIR also survives bundling. Live data was consolidated into `<repo>/db/data/co-newbiz.sqlite` via VACUUM INTO (safe against a live WAL DB); the stale app/web-next/db/data copy was archived. Verified: server health + admin login against the unified DB, identical account list from both cwds, full unit suite green (tests keep using CO_NEWBIZ_DB_PATH).
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(bootstrap): **First-boot database bootstrap — fresh clones get a working login.** The SQLite DB is gitignored, so a fresh clone previously started the server with zero tables and zero accounts (no way to log in; migrations were manual-only). New `app/web-next/instrumentation.ts` runs on server start: applies pending migrations, and when no account exists, creates an initial `admin` (random password printed once to the console, `must_change_password=1`, self-approved and audited with actor `bootstrap`); subsequent boots are idempotent (skip when any account exists). README Quick Start now documents the fresh-clone path (`bun install` → `bun run dev`) and the manual `newbiz.ts db:migrate / user:create / user:approve` alternative, plus the cwd-relative default DB path caveat (running from `app/web-next` is the supported layout).
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(auth,admin): **Password-change flow repaired + forced first-login change + user-delete FK fix.** (A) The must-change-password flow reported after an admin reset actually failed because the UI posted to a missing endpoint shape and gave no field guidance: /password-change now has current(temporary)/new/confirm-new-password fields with client-side mismatch detection and an explicit explanation that the temporary password cannot be reused as the new one (the password_history reuse block was being mistaken for a bug); the dead `/projects` redirect is now `/`. (B) Voluntary self-service change: the topbar gains a password-change action reusing the same page (the backend POST /api/auth/password-change already existed and is covered by tests). (C) createUser now sets must_change_password=1 — admin-created and self-registered accounts start with a communicated initial password, so the first login forces the change flow (verified E2E: create → login mustChangePassword=true → same-value change correctly refused by the reuse block → different new password lifts the gate and revokes old sessions). Seeded test accounts clear the flag explicitly. (D) Fixed admin user deletion 500: sessions/api_tokens/password_history rows are now removed before the app_user row (enforced FKs); verified live. Full unit suite green.
- **[2026-09-03]**: fix(test,quality): **Unit-suite restoration + S1–S7 spec-enforcement hardening (post-ADR-0094 audit).** (A) Unit suite green again (was 9/59 files failing): stale tests over the ADR-0094-deleted `/api/projects/**` surface rewritten/deleted (api.test.ts re-pointed to the live opportunity surface with security guards ported — 401/viewer-403/invalid-score; admin-deal-block.test.ts deleted, no live analogue since opportunities are open-read by design); MCP tool catalog reconciled (14 write-tool names ending in a leaked `✱` marker fixed, ~40 tools relaying deleted REST routes removed — 13 live tools remain, `projectId` args renamed `opportunityId`); docs/architecture/openapi.yaml drift gate satisfied (17 undocumented live routes added); skill-graph procedure→skill edge fixed (competitive-intelligence gains `company-profile-analysis`); migration-0013 shape pin updated for 0047's appended `company_id`; verify-skills-guard re-pinned to the `isLegacyIndex` structure. (B) Spec-enforcement fixes in evaluateProcess: S3 completion now requires the opportunity to be `selected` (flow.spec S3 criterion previously unenforced — scores+rationale alone completed the stage) and `dropped` opportunities can no longer proceed to S4; `nextStage` now skips track-inapplicable (skipped) stages (brownfield S4 → S6, not S5). (C) Screening gate surface: GateDecisionPanel (`gates={["screening"]}`) mounted on /synergy shortlist tab — flow.spec attaches screening to S1–S3 but no earlier surface recorded it. (D) Audit completeness: appendAudit added to the previously un-audited mutating routes (opportunity discover/sketch/factors, greenfield assessment save, brownfield target update/screening/shortlist). (E) DRY: latest-per-gate and latest-route-decision queries extracted to exported helpers in lib/process-flow.ts and reused by the gate-decisions API so tie-breaking cannot drift. (F) Environment: python/*.py runners (fin-pipeline/report/fin-analyze/driver-tree/simulate) now prefer the project `.venv` (python/requirements.txt: pandas, python-docx, reportlab, python-hwpx, PyYAML) over the PEP 668 system python; OFL NanumGothic TTF pair dropped into python/report/fonts/ so document rendering works on non-Windows machines (docx test de-hardcoded from Malgun Gothic); brownfield Long List fragment-key React warning fixed.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,s1): **ADR-0092 — S1 market-sizing schema extension + S1~S4 consistency fixes.** (A) SizingMatrix gains optional research fields (zero DB migration, ADR-0077 precedent): anchor source methodology/sourceUrl/asOf, demandPipeline entries (tier 1-4), competitive player techType/capacityMWe/projectStatus, regulatoryComparison, sectionConfidence, killCriteriaProposals, verificationLedger, ragCapRecommendation. (B) Migration 0061 adds market_sizing_analysis status failed + error_message (table-rebuild pattern); pipeline and POST analyses catch failures into the terminal failed state instead of stuck in_progress. - **[2026-09-03]**: feat(admin): **ADR-0091 — admin console split + role-separated navigation + system dashboard.** (A) Role-screen separation: an `admin` account sees only the management group (AppShell renders an admin-only navigation); non-admins never see the admin entry. The dead `label.startsWith("S")` hide-logic is removed. (B) Single-page 10-tab console split into per-section routes (`/admin/users|audit|data-sources|schedule|database|notifications|glossary|governance|master-data|adr`) over `app/admin/layout.tsx` (admin gate) with the shared `AdminConsole` section component; section state lives in the URL. ### Changed

- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,our-company): **Dynamic competitive attributes + S3 table/layout fixes + S1 request button.** (A) S1 competitive players gain industry-specific `attributes` [{label,value}] (pipeline prompt defines labels per industry, e.g. service scope / licensing / main markets for power maintenance) rendered as dynamic columns; legacy seeded techType/capacityMWe/projectStatus columns still render when present. (B) S3 financials tab: DART raw-KRW values are normalized to hundred-million-won in the derived trend chart (previously rendered trillion-scale axis labels), and the ratios table is wrapped in table-scroll (previously overflowed, cutting the debt-ratio/ROE columns). (C) S3 industry-linkage table: natural-width columns + per-row "S1 request analysis" button (analyst+) that starts the S1 8-step pipeline for industries without a market matrix, showing an in-progress chip with a /market report link; viewer-role requests surface the server 403 message. (D) Versions-and-notes tab removed per user feedback (version data remains in payload). Verified in-browser: chart units, table alignment, dynamic columns on a live power-maintenance S1 run, request-button create/link flow.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(our-company,s3): **ADR-0093 — company-profile expansion to S1 level.** (A) Payload v2 (TEXT JSON extension, zero migration, all v1 field names preserved so synergy's loadLatestProfile reuse is unaffected): financialAnalysis (deterministic ratios/trends from DART accounts via new lib/company-financials.ts), swot (opportunities/threats), competitive (LLM positioning step, degraded-safe), industryLinkage (each exposure entry matched to md_industry and its latest completed S1 matrix: anchor, CAGR, external-forecast gap, qualitative factors), sectionConfidence, verificationLedger, ragCapRecommendation, schemaVersion 2. (B) Pipeline 4 -> 6 steps: step 3 prompt extended to SWOT; new step 4 competitive positioning (never blocks the save); new step 5 deterministic synthesis (ratios, linkage, confidence grades from evidence class, verification ledger — uploaded credit report explicitly recorded as not-yet-parsed, RAG cap amber-until-analyst-confirmation); save becomes step 6. (C) /our-company report restructured 3 -> 7 tabs (overview / financials / SWOT / industry linkage / competitive / sources & confidence / versions & step notes); version history and step notes the API already returned are now rendered; all new sections conditional so pre-expansion payloads render the legacy subset with a re-analysis hint. (D) Governance: ADR-0093, procedure schema.yaml v1.1.0 (company_profile_competitive + company_profile_synthesis output types), seed route explicitly deferred.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,opportunities): **Industry-adaptive competitive table + opportunity deletion.** (A) /market competitive tab renders the tech-type/capacity-MWe/project-status columns only when at least one player carries those ADR-0092 optional fields (SMR-style project industries); generic manufacturing matrices like hydraulic breaker no longer show all-dash columns. (B) New DELETE /api/opportunities/[id] (analyst+) with guards — promoted (project-linked) opportunities and those referenced by route decisions, target lists, feasibility assessments, or synergy analyses are refused — plus a per-row delete button with confirmation on /opportunities for cleaning up test/erroneous registrations.
- **[2026-09-03]**: feat(market,skills): **Pipeline adoption of ADR-0092 extension fields + deterministic outlier rule (ADR-0092 decision 6, systematic-market-sizing skill v1.5.0).** (A) The sizing pipeline now generates the extension fields itself — demand pipeline (web-researched, tiered with source URLs), regulatory comparison (confirmedDirectly only behind primary legal sources), verification ledger (deterministically derived from step-6 cross-validation verdicts), kill-criteria proposals, per-section confidence grades (evidence-class based), and a mechanical Amber RAG cap for llm-estimate matrices — so re-run analyses fill every tab the seed path fills. (B) Step 2 deterministic outlier exclusion: sources beyond 3x / below 1/3 of the median estimate are mechanically excluded and the anchor is recomputed from the remaining consensus sources only (estimating-party totals no longer trusted); anchor sources now carry methodology/sourceUrl/asOf and the /market size tab renders them. (C) Step 5 retrieves a public top-down consensus CAGR instead of hard-coding topDownCagr to 0 (unavailable recorded as unavailable). (D) Overview connected-driver counts read each industry's real matrix cell count instead of hardcoded 18. (E) /market regulation-tab table no longer compressed (report-table col-label/col-type/col-text min-widths per ADR-0088).

The OpenDART ZIP is STREAMING (local header sizes are 0, data descriptors follow the data) — parseCorpXml now handles data-descriptor entries by scanning to the next PK signature (slip/bomb guards retained: 50MB cap, 100x ratio, single-file check). - **[2026-08-31]**: feat(adr,skills,scripts): **ADR-0075 pipeline execution environment discipline.** (A) New ADR-0075 + design doc (`docs/designs/2026-08-31-pipeline-execution-environment.md`) codifying the 2026-08-31 pipeline lesson: each tool shell starts with an empty environment, the project `.env` is CRLF so secrets must load via `set -a; source <(tr -d '\r' < .env); set +a` in the same invocation, and an "API key missing" report requires a reproducible curl probe before it may lower data confidence. (B) New pre-flight script `scripts/co-newbiz/env-check.ts` (SCRIPTS.md-registered, v1.0.0): verifies the four required API keys (names only, values never printed), reports CRLF contamination with the canonical load preamble, `--exports` emits CRLF-stripped shell-safe export lines. (C) Skills k-dart 2.1.0 / k-law 1.1.0 / k-kosis 1.1.0: Verify Prerequisites step 1 now mandates per-invocation normalized loading, the env-check pre-flight, and curl-evidence before concluding a key is absent. (D) Project memory: `memory/pipeline-s1-s7-execution-procedure.md` created (S1–S7 standing execution procedure with §4 environment loading) and MEMORY.md ADR index updated.
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(market,opportunities,skills): **Chain-direction analysis (upstream vs downstream), qualitative opportunity/risk factors, and an industry-list-first S2.** (A) Chain-direction rule (skill v1.3.0 step 1 + pipeline): the analysis direction now follows the industry's value-chain position — downstream/end-product industries analyze forward industries (their demand side) while upstream/raw-material industries analyze backward industries (their supply side); the pipeline's step 1 asks the LLM for the position (upstream/midstream/downstream) and 3–5 adjacent industries with relation labels, rendered as a new table in market step 1. Verified with a rare-earths (upstream) analysis correctly producing backward (supplier-side) industries. (B) Qualitative factors: the pipeline also generates opportunity and risk factors (demand triggers, regulation, technology shifts vs supply constraints, substitutes, geopolitics), rendered as a new 5-A block in the market report and included in the S2 insights. (C) Opportunities (/opportunities) restructured to the same select-then-insight flow as /market: an analyzed-industry chip list (CAGR + gap % per chip) opens a per-industry insight card combining quantitative metrics (bottom-up CAGR, external forecast, gap %) with the qualitative opportunity/risk factors; the funnel board remains below. (D) Step 1 relabeled to the plainer market-structure-definition wording than the previous proxy-jargon title — with the axes-table label column widened (nowrap) to fix line-breaking. (E) Linked-driver semantics clarified and extended: industries without deal-linked chain_node_sensitivity rows (pipeline-created industries) now derive 18 region×segment demand-sensitivity nodes (cell CAGR × 10, the seed script's placeholder scale) from the completed analysis payload, so the driver count and the step-1 sensitivity chart render for every analyzed industry.
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(s2,s3): **Research-backed S2 qualitative analysis + new S3 synergy stage (company-linked Short List) + project company requirement.** (A) S2: the market pipeline qualitative step now runs web research (TAVILY_API_KEY — global org reports, broker notes, statistics, news), generates opportunity/risk factors grounded in the retrieved excerpts, and records the retrieved sources with links (source citation mandatory; renders as an expandable source list in market 5-A). (B) S3 synergy-alignment stage: migration 0047 adds synergy_analysis + append-only synergy_version and md_project.company_id; new /synergy page (sidebar between opportunity discovery and deal list) registers a target company and runs a 5-step pipeline (lib/synergy-pipeline.ts): DART financial-statement ingest for listed/externally-audited companies (corp-code bulk lookup + fnlttSinglAcntAll; graceful skip for non-listed), external research with mandatory source citation and cross-checking, LLM financial strength/weakness analysis, S1+S2 linkage analysis (which market areas yield business opportunity and whether the company can create synergy — Long List to Short List), and a ranked Short List. New APIs POST/GET /api/synergy/analyses(+/:id). (C) Project registration now requires company_name (both POST /api/projects and opportunity promote) — the target company is created/linked in md_company and stamped on md_project so S4-S7 run against a named company. (D) Root-.env pipeline keys (CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_*, DART_API_KEY, TAVILY_API_KEY) are auto-loaded for the web server; LLM anthropic requests disable reasoning blocks (GLM) to prevent empty responses. Verified live: cobalt/robot matrices regenerated, rare-earths upstream chain-direction analysis, Samsung Electronics synergy run (12 web sources cited, 5 synergy areas, ranked Short List with AI-memory/HBM supply-chain pick).
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(process,market): **Brownfield route expansion + S5 Target Long/Short List screening + flow-spec branch alignment.** (1) Brownfield ≠ M&A (meta-model v2): migration 0049 widens route_decision.sub_track to brownfield routes (m_and_a / jv / strategic_investment / minority_investment / partnership / rbd / corporate_venture) — M&A is one route among seven; the S4 route form gains a Brownfield-route select, and process/flow.spec.yaml S4 objective/outputs/completionCriteria record the chosen route type. (2) S5 renamed to business-viability-and-target-selection: the brownfield branch now runs Target Universe → Long List → screening → Short List via the new targets API (POST /api/projects/:id/targets — add_candidates / promote_to_shortlist / set_primary on project_company) and a TargetScreeningPanel in the workflow tab; set_primary stamps md_project.company_id so DD (S6) and IC (S7) proceed against the selected company. Greenfield keeps Business Case with feasibility (not DD) wording at S6. (3) /opportunities: opportunities are registered FROM the selected analyzed industry (market scope auto-linked, analysis CAGR pre-applied) instead of a free-form form; the promote form branches by track — brownfield requires the target-candidate Long List, greenfield needs no target object. (4) systematic-market-sizing skill v1.4.0 documents the meta-model v2 branch structure (Brownfield routes, Target Company as the brownfield core object, Greenfield capability path, Project as the shared execution unit). flow spec integrity tests 10/10 pass.
### Fixed
- **[2026-08-31]**: feat(market,skills): **Full-matrix pipeline reports, re-analysis with versioning, and external-forecast gap analysis bridged into S2.** (A) The sizing pipeline now generates a complete `SizingMatrix` (anchor sources, region weights/mixes, per-cell baselines reconciled to the anchor, per-cell CAGRs with documented band overrides, consistency check, filing cross-validation candidates, limitations) via LLM JSON steps — pipeline-generated industries render the SAME rich 8-step report with charts as the frozen HYD-BRK analysis (no more summary-card-only view), banner-flagged `llm-estimate` pending analyst confirmation. (B) Re-analysis: new `POST /api/market-overview/analyses/:id/rerun` flips a completed analysis back to in_progress and lands the result as version N+1 (append-only — prior versions preserved so decisions can cite the exact version); the landing table adds a re-analyze action and an update-recommended status when the analysis date goes stale (>90 days), covering both failed-run recovery and periodic refresh. (C) External forecast gap analysis (step 5-B): when a public future estimate is obtainable, the pipeline records source/target-year size/external CAGR, computes our extrapolation for the same year and the gap %, and produces a driver-gap analysis listing new/strengthened drivers that explain why the external view diverges from historical-CAGR extrapolation; without one the CAGR-extrapolation method is kept unchanged. Rendered as a dedicated block in step 5 of the market report and bridged to S2 via a new market-insight panel on /opportunities (bottom-up CAGR, gap %, gap analysis, new-driver chips per industry). (D) Methodology/docs feedback: `systematic-market-sizing` skill v1.2.0 (step 5-B, re-analysis/versioning policy, gap-aware S2 input guidance; mirrored into skills/ so the flow spec gate passes), `process/flow.spec.yaml` S1 gains the gap-analysis output + skill reference with `docs/process-flow.md` regenerated, and `docs/architecture/ui-spec.md` gains the /market as-built note.
### Fixed
- **[2026-08-31]**: fix(llm): **Root-.env LLM config now reaches the web server + reasoning-model token fix; verified live with z.ai GLM.** (1) `lib/llm.ts` walks up from `process.cwd()` (the bundled server chunks make `import.meta.dir` unreliable) to import CO_NEWBIZ_LLM_* keys from the repo-root .env when they are absent from the environment — operators configure once at the root and the Next server (which otherwise only loads app/web-next/.env) picks it up; explicitly-set env vars are never overridden. (2) Anthropic-compatible URL resolution now appends `/v1/messages` to a bare base URL (e.g. z.ai's `https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic`). (3) Reasoning models (GLM) can spend the whole max_tokens budget on thinking blocks and return an empty text part — an empty extraction now throws and triggers the existing retry loop with a 4× token budget. Verified live: smoke-test reply + a full 8-step pipeline analysis (cobalt) generated with LLM summaries throughout (LLM-summary provenance chips, ~60s end-to-end); the analysis-date column and LLM configuration note were confirmed on the /market landing.

- **[2026-08-30]**: feat(process): **Process redesign — S1-S7 stage model over the 6-gate system, with an Opportunity funnel above projects (process metamodel in docs/process-flow.md).** Stage is not Gate, Opportunity is not Project, Artifact is not Decision — enforced as invariants: stages execute, gates decide (GO/HOLD/KILL as recorded `gate_decision` rows — HOLD requires a missing-artifacts list; re-evaluation clears it by computation, not hand-editing); `current_stage` is never stored — `evaluateProcess(entity)` (lib/process-flow.ts) derives the full state from artifacts/opportunities/route/gates, for two lifecycles (Opportunity S1-S4 then promote; Project S5-S7 to the IC decision; Execute is an entry point to the existing PMI/monitoring lifecycles, not a new engine). Two genuine gaps confirmed by direct code inspection and flagged in a new design-doc addendum (§20) and in `skills/agent-output-provenance/SKILL.md` (1.1.0, corrected: the `analysis_task` table DOES exist since migration 0011 and is already read by the kill-criteria engine, but no code path ever writes a row into it, so the "unreviewed count" kill-criteria check is currently vacuously true rather than a real gate): (1) the 3-stage-review persistence layer (`review_stage` table, `reviewer_domain` column) is schema-only with no repository/API/UI consumer anywhere; (2) human-final gate approval (`analysis_task.reviewed_by`) has never been exercised on any of the running pilots (002-004, 011-014) — axis C's "real deal review" claim currently means AI 1st/2nd-stage review only. - **[2026-08-28]**: feat(web): **ROIC projection v1 (ADR-0018 base-rate anchoring) — the last engine-pending Model-tab item now has a real, honest implementation.** `GET /api/projects/:id/roic-tree` now derives a per-fiscal-year historical ROIC series from `fact_industry` (previously only the latest year was used) and a 3-year-forward CAGR projection when at least two historical data points exist — under two points it stays explicitly `computable: false` with a reason, never fabricating a trend from one point. Each computed projection carries an `anchor_ref` back to the historical series it was derived from (ADR-0018's anchoring requirement) and flags `base_rate_warning` per year when the projected value lands above the historical max by more than 10% of the historical range (ADR-0018's mechanical outlier check — peer-distribution comparison is not wired yet, so v1 is historical-only). `RoicTree.tsx`'s `EnginePendingPanel` placeholder for projection is replaced with a real table (fiscal year / projected ROIC / base-rate flag) plus the anchor_ref and CAGR. Also fixed a stray stray Chinese-character typo (corrected to the Korean wording) found in the adjacent, previously-uncommitted customer-attrition CoC section (`MaReviewTab.tsx`, ADR-0072/migration 0037/`contract-coc-clauses` route — implemented in a prior session, now completed and synced) and added its missing `docs/architecture/openapi.yaml` entries (caught by the drift gate). New tests: 5 cases across `contract-coc-clauses` (list/create/audit/invalid-direction) and `roic-tree` projection (CAGR + base-rate-warning trip, and the <2-datapoints non-computable path). (§3) no published numeric synergy-certification floor (structural Finance-owns-validation practice) + attrition benchmarks (≈50% senior managers in year 1; 21%/yr for a decade; AMA 25% of top performers in 90d) — suggests ≥90% cost-synergy floor sustained 2 quarters, CFO-signed with owner countersigns, 10% regretted-only 90d escalation with 15%/20% severity bands + a 12-month ≤20% retention milestone for demob; (§4) "control, risk, flexibility, speed" SLR anchor + trust/value-certainty drivers (no canonical "3C" framework exists) — suggests a two-stage intent-split refinement combining D3+D2 (NVCA-derived rights floor, equal 20% in-branch weights, lexicographic tacit-knowledge override, pre-negotiated step-ups); (§5) calibration-corpus methodology — bounded analogy-corpus framing (5 minimal fields, 6 overfitting guards; Green-cap lift justified by wider-class base rate + corpus consistency, never hit-rate alone); (§6) practice screens antitrust early at bid/LOI — premise reversal externally supported in direction with a flag-first design (Amber at P1/P3, Red only at presumption territory with no remedy path, verified_on-dated annually-indexed thresholds, never constants); post-2026-09-01 verification backlog listed. **`docs/decisions/2026-08-24-residual-decisions-pack.md`** — a `### Best-practice input (2026-08-24)` subsection added under each of the six agenda items (externally-supported direction + research-suggested default + evidence pointer) and the header Status line notes the annotations; **every DECISION field stays blank** — research input is non-binding decision support (boundary #2), and nothing becomes enforcement-active absent the user's decisions and the anti-tamper-gated reflection PR. No procedures/checklists/migrations/code touched.

- **[2026-08-22]**: PR-1 (predicate engine) of the approved redesign — ADR-0046 §1-§2 implemented as specified. NEW `db/lib/predicate.ts` 1.0.0: the typed JSON predicate AST (6 operators: `all_of`/`any_of`/`none_of`/`at_least`/`cmp`/`in`/`is_null`/`count`), Kleene three-valued evaluation following ADR-0046 §2's NORMATIVE truth tables (`any_of(TRUE,UNASSESSED)=TRUE`, `at_least(k=2,[T,T,U])=TRUE`, `all_of(FALSE,U)=FALSE`, `none_of` symmetric — the flat missing-evidence rule from the original plan was wrong in both directions), zod `PredicateSchema`/`parsePredicate` rejecting malformed ASTs, and **IEEE-754 parity quantization**: numeric `cmp` applies the same `Math.round(v*1e6)/1e6` guard `financialBand` uses, so the `(0.12-0.10)*100 === 1.9999999999999836` boundary cannot silently misgrade. **`renderKo`** renders predicates as the Korean governance artifact a human reviewer signs: batchim-based Korean particle selection (topic/subject/object/conjunctive particles chosen by final-jamo presence via `hasBatchim`/`particle` helpers), `none_of` negation scoping over the whole list (not per item), `at_least` k interpolation, nested depth-N bullet lists, unit suffixes. Evaluation interface only — no DB reads; PR-2's `project_variable_snapshot` table will back the resolver (keeping the engine pure is what makes the truth-table matrix an acceptance condition). `zod` 4.4.3 promoted to a DIRECT dependency (was transitive-only via `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` — finding F-12). NEW `tests/unit/predicate.test.ts`: 75 tests — all 8 normative ADR rows verbatim with line-number citations, full per-operator grids incl. degenerate cases, parity triple-check (raw float provably fails `>= 2`; quantized `cmp` matches `financialBand`'s band decision using rag-engine's own exported constants; a genuinely-below-band control case still grades below), zod malformation rejections, particle/negation-scope/unit rendering. - **[2026-08-22]**: Stage 0 (design-first, ADR-0032) of the RAG-formalization and case-classification redesign — documentation only, no code. This closes a 6-perspective design review (risk-officer, process-governance-manager+compliance-officer, data-architect+platform-lead, market-intelligence-agent+quant-methodologist, brownfield-agent+greenfield-agent, legal-counsel) that overturned the original implementation plan on 7 points, most critically that the proposed classification-aware six-validator completeness check was a waiver bypass with none of a waiver's controls (approver/expiry/history/revocation) — 4 independent reviewers converged on this finding separately. `docs/designs/2026-08-22-rag-formalization-and-case-classification-design.md` (new `docs/designs/` dir, 477 lines) records the full revised design: a typed JSON predicate AST with Kleene three-valued logic (not a DSL, not decomposed rows), a `project_variable_snapshot`-based evidence layer (replacing a dynamic per-var query design found to have SQL-injection surface and no ordering column on half its target tables), a 4-value `origin` axis (`internal`/`external`/`hybrid`/`joint` — the 3-value original had no room for a true bilateral JV), asymmetric classification write authority (single-party for `origin=external`, two-party approval for the three values that reduce the validator set), and a `not_applicable` compliance-check result so a classification-driven skip is recorded honestly instead of as a false `pass`. `docs/governance/rag-framework.md` gains a Market-dimension staged reduced-check subsection and a corrected evidence mapping (`concentration_hhi` measures bargaining power, not competitive intensity — the original mapping could silently misgrade in either direction). Five ADRs drafted at Proposed status (`0046` predicate AST + evidence registry, `0047` three-axis classification model + `case_subtype` retirement + mandatory `project_policy_pin` backfill for in-flight projects, `0048` amends ADR-0037 to scope the six-validator no-waiver rule to applicable validators, `0049` amends ADR-0016 to add UNASSESSED as a fourth grade state and a Green-cap on uncalibrated market/technical/risk rules, `0050` records ADR-0045's dangling reference as an errata note rather than rewriting an Accepted decision). Three open-decision agendas (`docs/decisions/open/{market,technical-execution,risk}-thresholds.md`, 51 TBD markers, zero invented numbers) stage the future threshold-setting meeting. Formal transcript: `memory/meeting-2026-08-22-rag-formalization-review.md`. Full suite **278 pass / 0 fail** (unchanged — documentation-only change). `bun scripts/audit.ts` all checks passed.
- **[2026-08-21]**: Combined wave — three dependency-free CLI commands + in-context glossary auto-linking. **CLI** (design §4-8 catalog; undeveloped list 12 → 9): `assume` (register assumption rows with the SAME validation the REST route enforces, via a new shared write path `db/lib/assumption.ts` — `recordAssumption()` used by both `POST /api/assumptions` and the CLI so the two can never drift on the kind enum, finite value, mandatory as_of, and the FR-DATA-07 assumed-requires-rationale rule; the REST route was refactored onto it, existing api tests unchanged-green); `glossary:lint` (scans skills/ and docs/ markdown for acronym-like tokens against `glossary/*.yaml`, report-only exit 0 in v1 — backs the glossary-curator's weekly cadence, with a tight documented allowlist and inline/fenced-code stripping); `adr:new` (scaffolds `docs/adr/NNNN-<slug>.md` at the next free number from the existing-ADR template, refuses duplicates). `newbiz.ts` 1.10.0 → 1.11.0; SCRIPTS.md rows added/refreshed; each CLI manually verified on a scratch DB (success + failure paths) plus 12 unit tests (assume 4, glossary-lint 4, adr-new 4). **Frontend — FR-SYS-04's missing half**: `app/web/src/glossary.tsx` — `GlossaryProvider` (session-once `GET /api/glossary` fetch, retry-on-failure), and a memoized `GlossaryText` renderer that auto-wraps registered terms in body text with the existing tooltip (longest-match-first, Latin `\b`-bounded case-insensitive, Korean exact-match — particle attachment makes boundary heuristics dishonest there, documented; bounded 500-entry segmentation cache; malformed regex can never break rendering). Applied to prose fields only (DD descriptions, assumption rationales, contingent-liability descriptions, antitrust remedy detail) — never keys/numbers/labels. Alias auto-detection remains gated on the list endpoint returning aliases (no N+1 fetch).
